	internal static class BlockSort
	{
		private const int FALLBACK_QSORT_SMALL_THRESH = 10;

		private const int FALLBACK_QSORT_STACK_SIZE = 100;

		private const int MAIN_QSORT_SMALL_THRESH = 20;

		private const int MAIN_QSORT_DEPTH_THRESH = 14;

		private const int MAIN_QSORT_STACK_SIZE = 100;

		private const int SETMASK = 2097152;

		private const int CLEARMASK = -2097153;

		private const int BZ_N_RADIX = 2;

		private const int BZ_N_QSORT = 12;

		private const int BZ_N_SHELL = 18;

		private const int BZ_N_OVERSHOOT = 34;

		internal static int[] incs = new int[14]
		{
			1, 4, 13, 40, 121, 364, 1093, 3280, 9841, 29524,
			88573, 265720, 797161, 2391484
		};

		private static void fswap(ref uint zz1, ref uint zz2)
		{
			uint num = zz1;
			zz1 = zz2;
			zz2 = num;
		}

		private static void fvswap(int zzp1, int zzp2, int zzn, ref uint[] fmap)
		{
			int num = zzp1;
			int num2 = zzp2;
			for (int num3 = zzn; num3 > 0; num3--)
			{
				fswap(ref fmap[num], ref fmap[num2]);
				num++;
				num2++;
			}
		}

		private static int fmin(int a, int b)
		{
			if (a >= b)
			{
				return b;
			}
			return a;
		}

		private static void fpush(int lz, int hz, ref int sp, ref int[] stackLo, ref int[] stackHi)
		{
			stackLo[sp] = lz;
			stackHi[sp] = hz;
			sp++;
		}

		private static void fpop(ref int lz, ref int hz, ref int sp, ref int[] stackLo, ref int[] stackHi)
		{
			sp--;
			lz = stackLo[sp];
			hz = stackHi[sp];
		}

		private static void SET_BH(int zz, ref uint[] bhtab)
		{
			bhtab[zz >> 5] |= (uint)(1 << zz);
		}

		private static void CLEAR_BH(int zz, ref uint[] bhtab)
		{
			bhtab[zz >> 5] &= (uint)(~(1 << zz));
		}

		private static bool ISSET_BH(int zz, ref uint[] bhtab)
		{
			return (bhtab[zz >> 5] & (uint)(1 << (zz & 0x1F))) != 0;
		}

		private static uint WORD_BH(int zz, ref uint[] bhtab)
		{
			return bhtab[zz >> 5];
		}

		private static bool UNALIGNED_BH(int zz)
		{
			return (zz & 0x1F) != 0;
		}

		private static void mswap(ref uint zz1, ref uint zz2)
		{
			uint num = zz1;
			zz1 = zz2;
			zz2 = num;
		}

		private static void mvswap(int zzp1, int zzp2, int zzn, ref uint[] ptr)
		{
			int num = zzp1;
			int num2 = zzp2;
			for (int num3 = zzn; num3 > 0; num3--)
			{
				mswap(ref ptr[num], ref ptr[num2]);
				num++;
				num2++;
			}
		}

		private static byte mmed3(byte a, byte b, byte c)
		{
			if (a > b)
			{
				byte b2 = a;
				a = b;
				b = b2;
			}
			if (b > c)
			{
				b = c;
				if (a > b)
				{
					b = a;
				}
			}
			return b;
		}

		private static int mmin(int a, int b)
		{
			if (a >= b)
			{
				return b;
			}
			return a;
		}

		private static void mpush(int lz, int hz, int dz, ref int[] stackLo, ref int[] stackHi, ref int[] stackD, ref int sp)
		{
			stackLo[sp] = lz;
			stackHi[sp] = hz;
			stackD[sp] = dz;
			sp++;
		}

		private static void mpop(ref int lz, ref int hz, ref int dz, ref int sp, int[] stackLo, int[] stackHi, int[] stackD)
		{
			sp--;
			lz = stackLo[sp];
			hz = stackHi[sp];
			dz = stackD[sp];
		}

		private static int mnextsize(int az, ref int[] nextLo, ref int[] nextHi)
		{
			return nextHi[az] - nextLo[az];
		}

		private static void mnextswap(int az, int bz, ref int[] nextLo, ref int[] nextHi, ref int[] nextD)
		{
			int num = nextLo[az];
			nextLo[az] = nextLo[bz];
			nextLo[bz] = num;
			num = nextHi[az];
			nextHi[az] = nextHi[bz];
			nextHi[bz] = num;
			num = nextD[az];
			nextD[az] = nextD[bz];
			nextD[bz] = num;
		}

		private static uint BIGFREQ(int b, uint[] ftab)
		{
			return ftab[b + 1 << 8] - ftab[b << 8];
		}

		internal static void fallbackSimpleSort(ref uint[] fmap, ref uint[] eclass, int lo, int hi)
		{
			if (lo == hi)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (hi - lo > 3)
			{
				for (int num = hi - 4; num >= lo; num--)
				{
					int num2 = (int)fmap[num];
					uint num3 = eclass[num2];
					int i;
					for (i = num + 4; i <= hi && num3 > eclass[fmap[i]]; i += 4)
					{
						fmap[i - 4] = fmap[i];
					}
					fmap[i - 4] = (uint)num2;
				}
			}
			for (int num = hi - 1; num >= lo; num--)
			{
				int num2 = (int)fmap[num];
				uint num3 = eclass[num2];
				int i;
				for (i = num + 1; i <= hi && num3 > eclass[fmap[i]]; i++)
				{
					fmap[i - 1] = fmap[i];
				}
				fmap[i - 1] = (uint)num2;
			}
		}

		internal static void fallbackQSort3(ref uint[] fmap, ref uint[] eclass, int loSt, int hiSt)
		{
			int lz = 0;
			int hz = 0;
			int[] stackLo = new int[100];
			int[] stackHi = new int[100];
			uint num = 0u;
			int sp = 0;
			fpush(loSt, hiSt, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
			while (sp > 0)
			{
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(sp < 100, 1004);
				fpop(ref lz, ref hz, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
				if (hz - lz < 10)
				{
					fallbackSimpleSort(ref fmap, ref eclass, lz, hz);
					continue;
				}
				num = (num * 7621 + 1) % 32768;
				uint num2 = (num % 3) switch
				{
					0u => eclass[fmap[lz]], 
					1u => eclass[fmap[lz + hz >> 1]], 
					_ => eclass[fmap[hz]], 
				};
				int num4;
				int num3 = (num4 = lz);
				int num6;
				int num5 = (num6 = hz);
				while (true)
				{
					if (num3 <= num5)
					{
						int num7 = (int)(eclass[fmap[num3]] - num2);
						if (num7 == 0)
						{
							fswap(ref fmap[num3], ref fmap[num4]);
							num4++;
							num3++;
							continue;
						}
						if (num7 <= 0)
						{
							num3++;
							continue;
						}
					}
					while (num3 <= num5)
					{
						int num7 = (int)(eclass[fmap[num5]] - num2);
						if (num7 == 0)
						{
							fswap(ref fmap[num5], ref fmap[num6]);
							num6--;
							num5--;
							continue;
						}
						if (num7 < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						num5--;
					}
					if (num3 > num5)
					{
						break;
					}
					fswap(ref fmap[num3], ref fmap[num5]);
					num3++;
					num5--;
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(num5 == num3 - 1, "fallbackQSort3(2)");
				if (num6 >= num4)
				{
					int num7 = fmin(num4 - lz, num3 - num4);
					fvswap(lz, num3 - num7, num7, ref fmap);
					int num8 = fmin(hz - num6, num6 - num5);
					fvswap(num3, hz - num8 + 1, num8, ref fmap);
					num7 = lz + num3 - num4 - 1;
					num8 = hz - (num6 - num5) + 1;
					if (num7 - lz > hz - num8)
					{
						fpush(lz, num7, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
						fpush(num8, hz, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
					}
					else
					{
						fpush(num8, hz, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
						fpush(lz, num7, ref sp, ref stackLo, ref stackHi);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void fallbackSort(ref uint[] fmap, ref uint[] eclass, ref uint[] bhtab, int nblock, int verb)
		{
			int[] array = new int[257];
			int[] array2 = new int[256];
			DataStreamUInt32 dataStreamUInt = new DataStreamUInt32(ref eclass);
			if (verb >= 4)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        bucket sorting ...\n");
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 257; i++)
			{
				array[i] = 0;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < nblock; i++)
			{
				array[dataStreamUInt.ReadByte(i)]++;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				array2[i] = array[i];
			}
			for (int i = 1; i < 257; i++)
			{
				array[i] += array[i - 1];
			}
			int num;
			for (int i = 0; i < nblock; i++)
			{
				num = dataStreamUInt.ReadByte(i);
				int num2 = --array[num];
				fmap[num2] = (uint)i;
			}
			int num3 = 2 + nblock / 32;
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
			{
				bhtab[i] = 0u;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				SET_BH(array[i], ref bhtab);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++)
			{
				SET_BH(nblock + 2 * i, ref bhtab);
				CLEAR_BH(nblock + 2 * i + 1, ref bhtab);
			}
			int num4 = 1;
			int num5;
			do
			{
				if (verb >= 4)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        depth {0} has ", num4);
				}
				num = 0;
				for (int i = 0; i < nblock; i++)
				{
					if (ISSET_BH(i, ref bhtab))
					{
						num = i;
					}
					int num2 = (int)fmap[i] - num4;
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						num2 += nblock;
					}
					eclass[num2] = (uint)num;
				}
				num5 = 0;
				int num6 = -1;
				while (true)
				{
					int num2;
					for (num2 = num6 + 1; ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab) && UNALIGNED_BH(num2); num2++)
					{
					}
					if (ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab))
					{
						for (; WORD_BH(num2, ref bhtab) == uint.MaxValue; num2 += 32)
						{
						}
						for (; ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab); num2++)
						{
						}
					}
					int num7 = num2 - 1;
					if (num7 >= nblock)
					{
						break;
					}
					for (; !ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab) && UNALIGNED_BH(num2); num2++)
					{
					}
					if (!ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab))
					{
						for (; WORD_BH(num2, ref bhtab) == 0; num2 += 32)
						{
						}
						for (; !ISSET_BH(num2, ref bhtab); num2++)
						{
						}
					}
					num6 = num2 - 1;
					if (num6 >= nblock)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (num6 <= num7)
					{
						continue;
					}
					num5 += num6 - num7 + 1;
					fallbackQSort3(ref fmap, ref eclass, num7, num6);
					int num8 = -1;
					for (int i = num7; i <= num6; i++)
					{
						int num9 = (int)eclass[fmap[i]];
						if (num8 != num9)
						{
							SET_BH(i, ref bhtab);
							num8 = num9;
						}
					}
				}
				if (verb >= 4)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("{0} unresolved strings\n", num5);
				}
				num4 *= 2;
			}
			while (num4 <= nblock && num5 != 0);
			if (verb >= 4)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        reconstructing block ...\n");
			}
			num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < nblock; i++)
			{
				for (; array2[num] == 0; num++)
				{
				}
				array2[num]--;
				dataStreamUInt.WriteByte((int)fmap[i], (byte)num);
			}
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(num < 256, 1005);
		}

		internal static bool mainGtU(int i1, int i2, ref DataStream block, ref int quadrantPos, int nblock, ref int budget)
		{
			BZLibCommon.AssertD(i1 != i2, "mainGtU");
			byte b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			byte b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			b = block.ReadByte(i1);
			b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
			if (b != b2)
			{
				return b > b2;
			}
			i1++;
			i2++;
			int num = nblock + 8;
			do
			{
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				ushort num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				ushort num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				b = block.ReadByte(i1);
				b2 = block.ReadByte(i2);
				if (b != b2)
				{
					return b > b2;
				}
				num2 = block.ReadUInt16(i1 + quadrantPos);
				num3 = block.ReadUInt16(i2 + quadrantPos);
				if (num2 != num3)
				{
					return num2 > num3;
				}
				i1++;
				i2++;
				if (i1 >= nblock)
				{
					i1 -= nblock;
				}
				if (i2 >= nblock)
				{
					i2 -= nblock;
				}
				num -= 8;
				budget--;
			}
			while (num >= 0);
			return false;
		}

		internal static void mainSimpleSort(ref uint[] ptr, ref DataStream block, ref int quadrantPos, int nblock, int lo, int hi, int d, ref int budget)
		{
			int num = hi - lo + 1;
			if (num < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			int i;
			for (i = 0; incs[i] < num; i++)
			{
			}
			for (i--; i >= 0; i--)
			{
				int num2 = incs[i];
				int num3 = lo + num2;
				while (num3 <= hi)
				{
					uint num4 = ptr[num3];
					int num5 = num3;
					while (mainGtU((int)(ptr[num5 - num2] + d), (int)(num4 + d), ref block, ref quadrantPos, nblock, ref budget))
					{
						ptr[num5] = ptr[num5 - num2];
						num5 -= num2;
						if (num5 <= lo + num2 - 1)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					ptr[num5] = num4;
					num3++;
					if (num3 > hi)
					{
						break;
					}
					num4 = ptr[num3];
					num5 = num3;
					while (mainGtU((int)(ptr[num5 - num2] + d), (int)(num4 + d), ref block, ref quadrantPos, nblock, ref budget))
					{
						ptr[num5] = ptr[num5 - num2];
						num5 -= num2;
						if (num5 <= lo + num2 - 1)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					ptr[num5] = num4;
					num3++;
					if (num3 > hi)
					{
						break;
					}
					num4 = ptr[num3];
					num5 = num3;
					while (mainGtU((int)(ptr[num5 - num2] + d), (int)(num4 + d), ref block, ref quadrantPos, nblock, ref budget))
					{
						ptr[num5] = ptr[num5 - num2];
						num5 -= num2;
						if (num5 <= lo + num2 - 1)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					ptr[num5] = num4;
					num3++;
					if (budget < 0)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void mainQSort3(ref uint[] ptr, ref DataStream block, ref int quadrantPos, int nblock, int loSt, int hiSt, int dSt, ref int budget)
		{
			int lz = 0;
			int hz = 0;
			int dz = 0;
			int[] stackLo = new int[100];
			int[] stackHi = new int[100];
			int[] stackD = new int[100];
			int[] nextLo = new int[3];
			int[] nextHi = new int[3];
			int[] nextD = new int[3];
			int sp = 0;
			mpush(loSt, hiSt, dSt, ref stackLo, ref stackHi, ref stackD, ref sp);
			while (sp > 0)
			{
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(sp < 100, 1001);
				mpop(ref lz, ref hz, ref dz, ref sp, stackLo, stackHi, stackD);
				if (hz - lz < 20 || dz > 14)
				{
					mainSimpleSort(ref ptr, ref block, ref quadrantPos, nblock, lz, hz, dz, ref budget);
					if (budget < 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					continue;
				}
				int num = mmed3(block.ReadByte((int)ptr[lz] + dz), block.ReadByte((int)ptr[hz] + dz), block.ReadByte((int)ptr[lz + hz >> 1] + dz));
				int num3;
				int num2 = (num3 = lz);
				int num5;
				int num4 = (num5 = hz);
				int num6;
				while (true)
				{
					if (num2 <= num4)
					{
						num6 = block.ReadByte((int)ptr[num2] + dz) - num;
						if (num6 == 0)
						{
							mswap(ref ptr[num2], ref ptr[num3]);
							num3++;
							num2++;
							continue;
						}
						if (num6 <= 0)
						{
							num2++;
							continue;
						}
					}
					while (num2 <= num4)
					{
						num6 = block.ReadByte((int)ptr[num4] + dz) - num;
						if (num6 == 0)
						{
							mswap(ref ptr[num4], ref ptr[num5]);
							num5--;
							num4--;
							continue;
						}
						if (num6 < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						num4--;
					}
					if (num2 > num4)
					{
						break;
					}
					mswap(ref ptr[num2], ref ptr[num4]);
					num2++;
					num4--;
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(num4 == num2 - 1, "mainQSort3(2)");
				if (num5 < num3)
				{
					mpush(lz, hz, dz + 1, ref stackLo, ref stackHi, ref stackD, ref sp);
					continue;
				}
				num6 = mmin(num3 - lz, num2 - num3);
				mvswap(lz, num2 - num6, num6, ref ptr);
				int num7 = mmin(hz - num5, num5 - num4);
				mvswap(num2, hz - num7 + 1, num7, ref ptr);
				num6 = lz + num2 - num3 - 1;
				num7 = hz - (num5 - num4) + 1;
				nextLo[0] = lz;
				nextHi[0] = num6;
				nextD[0] = dz;
				nextLo[1] = num7;
				nextHi[1] = hz;
				nextD[1] = dz;
				nextLo[2] = num6 + 1;
				nextHi[2] = num7 - 1;
				nextD[2] = dz + 1;
				if (mnextsize(0, ref nextLo, ref nextHi) < mnextsize(1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi))
				{
					mnextswap(0, 1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi, ref nextD);
				}
				if (mnextsize(1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi) < mnextsize(2, ref nextLo, ref nextHi))
				{
					mnextswap(1, 2, ref nextLo, ref nextHi, ref nextD);
				}
				if (mnextsize(0, ref nextLo, ref nextHi) < mnextsize(1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi))
				{
					mnextswap(0, 1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi, ref nextD);
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(mnextsize(0, ref nextLo, ref nextHi) >= mnextsize(1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi), "mainQSort3(8)");
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(mnextsize(1, ref nextLo, ref nextHi) >= mnextsize(2, ref nextLo, ref nextHi), "mainQSort3(9)");
				mpush(nextLo[0], nextHi[0], nextD[0], ref stackLo, ref stackHi, ref stackD, ref sp);
				mpush(nextLo[1], nextHi[1], nextD[1], ref stackLo, ref stackHi, ref stackD, ref sp);
				mpush(nextLo[2], nextHi[2], nextD[2], ref stackLo, ref stackHi, ref stackD, ref sp);
			}
		}

		internal static void mainSort(ref uint[] ptr, ref DataStream block, ref int quadrantPos, ref uint[] ftab, int nblock, int verb, ref int budget)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int[] array = new int[256];
			bool[] array2 = new bool[256];
			int[] array3 = new int[256];
			int[] array4 = new int[256];
			if (verb >= 4)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        main sort initialise ...\n");
			}
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i <= 65536; i++)
			{
				ftab[i] = 0u;
			}
			int num2 = block.ReadByte(0) << 8;
			for (i = nblock - 1; i >= 3; i -= 4)
			{
				block.WriteUInt16(i + quadrantPos, 0);
				num2 = (num2 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i) << 8);
				ftab[num2]++;
				block.WriteUInt16(i - 1 + quadrantPos, 0);
				num2 = (num2 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 1) << 8);
				ftab[num2]++;
				block.WriteUInt16(i - 2 + quadrantPos, 0);
				num2 = (num2 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 2) << 8);
				ftab[num2]++;
				block.WriteUInt16(i - 3 + quadrantPos, 0);
				num2 = (num2 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 3) << 8);
				ftab[num2]++;
			}
			while (i >= 0)
			{
				block.WriteUInt16(i + quadrantPos, 0);
				num2 = (num2 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i) << 8);
				ftab[num2]++;
				i--;
			}
			for (i = 0; i < 34; i++)
			{
				block.WriteByte(nblock + i, block.ReadByte(i));
				block.WriteUInt16(nblock + i + quadrantPos, 0);
			}
			if (verb >= 4)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        bucket sorting ...\n");
			}
			for (i = 1; i <= 65536; i++)
			{
				ftab[i] += ftab[i - 1];
			}
			ushort num3 = (ushort)(block.ReadByte(0) << 8);
			for (i = nblock - 1; i >= 3; i -= 4)
			{
				num3 = (ushort)((num3 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i) << 8));
				num2 = (int)(ftab[num3] - 1);
				ftab[num3] = (uint)num2;
				ptr[num2] = (uint)i;
				num3 = (ushort)((num3 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 1) << 8));
				num2 = (int)(ftab[num3] - 1);
				ftab[num3] = (uint)num2;
				ptr[num2] = (uint)(i - 1);
				num3 = (ushort)((num3 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 2) << 8));
				num2 = (int)(ftab[num3] - 1);
				ftab[num3] = (uint)num2;
				ptr[num2] = (uint)(i - 2);
				num3 = (ushort)((num3 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i - 3) << 8));
				num2 = (int)(ftab[num3] - 1);
				ftab[num3] = (uint)num2;
				ptr[num2] = (uint)(i - 3);
			}
			while (i >= 0)
			{
				num3 = (ushort)((num3 >> 8) | (block.ReadByte(i) << 8));
				num2 = (int)(ftab[num3] - 1);
				ftab[num3] = (uint)num2;
				ptr[num2] = (uint)i;
				i--;
			}
			for (i = 0; i <= 255; i++)
			{
				array2[i] = false;
				array[i] = i;
			}
			int num4 = 1;
			do
			{
				num4 = 3 * num4 + 1;
			}
			while (num4 <= 256);
			do
			{
				num4 /= 3;
				for (i = num4; i <= 255; i++)
				{
					int num5 = array[i];
					num2 = i;
					while (BIGFREQ(array[num2 - num4], ftab) > BIGFREQ(num5, ftab))
					{
						array[num2] = array[num2 - num4];
						num2 -= num4;
						if (num2 <= num4 - 1)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					array[num2] = num5;
				}
			}
			while (num4 != 1);
			int num6 = 0;
			for (i = 0; i <= 255; i++)
			{
				int num7 = array[i];
				for (num2 = 0; num2 <= 255; num2++)
				{
					if (num2 == num7)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num8 = (num7 << 8) + num2;
					if ((ftab[num8] & 0x200000) == 0)
					{
						int num9 = (int)(ftab[num8] & -2097153);
						int num10 = (int)((ftab[num8 + 1] & -2097153) - 1);
						if (num10 > num9)
						{
							if (verb >= 4)
							{
								BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        qsort [0x{0:x}, 0x{1:x}   done {2}   this {3}\n", num7, num2, num6, num10 - num9 + 1);
							}
							mainQSort3(ref ptr, ref block, ref quadrantPos, nblock, num9, num10, 2, ref budget);
							num6 += num10 - num9 + 1;
							if (budget < 0)
							{
								return;
							}
						}
					}
					ftab[num8] |= 2097152u;
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(!array2[num7], 1006);
				for (num2 = 0; num2 <= 255; num2++)
				{
					array3[num2] = (int)(ftab[(num2 << 8) + num7] & -2097153);
					array4[num2] = (int)((ftab[(num2 << 8) + num7 + 1] & -2097153) - 1);
				}
				for (num2 = (int)ftab[num7 << 8] & -2097153; num2 < array3[num7]; num2++)
				{
					num = ((ptr[num2] == 0) ? (nblock - 1) : ((int)(ptr[num2] - 1)));
					byte b = block.ReadByte(num);
					if (!array2[b])
					{
						ptr[array3[b]++] = (uint)num;
					}
				}
				if ((ftab[num7 + 1 << 8] & -2097153) > 0)
				{
					for (num2 = (int)((ftab[num7 + 1 << 8] & 0xFFDFFFFFu) - 1); num2 > array4[num7]; num2--)
					{
						num = ((ptr[num2] == 0) ? (nblock - 1) : ((int)(ptr[num2] - 1)));
						byte b = block.ReadByte(num);
						if (!array2[b])
						{
							ptr[array4[b]--] = (uint)num;
						}
					}
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(array3[num7] - 1 == array4[num7], 1007);
				for (num2 = 0; num2 <= 255; num2++)
				{
					ftab[(num2 << 8) + num7] |= 2097152u;
				}
				array2[num7] = true;
				if (i >= 255)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num11 = (int)ftab[num7 << 8] & -2097153;
				int num12 = (int)(ftab[num7 + 1 << 8] & -2097153) - num11;
				int j;
				for (j = 0; num12 >> j > 65534; j++)
				{
				}
				if (num12 > 0)
				{
					for (num2 = num12 - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
					{
						int num13 = (int)ptr[num11 + num2];
						ushort valueUInt = (ushort)(num2 >> j);
						block.WriteUInt16(num13 + quadrantPos, valueUInt);
						if (num13 < 34)
						{
							block.WriteUInt16(num13 + nblock + quadrantPos, valueUInt);
						}
					}
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(num12 - 1 >> j <= 65535, 1002);
			}
			if (verb >= 4)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("        {0} pointers, {1} sorted, {2} scanned\n", nblock, num6, nblock - num6);
			}
		}

		internal static void BZ2_blockSort(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			uint[] ptr = s.arr1;
			DataStream block = s.block;
			uint[] ftab = s.ftab;
			int nblock = s.nblock;
			int verbosity = s.verbosity;
			int num = s.workFactor;
			if (nblock < 10000)
			{
				fallbackSort(ref s.arr1, ref s.arr2, ref ftab, nblock, verbosity);
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = nblock + 34;
				if ((num2 & 1) != 0)
				{
					num2++;
				}
				int quadrantPos = num2 >> 1;
				if (num < 1)
				{
					num = 1;
				}
				if (num > 100)
				{
					num = 100;
				}
				int num3 = nblock * ((num - 1) / 3);
				int budget = num3;
				mainSort(ref ptr, ref block, ref quadrantPos, ref ftab, nblock, verbosity, ref budget);
				if (verbosity >= 3)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("      {0} work, {1} block, ratio {2:f}\n", num3 - budget, nblock, (float)(num3 - budget) / (float)((nblock == 0) ? 1 : nblock));
				}
				if (budget < 0)
				{
					if (verbosity >= 2)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf("    too repetitive; using fallback sorting algorithm\n");
					}
					fallbackSort(ref s.arr1, ref s.arr2, ref ftab, nblock, verbosity);
				}
			}
			s.origPtr = -1;
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < s.nblock; num2++)
			{
				if (ptr[num2] == 0)
				{
					s.origPtr = num2;
					break;
				}
			}
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(s.origPtr != -1, 1003);
		}
	}

			{

				code = new int[6][];
				rfreq = new int[6][];
				for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
				{
					len[i] = new byte[258];
					code[i] = new int[258];
					rfreq[i] = new int[258];
				}
				len_pack = new uint[258][];
				for (int j = 0; j < 258; j++)
				{
					len_pack[j] = new uint[4];
				}
			}
		}

			internal int save_nblockMAX;

			internal int save_nblock;

			internal int save_es;

			internal int save_N;

			internal int save_curr;

			internal int save_zt;

			internal int save_zn;

			internal int save_zvec;

			internal int save_zj;

			internal int save_gSel;

			internal int save_gMinlen;

			internal int[] save_gLimit;

			internal int[] save_gBase;

			internal int[] save_gPerm;

			internal char bSmallSig;

			internal DState()
			{
				limit = new int[6][];
				m_base = new int[6][];
				perm = new int[6][];
				len = new byte[6][];
				for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
				{
					limit[i] = new int[258];
					m_base[i] = new int[258];
					perm[i] = new int[258];
					len[i] = new byte[258];
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void BZ_GET_FAST(ref DState s, ref int cccc)
		{
			s.tPos = s.tt[s.tPos];
			cccc = (byte)(s.tPos & 0xFF);
			s.tPos >>= 8;
		}

		internal static void BZ_GET_FAST(ref DState s, ref byte cccc)
		{
			s.tPos = s.tt[s.tPos];
			cccc = (byte)(s.tPos & 0xFF);
			s.tPos >>= 8;
		}

		private static void BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref uint[] c_tt, ref uint c_tPos, ref byte cccc)
		{
			c_tPos = c_tt[c_tPos];
			cccc = (byte)(c_tPos & 0xFF);
			c_tPos >>= 8;
		}

		private static void BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref uint[] c_tt, ref uint c_tPos, ref int cccc)
		{
			c_tPos = c_tt[c_tPos];
			cccc = (int)(c_tPos & 0xFF);
			c_tPos >>= 8;
		}

		private static void SET_LL4(ref DState s, int i, int n)
		{
			if ((i & 1) == 0)
			{
				s.ll4[i >> 1] = (byte)((s.ll4[i >> 1] & 0xF0) | n);
			}
			else
			{
				s.ll4[i >> 1] = (byte)((s.ll4[i >> 1] & 0xF) | (n << 4));
			}
		}

		private static uint GET_LL4(ref DState s, uint i)
		{
			return (uint)((s.ll4[i / 2] >> (int)((i * 4) & 4)) & 0xF);
		}

		internal static void SET_LL(ref DState s, int i, int n)
		{
			s.ll16[i] = (ushort)(n & 0xFFFF);
			SET_LL4(ref s, i, n >> 16);
		}

		internal static uint GET_LL(ref DState s, uint i)
		{
			return s.ll16[i] | (GET_LL4(ref s, i) << 16);
		}

		internal static void BZ_GET_SMALL(ref DState s, ref byte cccc)
		{
			cccc = (byte)BZ2_indexIntoF(s.tPos, ref s.cftab);
			s.tPos = GET_LL(ref s, s.tPos);
		}

		internal static void BZ_GET_SMALL(ref DState s, ref int cccc)
		{
			cccc = BZ2_indexIntoF(s.tPos, ref s.cftab);
			s.tPos = GET_LL(ref s, s.tPos);
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzCompressInit(ref bz_stream strm, int blockSize100k, int verbosity, int workFactor, char bSmallSig)
		{
			if (strm == null || blockSize100k < 1 || blockSize100k > 9 || workFactor < 0 || workFactor > 250)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (workFactor == 0)
			{
				workFactor = 30;
			}
			EState s = new EState();
			if (s == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_MEM_ERROR;
			}
			s.strm = strm;
			s.arr1 = null;
			s.arr2 = null;
			s.ftab = null;
			int num = 100000 * blockSize100k;
			s.arr1 = new uint[num];
			s.arr2 = new uint[num + 34];
			s.ftab = new uint[65537];
			if (s.arr1 == null || s.arr2 == null || s.ftab == null)
			{
				s.arr1 = null;
				s.arr2 = null;
				s.ftab = null;
				s = null;
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_MEM_ERROR;
			}
			s.blockNo = 0;
			s.state = BZCompressionState.BZ_S_INPUT;
			s.mode = BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_RUNNING;
			s.combinedCRC = 0u;
			s.blockSize100k = blockSize100k;
			s.nblockMAX = 100000 * blockSize100k - 19;
			s.verbosity = verbosity;
			s.workFactor = workFactor;
			s.block = new DataStreamUInt32(ref s.arr2);
			s.mtfv = new DataStreamUInt32(ref s.arr1);
			s.zbits = new DataStreamUInt32(ref s.arr2);
			s.zbits_index = 0;
			strm.state = s;
			strm.total_in = 0uL;
			strm.total_out = 0uL;
			init_RL(ref s);
			prepare_new_block(ref s);
			s.bSmallSig = bSmallSig;
			return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzCompress(ref bz_stream strm, BZActions action)
		{
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			EState s = strm.state as EState;
			if (s == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (s.strm != strm)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			while (true)
			{
				switch (s.mode)
				{
				case BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_IDLE:
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
				case BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_RUNNING:
					switch (action)
					{
					case BZActions.BZ_RUN:
						if (!handle_compress(ref strm))
						{
							return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
						}
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_RUN_OK;
					case BZActions.BZ_FLUSH:
						s.avail_in_expect = strm.avail_in;
						s.mode = BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FLUSHING;
						break;
					case BZActions.BZ_FINISH:
						s.avail_in_expect = strm.avail_in;
						s.mode = BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FINISHING;
						break;
					default:
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
					}
					break;
				case BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FLUSHING:
					if (action != BZActions.BZ_FLUSH)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
					}
					if (s.avail_in_expect != s.strm.avail_in)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
					}
					if (handle_compress(ref strm) && (s.avail_in_expect != 0 || !isempty_RL(ref s) || s.state_out_pos < s.numZ))
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_FLUSH_OK;
					}
					s.mode = BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_RUNNING;
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_RUN_OK;
				case BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FINISHING:
					if (action != BZActions.BZ_FINISH)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
					}
					if (s.avail_in_expect != s.strm.avail_in)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
					}
					if (!handle_compress(ref strm))
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
					}
					if (s.avail_in_expect != 0 || !isempty_RL(ref s) || s.state_out_pos < s.numZ)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_FINISH_OK;
					}
					s.mode = BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_IDLE;
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END;
				default:
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref bz_stream strm)
		{
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (!(strm.state is EState eState))
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (eState.strm != strm)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			eState.arr1 = null;
			eState.arr2 = null;
			eState.ftab = null;
			strm.state = null;
			strm.state = null;
			return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzDecompressInit(ref bz_stream strm, int verbosity, bool small, char bSmallSig)
		{
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (verbosity < 0 || verbosity > 4)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			DState dState = new DState();
			if (dState == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_MEM_ERROR;
			}
			dState.strm = strm;
			strm.state = dState;
			dState.state = ((bSmallSig == '\0') ? BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_MAGIC_1 : BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_MAGIC_4);
			dState.bsLive = 0;
			dState.bsBuff = 0u;
			dState.calculatedCombinedCRC = 0u;
			strm.total_in = 0uL;
			strm.total_out = 0uL;
			dState.smallDecompress = small;
			dState.ll4 = null;
			dState.ll16 = null;
			dState.tt = null;
			dState.currBlockNo = 0;
			dState.verbosity = verbosity;
			dState.bSmallSig = bSmallSig;
			return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzDecompress(ref bz_stream strm)
		{
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			DState s = strm.state as DState;
			if (s == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (s.strm != strm)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			BZReturnValue bZReturnValue;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.state == BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_IDLE)
				{
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_SEQUENCE_ERROR;
				}
				if (s.state == BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_OUTPUT)
				{
					if (s.smallDecompress)
					{
						unRLE_obuf_to_output_SMALL(ref s);
					}
					else
					{
						unRLE_obuf_to_output_FAST(ref s);
					}
					if (s.nblock_used != s.save_nblock + 1 || s.state_out_len != 0)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
					}
					CRCTable.BZ_FINALISE_CRC(ref s.calculatedBlockCRC);
					if (s.verbosity >= 3)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf(" {0x{0:x}, 0x{1:x}}", s.storedBlockCRC, s.calculatedBlockCRC);
					}
					if (s.verbosity >= 2)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf("]");
					}
					if (s.bSmallSig == '\0' && s.calculatedBlockCRC != s.storedBlockCRC)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
					}
					s.calculatedCombinedCRC = (s.calculatedCombinedCRC << 1) | (s.calculatedCombinedCRC >> 31);
					s.calculatedCombinedCRC ^= s.calculatedBlockCRC;
					s.state = BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_BLKHDR_1;
				}
				if (s.state < BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_MAGIC_1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bZReturnValue = Decompress.BZ2_decompress(ref s);
				if (bZReturnValue == BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END)
				{
					if (s.verbosity >= 3)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf("\n    combined CRCs: stored = 0x{0:x}, computed = 0x{1:x}", s.storedCombinedCRC, s.calculatedCombinedCRC);
					}
					if (s.bSmallSig == '\0' && s.calculatedCombinedCRC != s.storedCombinedCRC)
					{
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
					}
					return bZReturnValue;
				}
				if (s.state != BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_OUTPUT)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return bZReturnValue;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref bz_stream strm)
		{
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (!(strm.state is DState dState))
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (dState.strm != strm)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			dState.tt = null;
			dState.ll16 = null;
			dState.ll4 = null;
			strm.state = null;
			return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzBuffToBuffCompress(ref byte[] dest, ref uint destLen, ref byte[] source, uint sourceLen, int blockSize100k, int verbosity, int workFactor)
		{
			bz_stream strm = new bz_stream();
			BZReturnValue bZReturnValue = BZReturnValue.BZ_CONFIG_ERROR;
			if (dest == null || source == null || blockSize100k < 1 || blockSize100k > 9 || verbosity < 0 || verbosity > 4 || workFactor < 0 || workFactor > 250)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (workFactor == 0)
			{
				workFactor = 30;
			}
			bZReturnValue = BZ2_bzCompressInit(ref strm, blockSize100k, verbosity, workFactor, '\0');
			if (bZReturnValue == BZReturnValue.BZ_OK)
			{
				strm.next_in = source;
				strm.next_out = dest;
				strm.avail_in = sourceLen;
				strm.avail_out = destLen;
				bZReturnValue = BZ2_bzCompress(ref strm, BZActions.BZ_FINISH);
				switch (bZReturnValue)
				{
				case BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END:
					destLen -= strm.avail_out;
					BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref strm);
					bZReturnValue = BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
					break;
				case BZReturnValue.BZ_FINISH_OK:
					BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref strm);
					bZReturnValue = BZReturnValue.BZ_OUTBUFF_FULL;
					break;
				default:
					BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref strm);
					break;
				}
			}
			return bZReturnValue;
		}

		internal static BZReturnValue BZ2_bzBuffToBuffDecompress(ref byte[] dest, ref uint destLen, ref byte[] source, uint sourceLen, bool small, int verbosity)
		{
			bz_stream strm = new bz_stream();
			BZReturnValue bZReturnValue = BZReturnValue.BZ_UNEXPECTED_EOF;
			if (dest == null || source == null || verbosity < 0 || verbosity > 4)
			{
				return BZReturnValue.BZ_PARAM_ERROR;
			}
			bZReturnValue = BZ2_bzDecompressInit(ref strm, verbosity, small, '\0');
			if (bZReturnValue == BZReturnValue.BZ_OK)
			{
				strm.next_in = source;
				strm.next_out = dest;
				strm.avail_in = sourceLen;
				strm.avail_out = destLen;
				bZReturnValue = BZ2_bzDecompress(ref strm);
				switch (bZReturnValue)
				{
				case BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END:
					destLen -= strm.avail_out;
					BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref strm);
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
				case BZReturnValue.BZ_OK:
					if (strm.avail_out != 0)
					{
						BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref strm);
						return BZReturnValue.BZ_UNEXPECTED_EOF;
					}
					BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref strm);
					return BZReturnValue.BZ_OUTBUFF_FULL;
				}
				if (bZReturnValue != BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END)
				{
					BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref strm);
				}
			}
			return bZReturnValue;
		}

		private static void prepare_new_block(ref EState s)
		{
			s.nblock = 0;
			s.numZ = 0;
			s.state_out_pos = 0;
			CRCTable.BZ_INITIALISE_CRC(ref s.blockCRC);
			for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				s.inUse[i] = false;
			}
			s.blockNo++;
		}

		private static void init_RL(ref EState s)
		{
			s.state_in_ch = 256u;
			s.state_in_len = 0;
		}

		private static bool isempty_RL(ref EState s)
		{
			if (s.state_in_ch < 256 && s.state_in_len > 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void add_pair_to_block(ref EState s)
		{
			byte b = (byte)s.state_in_ch;
			for (int i = 0; i < s.state_in_len; i++)
			{
				CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref s.blockCRC, b);
			}
			s.inUse[s.state_in_ch] = true;
			switch (s.state_in_len)
			{
			case 1:
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				break;
			case 2:
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				break;
			case 3:
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				break;
			default:
				s.inUse[s.state_in_len - 4] = true;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, b);
				s.nblock++;
				s.block.WriteByte(s.nblock, (byte)(s.state_in_len - 4));
				s.nblock++;
				break;
			}
		}

		private static void flush_RL(ref EState s)
		{
			if (s.state_in_ch < 256)
			{
				add_pair_to_block(ref s);
			}
			init_RL(ref s);
		}

		private static void ADD_CHAR_TO_BLOCK(ref EState zs, uint zchh0)
		{
			if (zchh0 != zs.state_in_ch && zs.state_in_len == 1)
			{
				byte b = (byte)zs.state_in_ch;
				CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref zs.blockCRC, b);
				zs.inUse[zs.state_in_ch] = true;
				zs.block.WriteByte(zs.nblock, b);
				zs.nblock++;
				zs.state_in_ch = zchh0;
			}
			else if (zchh0 != zs.state_in_ch || zs.state_in_len == 255)
			{
				if (zs.state_in_ch < 256)
				{
					add_pair_to_block(ref zs);
				}
				zs.state_in_ch = zchh0;
				zs.state_in_len = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				zs.state_in_len++;
			}
		}

		private static bool copy_input_until_stop(ref EState s)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (s.mode == BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_RUNNING)
			{
				while (s.nblock < s.nblockMAX && s.strm.avail_in != 0)
				{
					result = true;
					ADD_CHAR_TO_BLOCK(ref s, s.strm.next_in[s.strm.next_in_index]);
					s.strm.next_in_index++;
					s.strm.avail_in--;
					s.strm.total_in++;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				while (s.nblock < s.nblockMAX && s.strm.avail_in != 0 && s.avail_in_expect != 0)
				{
					result = true;
					ADD_CHAR_TO_BLOCK(ref s, s.strm.next_in[s.strm.next_in_index]);
					s.strm.next_in_index++;
					s.strm.avail_in--;
					s.strm.total_in++;
					s.avail_in_expect--;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool copy_output_until_stop(ref EState s)
		{
			bool result = false;
			while (s.strm.avail_out != 0 && s.state_out_pos < s.numZ)
			{
				result = true;
				s.strm.next_out[s.strm.next_out_index] = s.zbits.ReadByte(s.zbits_index + s.state_out_pos);
				s.state_out_pos++;
				s.strm.avail_out--;
				s.strm.next_out_index++;
				s.strm.total_out++;
				if (s.strm.total_out == 0)
				{
					s.strm.total_out++;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool handle_compress(ref bz_stream strm)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			EState s = (EState)strm.state;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.state == BZCompressionState.BZ_S_OUTPUT)
				{
					flag2 |= copy_output_until_stop(ref s);
					if (s.state_out_pos < s.numZ || (s.mode == BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FINISHING && s.avail_in_expect == 0 && isempty_RL(ref s)))
					{
						break;
					}
					prepare_new_block(ref s);
					s.state = BZCompressionState.BZ_S_INPUT;
					if (s.mode == BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FLUSHING && s.avail_in_expect == 0 && isempty_RL(ref s))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (s.state == BZCompressionState.BZ_S_INPUT)
				{
					flag |= copy_input_until_stop(ref s);
					if (s.mode != BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_RUNNING && s.avail_in_expect == 0)
					{
						flush_RL(ref s);
						Compress.BZ2_compressBlock(ref s, s.mode == BZCompressionMode.BZ_M_FINISHING);
						s.state = BZCompressionState.BZ_S_OUTPUT;
					}
					else if (s.nblock >= s.nblockMAX)
					{
						Compress.BZ2_compressBlock(ref s, is_last_block: false);
						s.state = BZCompressionState.BZ_S_OUTPUT;
					}
					else if (s.strm.avail_in == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return flag2;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void unRLE_obuf_to_output_FAST(ref DState s)
		{
			byte cccc = 0;
			if (s.blockRandomised != 0)
			{
				while (s.strm.avail_out != 0)
				{
					if (s.state_out_len != 0)
					{
						s.strm.next_out[s.strm.next_out_index] = s.state_out_ch;
						CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref s.calculatedBlockCRC, s.state_out_ch);
						s.state_out_len--;
						s.strm.next_out_index++;
						s.strm.avail_out--;
						s.strm.total_out++;
						continue;
					}
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						break;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 1;
					s.state_out_ch = (byte)s.k0;
					BZ_GET_FAST(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (cccc != s.k0)
					{
						s.k0 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 2;
					BZ_GET_FAST(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (cccc != s.k0)
					{
						s.k0 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 3;
					BZ_GET_FAST(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used != s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						if (cccc != s.k0)
						{
							s.k0 = cccc;
							continue;
						}
						BZ_GET_FAST(ref s, ref cccc);
						RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
						cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
						s.nblock_used++;
						s.state_out_len = cccc + 4;
						BZ_GET_FAST(ref s, ref s.k0);
						RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
						s.k0 ^= RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
						s.nblock_used++;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			uint crcVar = s.calculatedBlockCRC;
			byte b = s.state_out_ch;
			int num = s.state_out_len;
			int num2 = s.nblock_used;
			int cccc2 = s.k0;
			uint[] c_tt = s.tt;
			uint c_tPos = s.tPos;
			byte[] next_out = s.strm.next_out;
			uint num3 = s.strm.next_out_index;
			uint num4 = s.strm.avail_out;
			uint num5 = num4;
			int num6 = s.save_nblock + 1;
			while (true)
			{
				if (num > 0)
				{
					while (num4 != 0)
					{
						if (num != 1)
						{
							next_out[num3] = b;
							CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref crcVar, b);
							num--;
							num3++;
							num4--;
							continue;
						}
						goto IL_02f6;
					}
					break;
				}
				goto IL_031c;
				IL_031c:
				while (num2 != num6)
				{
					b = (byte)cccc2;
					BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref c_tt, ref c_tPos, ref cccc);
					num2++;
					if (cccc != cccc2 || num2 == num6)
					{
						if (cccc != cccc2)
						{
							cccc2 = cccc;
						}
						if (num4 != 0)
						{
							next_out[num3] = b;
							CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref crcVar, b);
							num3++;
							num4--;
							continue;
						}
						goto IL_0355;
					}
					goto IL_0379;
				}
				num = 0;
				break;
				IL_02f6:
				if (num4 == 0)
				{
					num = 1;
					break;
				}
				next_out[num3] = b;
				CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref crcVar, b);
				num3++;
				num4--;
				goto IL_031c;
				IL_0355:
				num = 1;
				break;
				IL_0379:
				num = 2;
				BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref c_tt, ref c_tPos, ref cccc);
				num2++;
				if (num2 == num6)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (cccc != cccc2)
				{
					cccc2 = cccc;
					continue;
				}
				num = 3;
				BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref c_tt, ref c_tPos, ref cccc);
				num2++;
				if (num2 != num6)
				{
					if (cccc != cccc2)
					{
						cccc2 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref c_tt, ref c_tPos, ref cccc);
					num2++;
					num = cccc + 4;
					BZ_GET_FAST_C(ref c_tt, ref c_tPos, ref cccc2);
					num2++;
				}
			}
			_ = s.strm.total_out;
			s.strm.total_out += num5 - num4;
			s.calculatedBlockCRC = crcVar;
			s.state_out_ch = b;
			s.state_out_len = num;
			s.nblock_used = num2;
			s.k0 = cccc2;
			s.tt = c_tt;
			s.tPos = c_tPos;
			s.strm.next_out_index = num3;
			s.strm.avail_out = num4;
		}

		private static int BZ2_indexIntoF(uint indx, ref int[] cftab)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 256;
			do
			{
				int num3 = num + num2 >> 1;
				if (indx >= cftab[num3])
				{
					num = num3;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = num3;
				}
			}
			while (num2 - num != 1);
			return num;
		}

		private static void unRLE_obuf_to_output_SMALL(ref DState s)
		{
			byte cccc = 0;
			if (s.blockRandomised != 0)
			{
				while (s.strm.avail_out != 0)
				{
					if (s.state_out_len != 0)
					{
						s.strm.next_out[s.strm.next_out_index] = s.state_out_ch;
						CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref s.calculatedBlockCRC, s.state_out_ch);
						s.state_out_len--;
						s.strm.next_out_index++;
						s.strm.avail_out--;
						s.strm.total_out++;
						continue;
					}
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						break;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 1;
					s.state_out_ch = (byte)s.k0;
					BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (cccc != s.k0)
					{
						s.k0 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 2;
					BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (cccc != s.k0)
					{
						s.k0 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					s.state_out_len = 3;
					BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
					RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
					cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
					s.nblock_used++;
					if (s.nblock_used != s.save_nblock + 1)
					{
						if (cccc != s.k0)
						{
							s.k0 = cccc;
							continue;
						}
						BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
						RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
						cccc ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
						s.nblock_used++;
						s.state_out_len = cccc + 4;
						BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref s.k0);
						RandTable.BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref s);
						s.k0 ^= (byte)RandTable.BZ_RAND_MASK(ref s);
						s.nblock_used++;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			while (s.strm.avail_out != 0)
			{
				if (s.state_out_len != 0)
				{
					s.strm.next_out[s.strm.next_out_index] = s.state_out_ch;
					CRCTable.BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref s.calculatedBlockCRC, s.state_out_ch);
					s.state_out_len--;
					s.strm.next_out_index++;
					s.strm.avail_out--;
					s.strm.total_out++;
					continue;
				}
				if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
				{
					break;
				}
				s.state_out_len = 1;
				s.state_out_ch = (byte)s.k0;
				BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
				s.nblock_used++;
				if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (cccc != s.k0)
				{
					s.k0 = cccc;
					continue;
				}
				s.state_out_len = 2;
				BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
				s.nblock_used++;
				if (s.nblock_used == s.save_nblock + 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (cccc != s.k0)
				{
					s.k0 = cccc;
					continue;
				}
				s.state_out_len = 3;
				BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
				s.nblock_used++;
				if (s.nblock_used != s.save_nblock + 1)
				{
					if (cccc != s.k0)
					{
						s.k0 = cccc;
						continue;
					}
					BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref cccc);
					s.nblock_used++;
					s.state_out_len = cccc + 4;
					BZ_GET_SMALL(ref s, ref s.k0);
					s.nblock_used++;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class BZLibCommon
	{
		private const string BZ_VERSION = "1.0.1, 23-June-2000";

		private const int BZ_MAX_ALPHA_SIZE = 258;

		private const int BZ_MAX_CODE_LEN = 23;

		private const int BZ_N_GROUPS = 6;

		private const int BZ_G_SIZE = 50;

		private const int BZ_N_ITERS = 4;

		private const int BZ_MAX_SELECTORS = 18002;

		private static void Assert(bool bResult, string sTrace)
		{
			if (!bResult)
			{
				Debugger.Break();
			}
		}

		private static string BZ2_bzlibVersion()
		{
			return "1.0.1, 23-June-2000";
		}

		private static void BZ2_bz__AssertH__fail(int errcode)
		{
			Assert(bResult: false, "\n\nbzip2/libbzip2: internal error number %d.\nThis is a bug in bzip2/libbzip2, %s.\nPlease report it to me at: jseward@acm.org.  If this happened\nwhen you were using some program which uses libbzip2 as a\ncomponent, you should also report this bug to the author(s)\nof that program.  Please make an effort to report this bug;\ntimely and accurate bug reports eventually lead to higher\nquality software.  Thanks.  Julian Seward, 21 March 2000.\n\n" + errcode + "\n" + BZ2_bzlibVersion());
		}

		public static void AssertH(bool cond, int errcode)
		{
			if (!cond)
			{
				BZ2_bz__AssertH__fail(errcode);
			}
		}

		public static void AssertD(bool cond, string msg)
		{
			if (!cond)
			{
				Assert(cond, msg);
			}
		}

		public static void VPrintf(string zf, params object[] arg)
		{
			Console.WriteLine(zf, arg);
		}
	}
	internal class Compress
	{
		private const int BZ_LESSER_ICOST = 0;

		private const int BZ_GREATER_ICOST = 15;

		internal static void BZ2_bsInitWrite(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			s.bsLive = 0;
			s.bsBuff = 0u;
		}

		internal static void bsFinishWrite(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			while (s.bsLive > 0)
			{
				s.zbits.WriteByte(s.zbits_index + s.numZ, (byte)(s.bsBuff >> 24));
				s.numZ++;
				s.bsBuff <<= 8;
				s.bsLive -= 8;
			}
		}

		private static void bsNEEDW(int nz, ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			while (s.bsLive >= 8)
			{
				s.zbits.WriteByte(s.zbits_index + s.numZ, (byte)(s.bsBuff >> 24));
				s.numZ++;
				s.bsBuff <<= 8;
				s.bsLive -= 8;
			}
		}

		private static void BZ_FINALISE_CRC(ref uint crcVar)
		{
			crcVar = ~crcVar;
		}

		internal static void bsW(ref BZLib.EState s, int n, uint v)
		{
			bsNEEDW(n, ref s);
			s.bsBuff |= v << 32 - s.bsLive - n;
			s.bsLive += n;
		}

		internal static void bsPutUInt32(ref BZLib.EState s, uint u)
		{
			bsW(ref s, 8, (u >> 24) & 0xFF);
			bsW(ref s, 8, (u >> 16) & 0xFF);
			bsW(ref s, 8, (u >> 8) & 0xFF);
			bsW(ref s, 8, u & 0xFF);
		}

		internal static void bsPutUChar(ref BZLib.EState s, byte c)
		{
			bsW(ref s, 8, c);
		}

		internal static void makeMaps_e(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			s.nInUse = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				if (s.inUse[i])
				{
					s.unseqToSeq[i] = (byte)s.nInUse;
					s.nInUse++;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void generateMTFValues(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			byte[] array = new byte[256];
			uint[] arr = s.arr1;
			DataStreamUInt32 block = s.block;
			DataStreamUInt32 mtfv = s.mtfv;
			makeMaps_e(ref s);
			int num = s.nInUse + 1;
			for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
			{
				s.mtfFreq[i] = 0;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < s.nInUse; i++)
			{
				array[i] = (byte)i;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < s.nblock; i++)
			{
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(num2 <= i, "generateMTFValues(1)");
				int num4 = (int)(arr[i] - 1);
				if (num4 < 0)
				{
					num4 += s.nblock;
				}
				byte b = s.unseqToSeq[block.ReadByte(num4)];
				BZLibCommon.AssertD(b < s.nInUse, "generateMTFValues(2a)");
				if (array[0] == b)
				{
					num3++;
					continue;
				}
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					num3--;
					while (true)
					{
						if ((num3 & 1) != 0)
						{
							mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, 1);
							num2++;
							s.mtfFreq[1]++;
						}
						else
						{
							mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, 0);
							num2++;
							s.mtfFreq[0]++;
						}
						if (num3 < 2)
						{
							break;
						}
						num3 = (num3 - 2) / 2;
					}
					num3 = 0;
				}
				byte b2 = array[1];
				array[1] = array[0];
				byte b3 = 1;
				byte b4 = b;
				while (b4 != b2)
				{
					b3++;
					byte b5 = b2;
					b2 = array[b3];
					array[b3] = b5;
				}
				array[0] = b2;
				num4 = b3;
				mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, (ushort)(num4 + 1));
				num2++;
				s.mtfFreq[num4 + 1]++;
			}
			if (num3 > 0)
			{
				num3--;
				while (true)
				{
					if ((num3 & 1) != 0)
					{
						mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, 1);
						num2++;
						s.mtfFreq[1]++;
					}
					else
					{
						mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, 0);
						num2++;
						s.mtfFreq[0]++;
					}
					if (num3 < 2)
					{
						break;
					}
					num3 = (num3 - 2) / 2;
				}
				num3 = 0;
			}
			mtfv.WriteUInt16(num2, (ushort)num);
			num2++;
			s.mtfFreq[num]++;
			s.nMTF = num2;
		}

		internal static void sendMTFValues(ref BZLib.EState s)
		{
			int num = 0;
			ushort[] array = new ushort[6];
			int[] array2 = new int[6];
			DataStream mtfv = s.mtfv;
			if (s.verbosity >= 3)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("      {0} in block, {1} after MTF & 1-2 coding, {2}+2 syms in use\n", s.nblock, s.nMTF, s.nInUse);
			}
			int num2 = s.nInUse + 2;
			for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
				{
					s.len[i][j] = 15;
				}
			}
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(s.nMTF > 0, 3001);
			int num3 = ((s.nMTF < 200) ? 2 : ((s.nMTF < 600) ? 3 : ((s.nMTF < 1200) ? 4 : ((s.nMTF >= 2400) ? 6 : 5))));
			int num4 = num3;
			int num5 = s.nMTF;
			int num6 = 0;
			while (num4 > 0)
			{
				int num7 = num5 / num4;
				int num8 = num6 - 1;
				int k;
				for (k = 0; k < num7; k += s.mtfFreq[num8])
				{
					if (num8 >= num2 - 1)
					{
						break;
					}
					num8++;
				}
				if (num8 > num6 && num4 != num3 && num4 != 1 && (num3 - num4) % 2 == 1)
				{
					k -= s.mtfFreq[num8];
					num8--;
				}
				if (s.verbosity >= 3)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("      initial group {0}, [{1} .. {2}], has {3} syms ({4:f})\n", num4, num6, num8, k, 100.0 * (double)(float)k / (double)(float)s.nMTF);
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
				{
					if (j >= num6 && j <= num8)
					{
						s.len[num4 - 1][j] = 0;
					}
					else
					{
						s.len[num4 - 1][j] = 15;
					}
				}
				num4--;
				num6 = num8 + 1;
				num5 -= k;
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < 4; l++)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
				{
					array2[i] = 0;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
					{
						s.rfreq[i][j] = 0;
					}
				}
				if (num3 == 6)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
					{
						s.len_pack[j][0] = (uint)((s.len[1][j] << 16) | s.len[0][j]);
						s.len_pack[j][1] = (uint)((s.len[3][j] << 16) | s.len[2][j]);
						s.len_pack[j][2] = (uint)((s.len[5][j] << 16) | s.len[4][j]);
					}
				}
				num = 0;
				int num9 = 0;
				num6 = 0;
				while (num6 < s.nMTF)
				{
					int num8 = num6 + 50 - 1;
					if (num8 >= s.nMTF)
					{
						num8 = s.nMTF - 1;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
					{
						array[i] = 0;
					}
					if (num3 == 6 && 50 == num8 - num6 + 1)
					{
						uint num11;
						uint num12;
						uint num10 = (num11 = (num12 = 0u));
						for (int m = 0; m < 50; m++)
						{
							ushort num13 = mtfv.ReadUInt16(num6 + m);
							num10 += s.len_pack[num13][0];
							num11 += s.len_pack[num13][1];
							num12 += s.len_pack[num13][2];
						}
						array[0] = (ushort)(num10 & 0xFFFF);
						array[1] = (ushort)(num10 >> 16);
						array[2] = (ushort)(num11 & 0xFFFF);
						array[3] = (ushort)(num11 >> 16);
						array[4] = (ushort)(num12 & 0xFFFF);
						array[5] = (ushort)(num12 >> 16);
					}
					else
					{
						for (int n = num6; n <= num8; n++)
						{
							ushort num14 = mtfv.ReadUInt16(n);
							for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
							{
								array[i] += s.len[i][num14];
							}
						}
					}
					int num15 = 999999999;
					int num16 = -1;
					for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
					{
						if (array[i] < num15)
						{
							num15 = array[i];
							num16 = i;
						}
					}
					num9 += num15;
					array2[num16]++;
					s.selector[num] = (byte)num16;
					num++;
					if (num3 == 6 && 50 == num8 - num6 + 1)
					{
						for (int num17 = 0; num17 < 50; num17++)
						{
							s.rfreq[num16][mtfv.ReadUInt16(num6 + num17)]++;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						for (int n = num6; n <= num8; n++)
						{
							s.rfreq[num16][mtfv.ReadUInt16(n)]++;
						}
					}
					num6 = num8 + 1;
				}
				if (s.verbosity >= 3)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("      pass {0}: size is {1}, grp uses are ", l + 1, num9 / 8);
					for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf("{0} ", array2[i]);
					}
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("\n");
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
				{
					Huffman.BZ2_hbMakeCodeLengths(ref s.len, ref s.rfreq, num2, 20, i);
				}
			}
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(num3 < 8, 3002);
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(num < 32768 && num <= 18002, 3003);
			byte[] array3 = new byte[6];
			for (int n = 0; n < num3; n++)
			{
				array3[n] = (byte)n;
			}
			for (int n = 0; n < num; n++)
			{
				byte b = s.selector[n];
				int num18 = 0;
				byte b2 = array3[num18];
				while (b != b2)
				{
					num18++;
					byte b3 = b2;
					b2 = array3[num18];
					array3[num18] = b3;
				}
				array3[0] = b2;
				s.selectorMtf[n] = (byte)num18;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
			{
				int num19 = 32;
				int num20 = 0;
				for (int n = 0; n < num2; n++)
				{
					if (s.len[i][n] > num20)
					{
						num20 = s.len[i][n];
					}
					if (s.len[i][n] < num19)
					{
						num19 = s.len[i][n];
					}
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(num20 <= 20, 3004);
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(num19 >= 1, 3005);
				Huffman.BZ2_hbAssignCodes(ref s.code, ref s.len, num19, num20, num2, i);
			}
			bool[] array4 = new bool[16];
			for (int n = 0; n < 16; n++)
			{
				array4[n] = false;
				for (int num18 = 0; num18 < 16; num18++)
				{
					if (s.inUse[n * 16 + num18])
					{
						array4[n] = true;
					}
				}
			}
			int numZ = s.numZ;
			for (int n = 0; n < 16; n++)
			{
				if (array4[n])
				{
					bsW(ref s, 1, 1u);
				}
				else
				{
					bsW(ref s, 1, 0u);
				}
			}
			for (int n = 0; n < 16; n++)
			{
				if (!array4[n])
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int num18 = 0; num18 < 16; num18++)
				{
					if (s.inUse[n * 16 + num18])
					{
						bsW(ref s, 1, 1u);
					}
					else
					{
						bsW(ref s, 1, 0u);
					}
				}
			}
			if (s.verbosity >= 3)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("      bytes: mapping {0}, ", s.numZ - numZ);
			}
			numZ = s.numZ;
			bsW(ref s, 3, (uint)num3);
			bsW(ref s, 15, (uint)num);
			for (int n = 0; n < num; n++)
			{
				for (int num18 = 0; num18 < s.selectorMtf[n]; num18++)
				{
					bsW(ref s, 1, 1u);
				}
				bsW(ref s, 1, 0u);
			}
			if (s.verbosity >= 3)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("selectors {0}, ", s.numZ - numZ);
			}
			numZ = s.numZ;
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
			{
				int num21 = s.len[i][0];
				bsW(ref s, 5, (uint)num21);
				for (int n = 0; n < num2; n++)
				{
					for (; num21 < s.len[i][n]; num21++)
					{
						bsW(ref s, 2, 2u);
					}
					while (num21 > s.len[i][n])
					{
						bsW(ref s, 2, 3u);
						num21--;
					}
					bsW(ref s, 1, 0u);
				}
			}
			if (s.verbosity >= 3)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("code lengths {0}, ", s.numZ - numZ);
			}
			numZ = s.numZ;
			int num22 = 0;
			num6 = 0;
			while (num6 < s.nMTF)
			{
				int num8 = num6 + 50 - 1;
				if (num8 >= s.nMTF)
				{
					num8 = s.nMTF - 1;
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(s.selector[num22] < num3, 3006);
				if (num3 == 6 && 50 == num8 - num6 + 1)
				{
					int num23 = 0;
					int num24 = 0;
					for (int num25 = 0; num25 < 50; num25++)
					{
						ushort num26 = mtfv.ReadUInt16(num6 + num25);
						bsW(ref s, s.len[s.selector[num22]][num26 + num23], (uint)s.code[s.selector[num22]][num26 + num24]);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int n = num6; n <= num8; n++)
					{
						bsW(ref s, s.len[s.selector[num22]][mtfv.ReadUInt16(n)], (uint)s.code[s.selector[num22]][mtfv.ReadUInt16(n)]);
					}
				}
				num6 = num8 + 1;
				num22++;
			}
			BZLibCommon.AssertH(num22 == num, 3007);
			if (s.verbosity >= 3)
			{
				BZLibCommon.VPrintf("codes {0}\n", s.numZ - numZ);
			}
		}

		internal static void BZ2_compressBlock(ref BZLib.EState s, bool is_last_block)
		{
			if (s.nblock > 0)
			{
				BZ_FINALISE_CRC(ref s.blockCRC);
				s.combinedCRC = (s.combinedCRC << 1) | (s.combinedCRC >> 31);
				s.combinedCRC ^= s.blockCRC;
				if (s.blockNo > 1)
				{
					s.numZ = 0;
				}
				if (s.verbosity >= 2)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("    block {0}: crc = 0x{1:x}, combined CRC = 0x{2:x}, size = {3}\n", s.blockNo, s.blockCRC, s.combinedCRC, s.nblock);
				}
				BlockSort.BZ2_blockSort(ref s);
			}
			s.zbits_index = s.nblock;
			if (s.blockNo == 1)
			{
				BZ2_bsInitWrite(ref s);
				if (s.bSmallSig == '\0')
				{
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 66);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 90);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 104);
				}
				bsPutUChar(ref s, (byte)(48 + s.blockSize100k));
			}
			if (s.nblock > 0)
			{
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 49);
				if (s.bSmallSig == '\0')
				{
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 65);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 89);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 38);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 83);
					bsPutUChar(ref s, 89);
					bsPutUInt32(ref s, s.blockCRC);
				}
				bsW(ref s, 1, 0u);
				bsW(ref s, 24, (uint)s.origPtr);
				generateMTFValues(ref s);
				sendMTFValues(ref s);
			}
			if (!is_last_block)
			{
				return;
			}
			bsPutUChar(ref s, 23);
			if (s.bSmallSig == '\0')
			{
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 114);
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 69);
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 56);
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 80);
				bsPutUChar(ref s, 144);
				bsPutUInt32(ref s, s.combinedCRC);
				if (s.verbosity >= 2)
				{
					BZLibCommon.VPrintf("    final combined CRC = 0x{0:x}\n   ", s.combinedCRC);
				}
			}
			bsFinishWrite(ref s);
		}
	}

		internal static void BZ_INITIALISE_CRC(ref uint crcVar)
		{
			crcVar = uint.MaxValue;
		}

		internal static void BZ_FINALISE_CRC(ref uint crcVar)
		{
			crcVar = ~crcVar;
		}

		internal static void BZ_UPDATE_CRC(ref uint crcVar, byte cha)
		{
			crcVar = (crcVar << 8) ^ BZ2_crc32Table[(crcVar >> 24) ^ cha];
		}
	}
	internal abstract class DataStream
	{
		public abstract byte ReadByte(int iIndex);

		public abstract ushort ReadUInt16(int iIndex);

		public abstract uint ReadUInt32(int iIndex);

		public abstract void WriteByte(int iIndex, byte valueByte);

		public abstract void WriteUInt16(int iIndex, ushort valueUInt16);

		public abstract void WriteUInt32(int iIndex, uint valueUInt32);
	}
	internal class DataStreamByte : DataStream
	{
		private byte[] m_streamData;

		public DataStreamByte(ref byte[] array)
		{
			m_streamData = array;
		}

		public override byte ReadByte(int iIndex)
		{
			return m_streamData[iIndex];
		}

		public override ushort ReadUInt16(int iIndex)
		{
			iIndex *= 2;
			return (ushort)(m_streamData[iIndex + 1] | (m_streamData[iIndex] << 8));
		}

		public override uint ReadUInt32(int iIndex)
		{
			iIndex *= 4;
			return (uint)(m_streamData[iIndex + 3] | (m_streamData[iIndex + 2] << 8) | (m_streamData[iIndex + 1] << 16) | (m_streamData[iIndex] << 24));
		}

		public override void WriteByte(int iIndex, byte valueByte)
		{
			m_streamData[iIndex] = valueByte;
		}

		public override void WriteUInt16(int iIndex, ushort valueUInt16)
		{
			iIndex *= 2;
			m_streamData[iIndex + 1] = (byte)(valueUInt16 & 0xFF);
			m_streamData[iIndex] = (byte)((valueUInt16 >> 8) & 0xFF);
		}

		public override void WriteUInt32(int iIndex, uint valueUInt32)
		{
			iIndex *= 4;
			m_streamData[iIndex + 3] = (byte)(valueUInt32 & 0xFF);
			m_streamData[iIndex + 2] = (byte)((valueUInt32 >> 8) & 0xFF);
			m_streamData[iIndex + 1] = (byte)((valueUInt32 >> 16) & 0xFF);
			m_streamData[iIndex] = (byte)((valueUInt32 >> 24) & 0xFF);
		}
	}
	internal class DataStreamUInt16 : DataStream
	{
		private ushort[] m_streamData;

		public DataStreamUInt16(ref ushort[] array)
		{
			m_streamData = array;
		}

		public ushort Read(int iIndex)
		{
			return m_streamData[iIndex];
		}

		public override byte ReadByte(int iIndex)
		{
			iIndex /= 2;
			return (byte)((iIndex % 1 == 0) ? (m_streamData[iIndex] & 0xFF) : ((m_streamData[iIndex] >> 8) & 0xFF));
		}

		public override ushort ReadUInt16(int iIndex)
		{
			return m_streamData[iIndex];
		}

		public override uint ReadUInt32(int iIndex)
		{
			iIndex *= 2;
			return (uint)(m_streamData[iIndex] | (m_streamData[iIndex + 1] << 16));
		}

		public void Write(int iIndex, ushort valueUInt16)
		{
			m_streamData[iIndex] = valueUInt16;
		}

		public override void WriteByte(int iIndex, byte valueByte)
		{
			iIndex /= 2;
			if (iIndex % 1 == 0)
			{
				m_streamData[iIndex] = valueByte;
			}
			else
			{
				m_streamData[iIndex] = (ushort)(valueByte << 8);
			}
		}

		public override void WriteUInt16(int iIndex, ushort valueUInt16)
		{
			m_streamData[iIndex] = valueUInt16;
		}

		public override void WriteUInt32(int iIndex, uint valueUInt32)
		{
			iIndex *= 2;
			m_streamData[iIndex] = (ushort)((valueUInt32 >> 16) & 0xFFFF);
			m_streamData[iIndex + 1] = (ushort)(valueUInt32 & 0xFFFF);
		}
	}
	internal class DataStreamUInt32 : DataStream
	{
		private uint[] m_streamData;

		public DataStreamUInt32(ref uint[] array)
		{
			m_streamData = array;
		}

		public override byte ReadByte(int iIndex)
		{
			return (iIndex % 4) switch
			{
				0 => (byte)(m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] & 0xFF), 
				1 => (byte)((m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] >> 8) & 0xFF), 
				2 => (byte)((m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] >> 16) & 0xFF), 
				_ => (byte)((m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] >> 24) & 0xFF), 
			};
		}

		public override ushort ReadUInt16(int iIndex)
		{
			if (iIndex % 2 == 0)
			{
				return (ushort)(m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] & 0xFFFF);
			}
			return (ushort)((m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] >> 16) & 0xFFFF);
		}

		public override uint ReadUInt32(int iIndex)
		{
			return m_streamData[iIndex];
		}

		public override void WriteByte(int iIndex, byte valueByte)
		{
			switch (iIndex % 4)
			{
			case 0:
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] & 0xFFFFFF00u) | valueByte;
				break;
			case 1:
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] & 0xFFFF00FFu) | (uint)(valueByte << 8);
				break;
			case 2:
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] & 0xFF00FFFFu) | (uint)(valueByte << 16);
				break;
			default:
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 2] & 0xFFFFFF) | (uint)(valueByte << 24);
				break;
			}
		}

		public override void WriteUInt16(int iIndex, ushort valueUInt16)
		{
			if (iIndex % 2 == 0)
			{
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] & 0xFFFF0000u) | valueUInt16;
			}
			else
			{
				m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] = (m_streamData[iIndex >> 1] & 0xFFFF) | (uint)(valueUInt16 << 16);
			}
		}

		public override void WriteUInt32(int iIndex, uint valueUInt32)
		{
			m_streamData[iIndex] = valueUInt32;
		}
	}
	internal class Decompress
	{
		private static int groupPos;

		private static int groupNo;

		private static BZLib.BZReturnValue retVal;

		private static int nSelectors;

		private static int gSel;

		private static int gMinlen;

		private static int[] gLimit;

		private static int[] gBase;

		private static int[] gPerm;

		private static int zt;

		private static int zn;

		private static int zvec;

		private static int zj;

		internal static BZLib.BZReturnValue BZ2_decompress(ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			byte uuu = 0;
			_ = s.strm;
			BZLib.BZDecompressionState bZDecompressionState = BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None;
			if (s.state == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_MAGIC_1 || (s.state == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_MAGIC_4 && s.bSmallSig != 0))
			{
				s.save_i = 0;
				s.save_j = 0;
				s.save_t = 0;
				s.save_alphaSize = 0;
				s.save_nGroups = 0;
				s.save_nSelectors = 0;
				s.save_EOB = 0;
				s.save_groupNo = 0;
				s.save_groupPos = 0;
				s.save_nextSym = 0;
				s.save_nblockMAX = 0;
				s.save_nblock = 0;
				s.save_es = 0;
				s.save_N = 0;
				s.save_curr = 0;
				s.save_zt = 0;
				s.save_zn = 0;
				s.save_zvec = 0;
				s.save_zj = 0;
				s.save_gSel = 0;
				s.save_gMinlen = 0;
				s.save_gLimit = null;
				s.save_gBase = null;
				s.save_gPerm = null;
			}
			int i = s.save_i;
			int num = s.save_j;
			int j = s.save_t;
			int num2 = s.save_alphaSize;
			int vvv = s.save_nGroups;
			nSelectors = s.save_nSelectors;
			int num3 = s.save_EOB;
			groupNo = s.save_groupNo;
			groupPos = s.save_groupPos;
			int lval = s.save_nextSym;
			int num4 = s.save_nblockMAX;
			int num5 = s.save_nblock;
			int num6 = s.save_es;
			int num7 = s.save_N;
			int vvv2 = s.save_curr;
			zt = s.save_zt;
			zn = s.save_zn;
			zvec = s.save_zvec;

			{

					{

									i = s.save_i;
									bZDecompressionState = BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None;
								}
								if (!GET_BIT(BZLib.BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_CODING_2, ref uuu, ref s))
								{
									break;
								}
								if (uuu == 0)
								{
									goto IL_0adc;
								}
							}
							else
							{
								j = s.save_t;
								i = s.save_i;
								bZDecompressionState = BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None;
							}
							if (!GET_BIT(BZLib.BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_CODING_3, ref uuu, ref s))
							{
								break;
							}
							vvv2 = ((uuu != 0) ? (vvv2 - 1) : (vvv2 + 1));
						}
						goto IL_1430;
						IL_0adc:;
					}
					j++;
					continue;
				}
				goto IL_1430;
			}
			for (j = 0; j < vvv; j++)
			{
				int num17 = 32;
				int num18 = 0;
				for (i = 0; i < num2; i++)
				{
					if (s.len[j][i] > num18)
					{
						num18 = s.len[j][i];
					}
					if (s.len[j][i] < num17)
					{
						num17 = s.len[j][i];
					}
				}
				Huffman.BZ2_hbCreateDecodeTables(ref s.limit[j], ref s.m_base[j], ref s.perm[j], ref s.len[j], num17, num18, num2);
				s.minLens[j] = num17;
			}
			num3 = s.nInUse + 1;
			num4 = 100000 * s.blockSize100k;
			groupNo = -1;
			groupPos = 0;
			for (i = 0; i <= 255; i++)
			{
				s.unzftab[i] = 0;
			}
			int num19 = 4095;
			for (int num20 = 15; num20 >= 0; num20--)
			{
				for (int num21 = 15; num21 >= 0; num21--)
				{
					s.mtfa[num19] = (byte)(num20 * 16 + num21);
					num19--;
				}
				s.mtfbase[num20] = num19 + 1;
			}
			num5 = 0;
			goto IL_0c46;
			IL_066d:
			if (GET_UCHAR(BZLib.BZDecompressionState.BZ_X_BLKHDR_6, ref uuu, ref s))
			{
				if (uuu == 89)
				{
					s.currBlockNo++;
					if (s.verbosity >= 2)
					{
						BZLibCommon.VPrintf("\n    [{0}: huff+mtf ", s.currBlockNo);
					}
					s.storedBlockCRC = 0u;
					goto IL_06d2;
				}
				retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
			}
			goto IL_1430;
			IL_1347:

			{

				{
					goto IL_1367;
				}
				retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
			}
			goto IL_1430;
			IL_0735:

				goto IL_13ee;
