		internal static void makeMaps_d(ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			s.nInUse = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				if (s.inUse[i])
				{
					s.seqToUnseq[s.nInUse] = (byte)i;
					s.nInUse++;
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool GET_BITS(BZLib.BZDecompressionState state, ref int vvv, int nnn, ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			s.state = state;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.bsLive >= nnn)
				{
					vvv = (int)((s.bsBuff >> s.bsLive - nnn) & ((1 << nnn) - 1));
					s.bsLive -= nnn;
					return true;
				}
				if (s.strm.avail_in == 0)
				{
					break;
				}
				s.bsBuff = (s.bsBuff << 8) | s.strm.next_in[s.strm.next_in_index];
				s.bsLive += 8;
				s.strm.next_in_index++;
				s.strm.avail_in--;
				s.strm.total_in++;
			}
			retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK;
			return false;
		}

		private static bool GET_UCHAR(BZLib.BZDecompressionState state, ref byte uuu, ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			int vvv = 0;
			bool result = GET_BITS(state, ref vvv, 8, ref s);
			uuu = (byte)vvv;
			return result;
		}

		private static bool GET_BIT(BZLib.BZDecompressionState state, ref byte uuu, ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			int vvv = 0;
			bool result = GET_BITS(state, ref vvv, 1, ref s);
			uuu = (byte)vvv;
			return result;
		}

		private static bool GET_MTF_VAL(ref BZLib.BZDecompressionState forceLabel, BZLib.BZDecompressionState label1, BZLib.BZDecompressionState label2, ref int lval, ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			if (forceLabel == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None)
			{
				if (groupPos == 0)
				{
					groupNo++;
					if (groupNo >= nSelectors)
					{
						retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
						return false;
					}
					groupPos = 50;
					gSel = s.selector[groupNo];
					gMinlen = s.minLens[gSel];
					gLimit = s.limit[gSel];
					gPerm = s.perm[gSel];
					gBase = s.m_base[gSel];
				}
				groupPos--;
				zn = gMinlen;
			}
			if (forceLabel == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None || forceLabel == label1)
			{
				forceLabel = BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None;
				if (!GET_BITS(label1, ref zvec, zn, ref s))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			while (true)
			{
				if (forceLabel == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None)
				{
					if (zn > 20)
					{
						retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
						return false;
					}
					if (zvec <= gLimit[zn])
					{
						break;
					}
					zn++;
				}
				if (forceLabel == BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None || forceLabel == label2)
				{
					forceLabel = BZLib.BZDecompressionState.None;
					if (!GET_BITS(label2, ref zj, 1, ref s))
					{
						return false;
					}
					zvec = (zvec << 1) | zj;
					continue;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (zvec - gBase[zn] < 0 || zvec - gBase[zn] >= 258)
			{
				retVal = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_DATA_ERROR;
				return false;
			}
			lval = gPerm[zvec - gBase[zn]];
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal static class Huffman
	{
		private const int BZ_MAX_ALPHA_SIZE = 258;

		private const int BZ_MAX_CODE_LEN = 23;

		private static int[] heap = new int[260];

		private static int[] weight = new int[516];

		private static int[] parent = new int[516];

		private static int nNodes;

		private static int nHeap;

		private static int WEIGHTOF(int zz0)
		{
			return (int)(zz0 & 0xFFFFFF00u);
		}

		private static int DEPTHOF(int zz1)
		{
			return zz1 & 0xFF;
		}

		private static int MYMAX(int zz2, int zz3)
		{
			if (zz2 <= zz3)
			{
				return zz3;
			}
			return zz2;
		}

		private static int ADDWEIGHTS(int zw1, int zw2)
		{
			return (WEIGHTOF(zw1) + WEIGHTOF(zw2)) | (1 + MYMAX(DEPTHOF(zw1), DEPTHOF(zw2)));
		}

		private static void UPHEAP(int z)
		{
			int num = z;
			int num2 = heap[num];
			while (weight[num2] < weight[heap[num >> 1]])
			{
				heap[num] = heap[num >> 1];
				num >>= 1;
			}
			heap[num] = num2;
		}

		private static void DOWNHEAP(int z)
		{
			int num = z;
			int num2 = heap[num];
			while (true)
			{
				int num3 = num << 1;
				if (num3 > nHeap)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (num3 < nHeap && weight[heap[num3 + 1]] < weight[heap[num3]])
				{
					num3++;
				}
				if (weight[num2] < weight[heap[num3]])
				{
					break;
				}
				heap[num] = heap[num3];
				num = num3;
			}
			heap[num] = num2;
		}

		public static void BZ2_hbMakeCodeLengths(ref byte[][] len, ref int[][] freq, int alphaSize, int maxLen, int t)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < alphaSize; i++)
			{
				weight[i + 1] = ((freq[t][i] == 0) ? 1 : freq[t][i]) << 8;
			}
			while (true)
			{
				nNodes = alphaSize;
				nHeap = 0;
				heap[0] = 0;
				weight[0] = 0;
				parent[0] = -2;
				for (int i = 1; i <= alphaSize; i++)
				{
					parent[i] = -1;
					nHeap++;
					heap[nHeap] = i;
					UPHEAP(nHeap);
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(nHeap < 260, 2001);
				while (nHeap > 1)
				{
					int num = heap[1];
					heap[1] = heap[nHeap];
					nHeap--;
					DOWNHEAP(1);
					int num2 = heap[1];
					heap[1] = heap[nHeap];
					nHeap--;
					DOWNHEAP(1);
					nNodes++;
					parent[num] = (parent[num2] = nNodes);
					weight[nNodes] = ADDWEIGHTS(weight[num], weight[num2]);
					parent[nNodes] = -1;
					nHeap++;
					heap[nHeap] = nNodes;
					UPHEAP(nHeap);
				}
				BZLibCommon.AssertH(nNodes < 516, 2002);
				bool flag = false;
				for (int i = 1; i <= alphaSize; i++)
				{
					int num3 = 0;
					int num4 = i;
					while (parent[num4] >= 0)
					{
						num4 = parent[num4];
						num3++;
					}
					len[t][i - 1] = (byte)num3;
					if (num3 > maxLen)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					break;
				}
				for (int i = 1; i < alphaSize; i++)
				{
					int num3 = weight[i] >> 8;
					num3 = 1 + num3 / 2;
					weight[i] = num3 << 8;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void BZ2_hbAssignCodes(ref int[][] code, ref byte[][] length, int minLen, int maxLen, int alphaSize, int t)
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = minLen; i <= maxLen; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < alphaSize; j++)
				{
					if (length[t][j] == i)
					{
						code[t][j] = num;
						num++;
					}
				}
				num <<= 1;
			}
		}

		public static void BZ2_hbCreateDecodeTables(ref int[] limit, ref int[] base1, ref int[] perm, ref byte[] length, int minLen, int maxLen, int alphaSize)
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = minLen; i <= maxLen; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < alphaSize; j++)
				{
					if (length[j] == i)
					{
						perm[num] = j;
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 23; i++)
			{
				base1[i] = 0;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < alphaSize; i++)
			{
				base1[length[i] + 1]++;
			}
			for (int i = 1; i < 23; i++)
			{
				base1[i] += base1[i - 1];
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 23; i++)
			{
				limit[i] = 0;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int i = minLen; i <= maxLen; i++)
			{
				num2 += base1[i + 1] - base1[i];
				limit[i] = num2 - 1;
				num2 <<= 1;
			}
			for (int i = minLen + 1; i <= maxLen; i++)
			{
				base1[i] = (limit[i - 1] + 1 << 1) - base1[i];
			}
		}
	}

		};

		internal static void BZ_RAND_INIT_MASK(ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			s.rNToGo = 0;
			s.rTPos = 0;
		}

		internal static int BZ_RAND_MASK(ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			if (s.rNToGo != 1)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return 1;
		}

		internal static void BZ_RAND_UPD_MASK(ref BZLib.DState s)
		{
			if (s.rNToGo == 0)
			{
				s.rNToGo = BZ2_rNums[s.rTPos];
				s.rTPos++;
				if (s.rTPos == 512)
				{
					s.rTPos = 0;
				}
			}
			s.rNToGo--;
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Compression
{
	public class CompressedStream : Stream
	{
		private const int m_readSize = 32768;

		private Stream m_stream;

		private Compressor m_compressor;

		private Decompressor m_decompressor;

		private bool m_delayedInitialization;

		private CompressionMethod m_compressionMethod;

		private CompressionLevel m_compressionLevel;

		private byte[] m_decompressed;

		private int m_decompressedCount;

		private int m_decompressedOffset;

		private byte[] m_compressed = new byte[32768];

		private int m_compressedCount;

		private int m_compressedOffset;

		private bool m_endOfData;

		private bool m_transient;

		private bool m_noCompression;

		private byte[] m_remainingBytes = new byte[0];

		private bool m_disposed;

		private bool m_dataWritten;

		private bool m_useZlibHeaders;

		private static Assembly m_ppmdAssembly;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_noCompression)
				{
					return m_stream.CanRead;
				}
				if (!m_stream.CanRead)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (m_delayedInitialization)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return m_decompressor != null;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_noCompression)
				{
					return m_stream.CanWrite;
				}
				if (!m_stream.CanWrite)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (m_delayedInitialization)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return m_compressor != null;
			}
		}

		public Stream InnerStream => m_stream;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("A CompressedStream object is not seekable.");
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("A CompressedStream object is not seekable.");
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("A CompressedStream object is not seekable.");
			}
		}

		public bool Transient
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_transient;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_transient = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressedStream(Stream inner)
			: this(inner, CompressionMethod.Deflated, CompressionLevel.Highest)
		{
		}

		public CompressedStream(Stream inner, CompressionMethod method, CompressionLevel level)
			: this(inner, null, null)
		{
			if (method > (CompressionMethod)127)
			{
				m_useZlibHeaders = true;
				method -= 128;
			}
			bool flag = m_useZlibHeaders;
			if (level > (CompressionLevel)127)
			{
				flag = true;
				level -= 128;
			}
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(CompressionMethod), method))
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("method", method, "The compression method is not a valid CompressionMethod member.");
			}
			m_compressionMethod = method;
			m_compressionLevel = level;
			m_delayedInitialization = true;
			if (!flag && (m_compressionMethod == CompressionMethod.Stored || level == CompressionLevel.None))
			{
				m_noCompression = true;
			}
		}

		public CompressedStream(Stream inner, Compressor compressor)
			: this(inner, compressor, null)
		{
		}

		public CompressedStream(Stream inner, Decompressor decompressor)
			: this(inner, null, decompressor)
		{
		}

		public CompressedStream(Stream inner, Compressor compressor, Decompressor decompressor)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner", "The inner stream parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			try
			{
				m_stream = inner;
				m_compressor = compressor;
				m_decompressor = decompressor;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException("An unexpected internal failure prevented the CompressedStream object from being initialized properly.", ex);
			}
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!m_noCompression && m_dataWritten && CanWrite)
			{
				Write(new byte[0], 0, 0);
			}
			m_stream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("A CompressedStream object is not seekable.");
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("A CompressedStream object is not seekable.");
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_noCompression)
			{
				return m_stream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			}
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("This CompressedStream object does not support reading or decompressing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_decompressor == null)
				{
					switch (m_compressionMethod)
					{
					case CompressionMethod.Deflated:
						m_decompressor = new ManagedDeflateDecompressor(m_useZlibHeaders);
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
						m_decompressor = new ManagedDeflate64Decompressor(m_useZlibHeaders);
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
						m_decompressor = new ManagedBZip2Decompressor();
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.BWT:
						m_decompressor = new ManagedBZip2Decompressor('\u0001');
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
						if (m_ppmdAssembly == null)
						{
							m_ppmdAssembly = LoadAssemblyByDisplayName("Xceed.Compression.PPMd");
						}
						m_decompressor = new ManagedPPMdDecompressor(m_ppmdAssembly);
						break;
					default:
						throw new CompressionInternalException("Unknown CompressionMethod value");
					}
				}
				int num = 0;
				while (true)
				{
					if (m_decompressedCount > 0)
					{
						int num2 = ((count > m_decompressedCount) ? m_decompressedCount : count);
						if (num2 > 0)
						{
							Buffer.BlockCopy(m_decompressed, m_decompressedOffset, buffer, offset, num2);
							m_decompressedOffset += num2;
							m_decompressedCount -= num2;
							offset += num2;
							count -= num2;
							num += num2;
							if (m_decompressedCount == 0)
							{
								m_decompressed = null;
								m_decompressedOffset = 0;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (m_decompressedCount == 0)
					{
						m_decompressed = null;
					}
					if (count == 0 || m_endOfData)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (m_compressedCount == 0)
					{
						m_compressedOffset = 0;
						m_compressedCount = m_stream.Read(m_compressed, 0, m_compressed.Length);
						m_endOfData = m_compressedCount == 0;
					}
					int num3 = ((m_compressedCount > 1024) ? 1024 : m_compressedCount);
					int remaining = 0;
					m_decompressedCount = m_decompressor.Decompress(m_compressed, m_compressedOffset, num3, ref m_endOfData, out m_decompressed, out remaining);
					if (remaining > 0)
					{
						m_remainingBytes = new byte[remaining];
						Buffer.BlockCopy(m_compressed, m_compressedOffset + num3 - remaining, m_remainingBytes, 0, remaining);
					}
					m_compressedOffset += num3;
					m_compressedCount -= num3;
				}
				return num;
			}
			catch (IOException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_noCompression)
			{
				m_stream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
				return;
			}
			if (!CanWrite)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("This CompressedStream object does not support writing or compressing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			try
			{
				if (!m_dataWritten)
				{
					m_dataWritten = count > 0;
				}
				if (!m_dataWritten)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (m_compressor == null)
				{
					switch (m_compressionMethod)
					{
					case CompressionMethod.Deflated:
						m_compressor = new ManagedDeflateCompressor((int)m_compressionLevel, m_useZlibHeaders);
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
						m_compressor = new ManagedDeflate64Compressor((int)m_compressionLevel, m_useZlibHeaders);
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
						m_compressor = new ManagedBZip2Compressor((int)m_compressionLevel);
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.BWT:
						m_compressor = new ManagedBZip2Compressor((int)m_compressionLevel, '\u0001');
						break;
					case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
						if (m_ppmdAssembly == null)
						{
							m_ppmdAssembly = LoadAssemblyByDisplayName("Xceed.Compression.PPMd");
						}
						m_compressor = new ManagedPPMdCompressor(m_ppmdAssembly);
						break;
					default:
						throw new CompressionInternalException("Unknown CompressionMethod value");
					}
				}
				byte[] compressed = null;
				int num = m_compressor.Compress(buffer, offset, count, m_endOfData, out compressed);
				if (num > 0)
				{
					m_stream.Write(compressed, 0, num);
				}
			}
			catch (IOException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (NotSupportedException innerException)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("This CompressedStream object does not support writing or compressing.", innerException);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public Stream GetRemainingStream()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!Transient)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("An attempt was made to access the remaining data while the CompressedStream is not transient.");
			}
			if (!m_endOfData)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("An attempt was made to access the remaining stream before the end of the CompressedStream was reached.");
			}
			if (m_remainingBytes == null)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("GetRemainingStream can only be called once.");
			}
			Stream result = new CombinedStream(m_remainingBytes, m_stream);
			m_remainingBytes = null;
			return result;
		}

		private CompressedStream()
		{
		}

		~CompressedStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_dataWritten)
				{
					m_endOfData = true;
					Flush();
				}
			}
			catch (NotSupportedException)
			{
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (disposing)
				{
					if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
					{
						throw;
					}
					throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
				}
			}
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (!m_transient)
				{
					m_stream.Close();
				}
				if (m_compressor is IDisposable disposable)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
				if (m_decompressor is IDisposable disposable2)
				{
					disposable2.Dispose();
				}
				m_compressor = null;
				m_decompressor = null;
			}
			m_stream = null;
			m_disposed = true;
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("CompressedStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private Assembly LoadAssemblyByDisplayName(string assemblyDisplayName)
		{
			Assembly assembly = null;
			Assembly[] assemblies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies();
			Assembly[] array = assemblies;
			foreach (Assembly assembly2 in array)
			{
				if (assembly2.GetName().Name.Contains(assemblyDisplayName))
				{
					assembly = assembly2;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (assembly == null)
			{
				try
				{
					assembly = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.Load(assemblyDisplayName);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (assembly == null)
			{
				try
				{
					assembly = Assembly.Load(assemblyDisplayName);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			return assembly;
		}
	}
	public class CompressionException : IOException
	{
		public CompressionException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public CompressionException(string message, Exception inner)
			: base(message, inner)
		{
		}
	}
	public class CompressionInternalException : CompressionException
	{
		private const string m_defaultMessage = "An unexpected internal failure occurred in the compression engine.";

		public CompressionInternalException()
			: base("An unexpected internal failure occurred in the compression engine.")
		{
		}

		public CompressionInternalException(Exception inner)
			: base("An unexpected internal failure occurred in the compression engine.", inner)
		{
		}

		public CompressionInternalException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public CompressionInternalException(string message, Exception inner)
			: base(message, inner)
		{
		}
	}
	public enum CompressionLevel
	{
		None = 0,
		Lowest = 1,
		Normal = 6,
		Highest = 9
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Licensing

	internal abstract class XceedLicense
	{
		internal struct ProductVersion
		{
			public LicenseProduct Product;

			{
				Product = product;
				Major = major;
				Minor = minor;
				SupportSampleKey = true;
			}

			public ProductVersion(LicenseProduct product, byte major, byte minor, bool supportSampleKey)
			{
				Product = product;
				Major = major;
				Minor = minor;
				SupportSampleKey = supportSampleKey;
			}
		}

		private const string PackagePathKey = "Software\\Xceed Software";

		private const string LicenseAnalyzerPathKey = "Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer";

		private const string InstallDirValueKey = "InstallDirectory";

		private const string TrialLastDisplayValueKey = "TrialLastDisplay";

		private const string LastDisplayTrialDateValueKey = "TrialVerification";

		private const string OlderVersionStateValueKey = "OlderVersionState";

		private const string LicensesRegistryLocation = "Licenses\\4507C5F2-9077-40DE-A621-64EC59BF6829\\";

		private string m_licenseKey = "";

		private bool m_trial;

		private LicenseProduct m_product;

		private byte m_versionMajor;

		private byte m_versionMinor;

		private byte m_nbLicenses;

		private bool m_siteLicense;

		private static string s_installDirectory = string.Empty;

		private bool m_cannotFindAnalyzer;

		private bool m_isFromRegistry;

		private ArrayList m_performedFeratureAnalysis = new ArrayList();

		public bool IsFromRegistry
		{
			get
			{
				return m_isFromRegistry;
			}
			set
			{
				m_isFromRegistry = value;
			}
		}

		public string LicenseKey => m_licenseKey;

		public bool IsTrial => m_trial;

		public LicenseProduct Product => m_product;

		public byte VersionMajor => m_versionMajor;

		public byte VersionMinor => m_versionMinor;

		public byte NbLicenses => m_nbLicenses;

		public bool IsSiteLicense => m_siteLicense;

		protected abstract ProductVersion[] AllowedVersions { get; }

		protected virtual ProductVersion[] LatestVersions => new ProductVersion[1] { AllowedVersions[0] };

		protected abstract Type LicenseeType { get; }

		private static TrialState CurrentTrialState
		{
			get
			{
				if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
				{
					return TrialState.Welcome;
				}
				RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				try
				{
					registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						return TrialState.Welcome;
					}
					if (registryKey.GetValue("TrialLastDisplay") is string s)
					{
						try
						{
							return (TrialState)int.Parse(s);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return TrialState.Welcome;
						}
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					registryKey?.Close();
				}
				return TrialState.Welcome;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
				{
					return;
				}
				RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				try
				{
					registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
					if (registryKey != null)
					{
						RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey;
						int num = (int)value;
						registryKey2.SetValue("TrialLastDisplay", num.ToString());
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					registryKey?.Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool RunningOnDevelopperMachine
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(s_installDirectory))
					{
						return true;
					}
					RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software");
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software");
						if (registryKey == null)
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					s_installDirectory = registryKey.GetValue("InstallDirectory") as string;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s_installDirectory))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public XceedLicense()
		{
		}

		public void Initialize(string licenseKey, Type licenserType)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(licenseKey))
			{
				licenseKey = GetLicenseKeyFromRegistry();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(licenseKey))
				{
					ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry();
				}
			}
			XceedLicenseManager lm = new XceedLicenseManager();
			ValidateLicenseKey(ref lm, ref licenseKey, performNagIfNecessary: true);
			m_licenseKey = licenseKey;
			m_nbLicenses = lm.NbLicenses;
			m_siteLicense = lm.IsSiteLicense;
			m_trial = lm.Originator >= LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion && lm.Originator <= LicenseOrigin.TrialRenew9;
			m_product = lm.Product;
			m_versionMajor = lm.VerMajor;
			m_versionMinor = lm.VerMinor;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_licenseKey) || m_trial || !m_isFromRegistry)
			{
				return;
			}
			Assembly entryAssembly = Assembly.GetEntryAssembly();
			object[] customAttributes = entryAssembly.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyConfigurationAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes != null && customAttributes.Length > 0)
			{
				AssemblyConfigurationAttribute assemblyConfigurationAttribute = (AssemblyConfigurationAttribute)customAttributes[0];
				if (assemblyConfigurationAttribute.Configuration == "XCEED-SAMPLE-APPLICATION")
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry();
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry()
		{
			ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, LicenseeType.FullName + ".LicenseKey must be set to a valid trial or registered license key in the code of your application before using this product. Please refer to the Licensing topic in the documentation for specific instructions on how to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowKeyInvalid()
		{
			ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The license key used to license this Xceed product is invalid. " + LicenseeType.FullName + ".LicenseKey must be set to a valid trial or registered license key before using this product. Please refer to the Licensing topic in the documentation for specific instructions on how to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowKeyInvalidProductVersion()
		{
			ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The license key used to license this Xceed product does not match the current product. Please make sure that you are correctly licensing the product as described in the documentation and then recompile in order to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowTrialKeyExpired()
		{
			ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The trial license key used to license this Xceed product has expired. Please contact Xceed if you need additional time to evaluate the library, or if you wish to purchase a registered license.");
		}

		protected virtual ProductVersion LatestVersion(LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (product == LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct)
			{
				return AllowedVersions[0];
			}
			ProductVersion[] allowedVersions = AllowedVersions;
			for (int i = 0; i < allowedVersions.Length; i++)
			{
				ProductVersion result = allowedVersions[i];
				if (result.Product == product)
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			return AllowedVersions[0];
		}

		private string GetLicenseKeyFromRegistry()
		{
			string licenseKey = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				XceedLicenseManager lm = new XceedLicenseManager();
				RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				ProductVersion[] latestVersions = LatestVersions;
				for (int i = 0; i < latestVersions.Length; i++)
				{
					ProductVersion productVersion = latestVersions[i];
					if (!productVersion.SupportSampleKey)
					{
						continue;
					}
					registryKey = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("Licenses\\4507C5F2-9077-40DE-A621-64EC59BF6829\\" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(productVersion.Product));
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey;
					byte major = productVersion.Major;
					string text = major.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					byte minor = productVersion.Minor;
					licenseKey = (string)registryKey2.GetValue(text + "." + minor.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
					registryKey.Close();
					if (licenseKey != null)
					{
						try
						{
							ValidateLicenseKey(ref lm, ref licenseKey, performNagIfNecessary: false);
						}
						catch (LicenseException)
						{
							continue;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
			if (licenseKey == null)
			{
				licenseKey = string.Empty;
			}
			m_isFromRegistry = true;
			return licenseKey;
		}

		private void ValidateLicenseKey(ref XceedLicenseManager lm, ref string licenseKey, bool performNagIfNecessary)
		{
			try
			{
				lm.AnalyzeLicense(licenseKey);
			}
			catch (XceedLicenseManagerException)
			{
				ThrowKeyInvalid();
			}
			bool flag = false;
			ProductVersion[] allowedVersions = AllowedVersions;
			for (int i = 0; i < allowedVersions.Length; i++)
			{
				ProductVersion productVersion = allowedVersions[i];
				if (productVersion.Product == lm.Product && productVersion.Major == lm.VerMajor && productVersion.Minor == lm.VerMinor)
				{
					flag = true;
					ProductVersion productVersion2 = LatestVersion(lm.Product);
					if ((productVersion.Major != productVersion2.Major || productVersion.Minor != productVersion2.Minor) && RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
					{
						DisplayRegisteredOlderVersion(productVersion.Major, productVersion.Minor, productVersion.Product);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				ThrowKeyInvalidProductVersion();
			}
			if (lm.Originator < LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion || lm.Originator > LicenseOrigin.TrialRenew9)
			{
				return;
			}
			int days = new TimeSpan(lm.Date.Ticks - DateTime.Today.Ticks).Days;
			if (days <= 0)
			{
				if (RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
				{
					DisplayTrialExpired(-1 * default(TimeSpan).Days, lm.Product);
				}
				ThrowTrialKeyExpired();
			}
			else if (RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
			{
				DisplayTrial(days, lm.Product);
			}
		}

		private bool DisplayWelcome(int daysLeft, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			CurrentTrialState = TrialState.FirstTrial;
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\WelcomeInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayFirstTrial(int daysLeft, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			CurrentTrialState = TrialState.Trial;
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\FirstTrialInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayTrial(int daysLeft, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (CurrentTrialState == TrialState.Welcome)
			{
				return DisplayWelcome(daysLeft, product);
			}
			if (CurrentTrialState == TrialState.FirstTrial)
			{
				return DisplayFirstTrial(daysLeft, product);
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\TrialInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayTrialExpired(int daysLeft, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.TrialExpired))
			{
				return false;
			}
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\TrialExpiredInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayRegisteredOlderVersion(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.RegisteredOlderVersion))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (OlderVersionWasPerformedBefore(product))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\UpgradeInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType informationType)
		{
			if (!IsDisplayInformationAllowed(informationType))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!Environment.UserInteractive)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (m_cannotFindAnalyzer)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
			{
				m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
				return false;
			}
			if (informationType == LicenseInformationType.FeatureRequireUpgrade)
			{
				if (m_performedFeratureAnalysis.Contains(informationType))
				{
					return false;
				}
				m_performedFeratureAnalysis.Add(informationType);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool DisplayInformation(string licenseNotificationPath, string arguments, LicenseProduct product)
		{
			Process process = null;
			ProcessStartInfo processStartInfo = null;
			try
			{
				processStartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo(licenseNotificationPath, arguments);
				processStartInfo.ErrorDialog = false;
				processStartInfo.UseShellExecute = true;
				process = new Process();
				process.StartInfo = processStartInfo;
				if (!File.Exists(licenseNotificationPath))
				{
					m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
					return false;
				}
				if (!process.Start())
				{
					m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
					return false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				process?.Dispose();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool LastDisplayWasToday()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (registryKey.GetValue("TrialVerification") is string s)
				{
					try
					{
						if (long.Parse(s) == DateTime.Today.Date.Ticks)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				registryKey.SetValue("TrialVerification", DateTime.Today.Date.Ticks);
				return false;
			}
			catch
			{
				return true;
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool IsDisplayInformationAllowed(LicenseInformationType informationType)
		{
			return true;
		}

		private static bool OlderVersionWasPerformedBefore(LicenseProduct product)
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer\\" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product));
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (registryKey.GetValue("OlderVersionState") is string value)
				{
					try
					{
						if (bool.Parse(value))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				registryKey.SetValue("OlderVersionState", true);
				return false;
			}
			catch
			{
				return true;
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		internal bool DisplayFeatureRequireUpgrade(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, LicenseProduct product, string featureVersionDescription)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(featureVersionDescription))
			{
				featureVersionDescription = "a feature requiring an upgrade";
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.FeatureRequireUpgrade))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&featureDescription=" + featureVersionDescription + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\FeatureUpgradeInformation.hta";
			LatestVersion(product);
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		internal bool DisplayAdvancedFeature(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, LicenseProduct product, string featureVersionDescription)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(featureVersionDescription))
			{
				featureVersionDescription = "a feature requiring an upgrade";
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(LicenseInformationType.AdvancedFeature))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&featureDescription=" + featureVersionDescription + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\AdvancedFeatureInformation.hta";
			LatestVersion(product);
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Compression
{
	internal class CompressionLicense : XceedLicense
	{
		protected override ProductVersion[] AllowedVersions => new ProductVersion[34]
		{
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 5),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 4),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 3),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 2),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 2, 3),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 2, 2),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 2, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 2, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 1, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 5),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 4),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 3),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 2),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedCompressNET, 1, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET, 1, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET, 1, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 5),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 4),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 3),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 2),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 2, 3),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 2, 2),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 2, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 2, 0),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET, 1, 0)
		};

		protected override ProductVersion[] LatestVersions => new ProductVersion[5]
		{
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET, 1, 1),
			new ProductVersion(LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET, 1, 0)
		};

		protected override Type LicenseeType => typeof(Licenser);

		public static XceedLicense CreateLicense(string licenseKey, Type licenserType)
		{
			XceedLicense xceedLicense = new CompressionLicense();
			xceedLicense.Initialize(licenseKey, licenserType);
			if (xceedLicense.LicenseKey == null || xceedLicense.LicenseKey.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return xceedLicense;
		}

		private CompressionLicense()
		{
		}
	}
	public enum CompressionMethod
	{
		Stored = 0,
		Deflated = 8,
		Deflated64 = 9,
		BZip2 = 12,
		BWT = 18,
		PPMd = 98
	}
	public abstract class Compressor
	{
		protected Compressor()
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
		}

		public abstract int Compress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, bool endOfData, out byte[] compressed);
	}
	public abstract class Decompressor
	{
		protected Decompressor()
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
		}

		public abstract int Decompress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, ref bool endOfData, out byte[] decompressed, out int remaining);
	}
	internal enum FeatureID
	{
		NoFeatureSpecified,
		Deflate64,
		BZip2_BWT,
		PPMd
	}
	public static class Licenser
	{
		private static ArrayList LicenseList = new ArrayList();

		private static string _licenseKey = string.Empty;

		public static string LicenseKey
		{
			get
			{
				return _licenseKey;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("LicenseKey");
				}
				CreateLicense(value);
				_licenseKey = value;
			}
		}

		internal static bool AllowDeflate64
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (XceedLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					switch (license.Product)
					{
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETCF:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 2)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNETCF:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNETCF:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3 && license.VersionMinor >= 5)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedCompressNET:
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static bool AllowBZip2_BWT
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (XceedLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					switch (license.Product)
					{
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETCF:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3 && license.VersionMinor >= 4)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNETCF:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static bool AllowPPMd
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (XceedLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					switch (license.Product)
					{
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET:
					case LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3 && license.VersionMinor >= 5)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1 && license.VersionMinor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static void VerifyLicense()
		{
			if (LicenseList.Count == 0 && LicenseManager.UsageMode != LicenseUsageMode.Designtime)
			{
				CreateLicense(string.Empty);
			}
		}

		internal static void VerifyLicense(FeatureID featureId)
		{
			VerifyLicense();
			if (LicenseList.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			switch (featureId)
			{
			default:
				return;
			case FeatureID.BZip2_BWT:
				if (!AllowBZip2_BWT)
				{
					text = "BZip2 and BWT feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v3.4 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.Deflate64:
				if (!AllowDeflate64)
				{
					text = "Deflate64 feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v2.0 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.PPMd:
				if (!AllowPPMd)
				{
					text = "PPMd feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v3.5 or higher";
				}
				break;
			}
			if (text != null)
			{
				XceedLicense xceedLicense = LicenseList[LicenseList.Count - 1] as XceedLicense;
				xceedLicense.DisplayFeatureRequireUpgrade(xceedLicense.VersionMajor, xceedLicense.VersionMinor, xceedLicense.Product, text);
				ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(null, null, "You are using a v" + xceedLicense.VersionMajor + "." + xceedLicense.VersionMinor + " registered license key but are accessing the " + text + ".");
			}
		}

		private static void CreateLicense(string licenseKey)
		{
			XceedLicense xceedLicense = CompressionLicense.CreateLicense(licenseKey, typeof(Licenser));
			lock (LicenseList.SyncRoot)
			{
				foreach (CompressionLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					if (license.Product == xceedLicense.Product && license.VersionMajor == xceedLicense.VersionMajor && license.VersionMinor == xceedLicense.VersionMinor)
					{
						LicenseList.Remove(license);
						break;
					}
				}
				LicenseList.Add(xceedLicense);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ManagedBZip2Compressor : Compressor
	{
		private BZLib.bz_stream m_bzstream;

		private byte[] m_compressionBuffer;

		private bool m_flushCalled;

		public ManagedBZip2Compressor(int compressionLevel, char bSmallSig, int workFactor, int verbosity)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
			try
			{
				m_bzstream = new BZLib.bz_stream();
				if (BZLib.BZ2_bzCompressInit(ref m_bzstream, compressionLevel, verbosity, workFactor, bSmallSig) != BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK)
				{
					throw new CompressionException("Unable to initialize the managed BZip2 compression engine.");
				}
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Compressor(int compressionLevel, char bSmallSig, int workFactor)
			: this(compressionLevel, bSmallSig, workFactor, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Compressor(int compressionLevel, char bSmallSig)
			: this(compressionLevel, bSmallSig, 0, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Compressor(int compressionLevel)
			: this(compressionLevel, '\0', 0, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public override int Compress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, bool endOfData, out byte[] compressed)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			try
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (count == 0 && m_flushCalled && !endOfData)
				{
					compressed = new byte[0];
					return 0;
				}
				if (count > 0)
				{
					num = count + count / 9 + 12;
					m_bzstream.next_in = buffer;
					m_bzstream.next_in_index = (uint)offset;
					m_bzstream.avail_in = (uint)count;
				}
				else
				{
					num = 65536;
					m_bzstream.next_in = null;
					m_bzstream.next_in_index = 0u;
					m_bzstream.avail_in = 0u;
				}
				if (m_compressionBuffer == null || m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_compressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_compressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				ulong total_out = m_bzstream.total_out;
				BZLib.BZReturnValue bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_FINISH_OK;
				if (endOfData)
				{
					do
					{
						num += 65536;
						if (m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
						{
							byte[] array = new byte[num];
							m_compressionBuffer.CopyTo(array, 0);
							m_compressionBuffer = array;
						}
						m_bzstream.next_out = m_compressionBuffer;
						m_bzstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_bzstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out));
						bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZ2_bzCompress(ref m_bzstream, BZLib.BZActions.BZ_FINISH);
					}
					while (bZReturnValue == BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_FINISH_OK);
					switch (bZReturnValue)
					{
					case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END:
						break;
					case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_FINISH_OK:
						BZLib.BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref m_bzstream);
						throw new CompressionException("BZIP2: Out of compressor buffer");
					default:
						BZLib.BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref m_bzstream);
						throw new CompressionException("BZIP2: Expected end of stream not found");
					}
					num = (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
					BZLib.BZ2_bzCompressEnd(ref m_bzstream);
				}
				else
				{
					BZLib.BZActions action = BZLib.BZActions.BZ_RUN;
					m_flushCalled = false;
					if (count == 0)
					{
						m_flushCalled = true;
						action = BZLib.BZActions.BZ_FLUSH;
					}
					do
					{
						m_bzstream.next_out = m_compressionBuffer;
						m_bzstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_bzstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out));
						bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZ2_bzCompress(ref m_bzstream, action);
						if (m_bzstream.avail_out == 0 && m_bzstream.avail_in != 0)
						{
							num += 65536;
							if (m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
							{
								byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
								m_compressionBuffer.CopyTo(array2, 0);
								m_compressionBuffer = array2;
							}
						}
					}
					while ((m_bzstream.avail_out == 0 && m_bzstream.avail_in != 0) || BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_FLUSH_OK == bZReturnValue);
					if (bZReturnValue != BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_RUN_OK && bZReturnValue != BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_FLUSH_OK)
					{
						throw new CompressionException("BZIP2: Expected end of stream not found");
					}
					num = (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
				}
				compressed = m_compressionBuffer;
				return num;
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ManagedBZip2Decompressor : Decompressor
	{
		private BZLib.bz_stream m_bzstream;

		private byte[] m_decompressionBuffer;

		public ManagedBZip2Decompressor(char bSmallSig, bool small, int verbosity)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
			try
			{
				m_bzstream = new BZLib.bz_stream();
				if (BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompressInit(ref m_bzstream, verbosity, small, bSmallSig) != BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK)
				{
					throw new CompressionException("Unable to initialize the managed BZip2 decompression engine.");
				}
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Decompressor(char bSmallSig, bool small)
			: this(bSmallSig, small, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Decompressor(char bSmallSig)
			: this(bSmallSig, small: false, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public ManagedBZip2Decompressor()
			: this('\0', small: false, 0)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
		}

		public override int Decompress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, ref bool endOfData, out byte[] decompressed, out int remaining)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (count == 0 && m_bzstream.total_in == 0)
			{
				endOfData = true;
				decompressed = new byte[0];
				remaining = 0;
				return 0;
			}
			try
			{
				int num = count * 2;
				m_bzstream.next_in = buffer;
				m_bzstream.next_in_index = (uint)offset;
				m_bzstream.avail_in = (uint)count;
				if (count == 0)
				{
					num = 65536;
				}
				if (m_decompressionBuffer == null || m_decompressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_decompressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_decompressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				m_bzstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
				m_bzstream.next_out_index = 0u;
				m_bzstream.avail_out = (uint)num;
				ulong total_out = m_bzstream.total_out;
				if (endOfData)
				{
					byte[] array = new byte[count + 1];
					if (count > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(buffer, offset, array, 0, count);
					}
					m_bzstream.next_in = array;
					m_bzstream.next_in_index = 0u;
					m_bzstream.avail_in = (uint)(count + 1);
					BZLib.BZReturnValue bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompress(ref m_bzstream);
					while (true)
					{
						switch (bZReturnValue)
						{
						case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OUTBUFF_FULL:
						case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK:
						{
							m_bzstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
							m_bzstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
							m_bzstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out));
							ulong total_out2 = m_bzstream.total_out;
							bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompress(ref m_bzstream);
							if (total_out2 == m_bzstream.total_out)
							{
								bZReturnValue = BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END;
							}
							else if (m_bzstream.avail_in != 0)
							{
								num += ((num < 268435456) ? num : 134217728);
								byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
								m_decompressionBuffer.CopyTo(array2, 0);
								m_decompressionBuffer = array2;
							}
							continue;
						}
						case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END:
							break;
						default:
							BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref m_bzstream);
							throw new CompressionException("BZIP2: Expected end of stream not found");
						}
						break;
					}
					num = (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
					BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompressEnd(ref m_bzstream);
				}
				else
				{
					BZLib.BZReturnValue bZReturnValue2;
					do
					{
						m_bzstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
						m_bzstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_bzstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out));
						bZReturnValue2 = BZLib.BZ2_bzDecompress(ref m_bzstream);
						if (m_bzstream.avail_in != 0)
						{
							num += ((num < 268435456) ? num : 134217728);
							byte[] array3 = new byte[num];
							m_decompressionBuffer.CopyTo(array3, 0);
							m_decompressionBuffer = array3;
						}
					}
					while (m_bzstream.avail_out == 0 && m_bzstream.avail_in != 0 && bZReturnValue2 == BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK);
					switch (bZReturnValue2)
					{
					case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_OK:
						num = (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
						break;
					case BZLib.BZReturnValue.BZ_STREAM_END:
						num = (int)(m_bzstream.total_out - total_out);
						endOfData = true;
						break;
					default:
						throw new CompressionException("BZIP2: Expected end of stream not found");
					}
				}
				if (num > 0)
				{
					decompressed = m_decompressionBuffer;
				}
				else
				{
					decompressed = null;
				}
				remaining = (int)m_bzstream.avail_in;
				return num;
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ManagedDeflateCompressor : Compressor
	{
		private z_stream_s m_zstream;

		private byte[] m_compressionBuffer;

		private bool m_flushCalled;

		public virtual int Method => 8;

		public virtual int MaxWindowBits => 15;

		public ManagedDeflateCompressor(int compressionLevel)
			: this(compressionLevel, useZlibHeaders: false)
		{
		}

		public ManagedDeflateCompressor(int compressionLevel, bool useZlibHeaders)
		{
			try
			{
				m_zstream = new z_stream_s();
				int windowBits = (useZlibHeaders ? MaxWindowBits : (-MaxWindowBits));
				if (XZLib.DeflateInit2(m_zstream, compressionLevel, Method, windowBits, 8, CompressionStrategy.Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY) != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
				{
					throw new CompressionException("Unable to initialize the managed deflate compression engine.");
				}
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public override int Compress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, bool endOfData, out byte[] compressed)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			try
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (count == 0 && m_flushCalled && !endOfData)
				{
					compressed = new byte[0];
					return 0;
				}
				if (count > 0)
				{
					num = count + count / 9 + 12;
					m_zstream.next_in = buffer;
					m_zstream.next_in_index = (uint)offset;
					m_zstream.avail_in = (uint)count;
				}
				else
				{
					num = 65536;
					m_zstream.next_in = null;
					m_zstream.next_in_index = 0u;
					m_zstream.avail_in = 0u;
				}
				if (m_compressionBuffer == null || m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_compressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_compressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				m_zstream.next_out = m_compressionBuffer;
				m_zstream.next_out_index = 0u;
				m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)num;
				ulong total_out = m_zstream.total_out;
				if (endOfData)
				{
					ReturnCode returnCode = XZLib.Deflate(m_zstream, FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
					while (true)
					{
						switch (returnCode)
						{
						case ReturnCode.Z_OK:
							goto IL_0157;
						case ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END:
							break;
						default:
							throw new CompressionException("Expected end of stream not found");
						}
						break;
						IL_0157:
						num += 65536;
						byte[] array = new byte[num];
						m_compressionBuffer.CopyTo(array, 0);
						m_compressionBuffer = array;
						m_zstream.next_out = m_compressionBuffer;
						m_zstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out));
						returnCode = XZLib.Deflate(m_zstream, FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
					}
					num = (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
				}
				else
				{
					FlushValue flush = FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH;
					m_flushCalled = false;
					if (count == 0)
					{
						m_flushCalled = true;
						flush = FlushValue.Z_SYNC_FLUSH;
					}
					ReturnCode returnCode2 = XZLib.Deflate(m_zstream, flush);
					while (m_zstream.avail_out == 0 && m_zstream.avail_in != 0)
					{
						num += 65536;
						byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
						m_compressionBuffer.CopyTo(array2, 0);
						m_compressionBuffer = array2;
						m_zstream.next_out = m_compressionBuffer;
						m_zstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out));
						returnCode2 = XZLib.Deflate(m_zstream, flush);
					}
					if (returnCode2 != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
					{
						throw new CompressionException("Expected end of stream not found");
					}
					num = (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
				}
				compressed = m_compressionBuffer;
				return num;
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}

	internal class ManagedDeflateDecompressor : Decompressor
	{
		private z_stream_s m_zstream;

		private byte[] m_decompressionBuffer;

		public virtual int Method => 8;

		public virtual int MaxWindowBits => 15;

		public ManagedDeflateDecompressor()
			: this(useZlibHeaders: false)
		{
		}

		public ManagedDeflateDecompressor(bool useZlibHeaders)
		{
			try
			{
				m_zstream = new z_stream_s();
				int windowBits = (useZlibHeaders ? MaxWindowBits : (-MaxWindowBits));
				if (XZLib.InflateInit2(ref m_zstream, Method, windowBits) != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
				{
					throw new CompressionException("Unable to initialize the managed deflate decompression engine.");
				}
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}

		public override int Decompress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, ref bool endOfData, out byte[] decompressed, out int remaining)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (count == 0 && m_zstream.total_in == 0)
			{
				endOfData = true;
				decompressed = new byte[0];
				remaining = 0;
				return 0;
			}
			try
			{
				int num = count * 2;
				m_zstream.next_in = buffer;
				m_zstream.next_in_index = (uint)offset;
				m_zstream.avail_in = (uint)count;
				if (count == 0)
				{
					num = 65536;
				}
				if (m_decompressionBuffer == null || m_decompressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_decompressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_decompressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				m_zstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
				m_zstream.next_out_index = 0u;
				m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)num;
				ulong total_out = m_zstream.total_out;
				if (endOfData)
				{
					byte[] array = new byte[count + 1];
					if (count > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(buffer, offset, array, 0, count);
					}
					m_zstream.next_in = array;
					m_zstream.next_in_index = 0u;
					m_zstream.avail_in = (uint)(count + 1);
					ReturnCode returnCode = XZLib.Inflate(ref m_zstream, FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
					while (returnCode == ReturnCode.Z_OK || returnCode == ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR)
					{
						num += ((num < 268435456) ? num : 134217728);
						byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
						m_decompressionBuffer.CopyTo(array2, 0);
						m_decompressionBuffer = array2;
						m_zstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
						m_zstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out));
						ulong total_out2 = m_zstream.total_out;
						returnCode = XZLib.Inflate(ref m_zstream, FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
						if (total_out2 == m_zstream.total_out)
						{
							returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						}
					}
					m_zstream.avail_in--;
					if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END)
					{
						throw new CompressionException("Expected end of stream not found");
					}
					num = (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
				}
				else
				{
					FlushValue f = FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH;
					if (count == 0)
					{
						f = FlushValue.Z_SYNC_FLUSH;
					}
					ReturnCode returnCode2 = XZLib.Inflate(ref m_zstream, f);
					while (m_zstream.avail_out == 0 && m_zstream.avail_in != 0)
					{
						num += ((num < 268435456) ? num : 134217728);
						byte[] array3 = new byte[num];
						m_decompressionBuffer.CopyTo(array3, 0);
						m_decompressionBuffer = array3;
						m_zstream.next_out = m_decompressionBuffer;
						m_zstream.next_out_index = (uint)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						m_zstream.avail_out = (uint)(num - (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out));
						returnCode2 = XZLib.Inflate(ref m_zstream, f);
					}
					switch (returnCode2)
					{
					case ReturnCode.Z_OK:
						num = (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						break;
					case ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END:
						num = (int)(m_zstream.total_out - total_out);
						endOfData = true;
						break;
					default:
						throw new CompressionException("Expected end of stream not found");
					}
				}
				decompressed = m_decompressionBuffer;
				remaining = (int)m_zstream.avail_in;
				return num;
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}
