	internal class ManagedPPMdCompressor : Compressor
	{
		public const byte DEFAULT_MAX_ORDER = 16;

		public const byte DEFAULT_SUB_ALLOCATOR_SIZE = 64;

		public const int DEFAULT_MR_METHOD = 0;

		private byte[] m_compressionBuffer;

		private bool m_flushCalled;

		private object m_ppmdEncoder;

		private static Type m_ppmdEncoderType;

		public ManagedPPMdCompressor(Assembly ppmdAssembly)
			: this(16, 64, 0, ppmdAssembly)
		{
		}

		public ManagedPPMdCompressor(int maxOrder, int subAllocatorSize, int method, Assembly ppmdAssembly)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.PPMd);
			try
			{
				if (m_ppmdEncoderType == null)
				{
					m_ppmdEncoderType = ppmdAssembly.GetType("Xceed.Compression.PPMd.PPMdEncoder");
				}
				m_ppmdEncoder = Activator.CreateInstance(m_ppmdEncoderType);
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new TargetInvocationException("Xceed.Compression.PPMd.dll must be referenced as Copy Local in order to use PPMd CompressionMethod", inner);
			}
			if (method < 0 || method > 2)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("method", "Method of model restoration must be between 0 and 2 inclusively");
			}
			if (maxOrder < 2 || maxOrder > 16)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("maxOrder", "Maximum model order must be between 2 and 16 inclusively");
			}
			if (subAllocatorSize < 2 || subAllocatorSize > 256)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("subAllocatorSize", "SubAllocator size must be between 2 and 256 inclusively");
			}
			Initialize(maxOrder, subAllocatorSize, method);
		}

		public void Initialize(int maxOrder, int subAllocatorSize, int method)
		{
			try
			{
				object[] args = new object[3]
				{
					(uint)maxOrder,
					(uint)method,
					(uint)subAllocatorSize
				};
				m_ppmdEncoderType.InvokeMember("InitializeEncoder", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, m_ppmdEncoder, args);
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new TargetInvocationException("Error while calling InitializeEncoder method", inner);
			}
		}

		public override int Compress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, bool endOfData, out byte[] compressed)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			try
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (count == 0 && m_flushCalled && !endOfData)
				{
					compressed = new byte[0];
					return 0;
				}
				uint num2 = 0u;
				uint num3;
				if (count > 0)
				{
					num = count + count / 9 + 12;
					num3 = (uint)count;
				}
				else
				{
					num = 65536;
					num3 = 0u;
				}
				uint num4 = (uint)offset;
				if (m_compressionBuffer == null || m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_compressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_compressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				m_flushCalled = false;
				if (count == 0)
				{
					m_flushCalled = true;
				}
				num4 = (uint)offset;
				byte[] compressionBuffer;
				uint num6;
				do
				{
					compressionBuffer = m_compressionBuffer;
					uint num5 = num2;
					num6 = (uint)(num - num2);
					try
					{
						object[] array = new object[7]
						{
							buffer,
							num3,
							num4,
							compressionBuffer,
							num6,
							num5,
							endOfData ? 1 : 0
						};
						ParameterModifier[] array2 = new ParameterModifier[array.Length];
						ParameterModifier parameterModifier = new ParameterModifier(1);
						parameterModifier[0] = true;
						array2[1] = parameterModifier;
						array2[4] = parameterModifier;
						m_ppmdEncoderType.InvokeMember("EncodeStream", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, m_ppmdEncoder, array, array2, null, null);
						num3 = (uint)array[1];
						num6 = (uint)array[4];
					}
					catch (Exception inner)
					{
						throw new TargetInvocationException("Error while calling EncodeStream method", inner);
					}
					if (endOfData)
					{
						try
						{
							m_ppmdEncoderType.InvokeMember("UnInitializeEncoder", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, m_ppmdEncoder, new object[0]);
						}
						catch (Exception inner2)
						{
							throw new TargetInvocationException("Error while calling UnInitializeEncoder method", inner2);
						}
					}
					num2 += (uint)(int)(compressionBuffer.Length - num6);
					if (num6 == 0 && num3 != 0)
					{
						num += 65536;
						if (m_compressionBuffer.Length < num)
						{
							byte[] array3 = new byte[num];
							m_compressionBuffer.CopyTo(array3, 0);
							m_compressionBuffer = array3;
							array3 = null;
						}
					}
					num4 += (uint)(int)(count - num3);
				}
				while (num3 != 0);
				compressed = compressionBuffer;
				return (int)(compressionBuffer.Length - num6);
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ManagedPPMdDecompressor : Decompressor
	{
		private byte[] m_decompressionBuffer;

		private object m_ppmdDecoder;

		private static Type m_ppmdDecoderType;

		public ManagedPPMdDecompressor(Assembly ppmdAssembly)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.PPMd);
			try
			{
				if (m_ppmdDecoderType == null)
				{
					m_ppmdDecoderType = ppmdAssembly.GetType("Xceed.Compression.PPMd.PPMdDecoder");
				}
				m_ppmdDecoder = Activator.CreateInstance(m_ppmdDecoderType);
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new TargetInvocationException("Xceed.Compression.PPMd.dll must be referenced as Copy Local in order to use PPMd CompressionMethod", inner);
			}
		}

		public override int Decompress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, ref bool endOfData, out byte[] decompressed, out int remaining)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer", "The buffer parameter cannot be null.");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter must be zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (count == 0)
			{
				endOfData = true;
				decompressed = new byte[0];
				remaining = 0;
				try
				{
					m_ppmdDecoderType.InvokeMember("UnInitializeDecoder", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, m_ppmdDecoder, new object[0]);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException("Error while calling UnInitializeDecoder method", inner);
				}
				return 0;
			}
			try
			{
				int num = ((count != 0) ? (count * 2) : 65536);
				if (m_decompressionBuffer == null || m_decompressionBuffer.Length < num)
				{
					m_decompressionBuffer = new byte[num];
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_decompressionBuffer.Length;
				}
				uint num2 = (uint)count;
				uint num3 = (uint)m_decompressionBuffer.Length;
				uint num4 = (uint)offset;
				uint num5 = 0u;
				do
				{
					if (num3 == 0)
					{
						byte[] array = new byte[m_decompressionBuffer.Length * 2];
						m_decompressionBuffer.CopyTo(array, 0);
						num3 = (uint)(array.Length - m_decompressionBuffer.Length);
						m_decompressionBuffer = array;
					}
					uint num6 = 0u;
					try
					{
						object[] array2 = new object[7] { buffer, num2, num4, m_decompressionBuffer, num3, num5, endOfData };
						ParameterModifier[] array3 = new ParameterModifier[array2.Length];
						ParameterModifier parameterModifier = new ParameterModifier(1);
						parameterModifier[0] = true;
						array3[1] = parameterModifier;
						array3[4] = parameterModifier;
						array3[6] = parameterModifier;
						object obj = m_ppmdDecoderType.InvokeMember("DecodeStream", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, m_ppmdDecoder, array2, array3, null, null);
						num2 = (uint)array2[1];
						num3 = (uint)array2[4];
						endOfData = (bool)array2[6];
						num6 = (uint)obj;
					}
					catch (Exception inner2)
					{
						throw new TargetInvocationException("Error while calling DecodeStream method", inner2);
					}
					num4 = (uint)(offset + (count - num2));
					num5 = num6;
				}
				while (num2 != 0 && !endOfData);
				decompressed = m_decompressionBuffer;
				remaining = 0;
				return (int)(m_decompressionBuffer.Length - num3);
			}
			catch (CompressionException)
			{
				throw;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(ex2))
				{
					throw;
				}
				throw new CompressionInternalException(ex2);
			}
		}
	}
	public class QuickCompression
	{
		public static byte[] Compress(byte[] buffer)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			return Compress(buffer, 0, buffer.Length, CompressionMethod.Deflated, CompressionLevel.Normal);
		}

		public static byte[] Compress(byte[] buffer, CompressionMethod method, CompressionLevel level)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			return Compress(buffer, 0, buffer.Length, method, level);
		}

		public static byte[] Compress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, CompressionMethod method, CompressionLevel level)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(CompressionMethod), method))
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("method", method, "The compression method is not a valid CompressionMethod member.");
			}
			switch (method)
			{
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated:
			{
				Compressor compressor2 = new ManagedDeflateCompressor((int)level);
				byte[] compressed2;
				int num2 = compressor2.Compress(buffer, offset, count, endOfData: true, out compressed2);
				if (num2 < compressed2.Length)
				{
					byte[] array3 = new byte[num2];
					Buffer.BlockCopy(compressed2, 0, array3, 0, num2);
					return array3;
				}
				return compressed2;
			}
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
			{
				Compressor compressor = new ManagedDeflate64Compressor((int)level);
				byte[] compressed;
				int num = compressor.Compress(buffer, offset, count, endOfData: true, out compressed);
				if (num < compressed.Length)
				{
					byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
					Buffer.BlockCopy(compressed, 0, array2, 0, num);
					return array2;
				}
				return compressed;
			}
			case CompressionMethod.Stored:
			{
				byte[] array = new byte[count];
				Array.Copy(buffer, offset, array, 0, count);
				return array;
			}
			default:
				throw new CompressionInternalException();
			}
		}

		public static byte[] Decompress(byte[] buffer)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			return Decompress(buffer, 0, buffer.Length, CompressionMethod.Deflated);
		}

		public static byte[] Decompress(byte[] buffer, CompressionMethod method)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			return Decompress(buffer, 0, buffer.Length, method);
		}

		public static byte[] Decompress(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, CompressionMethod method)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count parameter exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(CompressionMethod), method))
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("method", method, "The compression method is not a valid DecompressionMethod member.");
			}
			switch (method)
			{
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated:
			{
				Decompressor decompressor2 = new ManagedDeflateDecompressor();
				bool endOfData2 = true;
				int remaining2 = 0;
				byte[] decompressed2;
				int num2 = decompressor2.Decompress(buffer, offset, count, ref endOfData2, out decompressed2, out remaining2);
				if (num2 < decompressed2.Length)
				{
					byte[] array3 = new byte[num2];
					Buffer.BlockCopy(decompressed2, 0, array3, 0, num2);
					return array3;
				}
				return decompressed2;
			}
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
			{
				Decompressor decompressor = new ManagedDeflate64Decompressor();
				bool endOfData = true;
				int remaining = 0;
				byte[] decompressed;
				int num = decompressor.Decompress(buffer, offset, count, ref endOfData, out decompressed, out remaining);
				if (num < decompressed.Length)
				{
					byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
					Buffer.BlockCopy(decompressed, 0, array2, 0, num);
					return array2;
				}
				return decompressed;
			}
			case CompressionMethod.Stored:
			{
				byte[] array = new byte[count];
				Array.Copy(buffer, offset, array, 0, count);
				return array;
			}
			default:
				throw new CompressionInternalException();
			}
		}

		private QuickCompression()
		{
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Exceptions
{
	internal class ExceptionFilter
	{
		public static bool IsPublicException(Exception exception)
		{
			if (!(exception is ExecutionEngineException) && !(exception is LicenseException) && !(exception is OutOfMemoryException) && !(exception is SecurityException) && !(exception is UnauthorizedAccessException) && !(exception is ArgumentException))
			{
				return exception is TargetInvocationException;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal class ThrowException
	{
		private ThrowException()
		{
		}

		public static void ThrowArgumentException(string message, string paramName, Exception innerExcept)
		{
			throw new ArgumentException(message, paramName, innerExcept);
		}

		public static void ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException(string paramName, object value, string message)
		{
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(paramName, value, message);
		}

		public static void ThrowLicenseException(Type type, object instance, string message)
		{
			throw new LicenseException(type, instance, message);
		}
	}
}

	internal class XceedLicenseManager
	{
		private const int RawKeyStringLen = 19;

		private const string LicKeyVersion = "A";

		private const byte SiteLicense = byte.MaxValue;

		private const uint BitCount = 65u;

		private const int BaseDateYear = 2001;

		private const int BaseDateMonth = 1;

		private const int BaseDateDay = 1;

		private const int NbLookupChar = 32;

		private const uint AdlerNMax = 5552u;

		private const uint AdlerBase = 65521u;

		private const string AlphaNumLookup = "ABJCKTDL4UEMW71FNX52YGP98Z63HRS0";

		private LicenseProduct m_Product;

		private byte m_VerMajor;

		private byte m_VerMinor;

		private LicenseOrigin m_Originator;

		private ushort m_UniqueSeed;

		private byte m_NbLicenses;

		private string m_Key;

		private DateTime m_Date;

		private static readonly string[] ProductIds = new string[38]
		{
			"", "ZIP", "SFX", "BKP", "WSL", "FTP", "SCO", "BEN", "CRY", "FTB",
			"ZIN", "ABZ", "GRD", "SCN", "ZIC", "SCC", "SUI", "SUN", "FTN", "FTC",
			"CHT", "DWN", "CHW", "IVN", "RDY", "EDN", "ZIL", "TAN", "DGF", "DGP",
			"WAN", "SYN", "ZIX", "ZII", "SFN", "ZRT", "ZRC", null
		};

		private static byte[] NbDaysBits = new byte[15]
		{
			2, 7, 12, 17, 22, 26, 31, 37, 42, 47,
			51, 55, 59, 62, 255
		};

		private static byte[] UniqueSeedBits = new byte[17]
		{
			64, 60, 56, 52, 48, 44, 39, 35, 32, 28,
			23, 19, 14, 10, 5, 1, 255
		};

		private static byte[] ProductCodeBits = new byte[7] { 3, 16, 29, 41, 53, 61, 255 };

		private static byte[] ProductVersionBits = new byte[8] { 4, 15, 25, 34, 43, 50, 58, 255 };

		private static byte[] NbLicensesBits = new byte[9] { 6, 13, 20, 30, 38, 40, 46, 57, 255 };

		private static byte[] OriginCodeBits = new byte[7] { 8, 11, 21, 24, 33, 49, 255 };

		private static byte[] ChecksumBits = new byte[9] { 0, 9, 18, 27, 36, 45, 54, 63, 255 };

		public LicenseProduct Product => m_Product;

		public string ProductCodeName => ProductIds[(int)m_Product];

		public byte VerMajor => m_VerMajor;

		public byte VerMinor => m_VerMinor;

		public LicenseOrigin Originator => m_Originator;

		public ushort UniqueSeed => m_UniqueSeed;

		public byte NbLicenses => m_NbLicenses;

		public bool IsSiteLicense => m_NbLicenses == byte.MaxValue;

		public DateTime Date => m_Date;

		public XceedLicenseManager()
		{
			m_Product = LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct;
			m_Originator = LicenseOrigin.InvalidOrigin;
			Reset();
		}

		public static string GetProductCodeName(LicenseProduct product)
		{
			return ProductIds[(int)product];
		}

		public void AnalyzeLicense(string key)
		{
			Reset();
			CopyKeyNoDashes(key);
			if (m_Key.Length != 19)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.InvalidKeyLength);
			}
			if (!m_Key.EndsWith("A"))
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.UnsupportedKey);
			}
			DecodeEncodedPart();
			if (m_Product < LicenseProduct.XceedZip || m_Product > LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNETCF)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.InvalidProductCode);
			}
			if (m_Originator < LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion || m_Originator > LicenseOrigin.CRM)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.InvalidOriginCode);
			}
			if (m_VerMajor > 9 || m_VerMinor > 9)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.InvalidVersion);
			}
			DecodeReadablePart();
		}

		private void CopyKeyNoDashes(string key)
		{
			m_Key = "";
			if (key == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < key.Length; i++)
			{
				if (key[i] != '-')
				{
					m_Key += key[i];
				}
			}
		}

		private void DecodeEncodedPart()
		{
			uint num = 8u;
			num++;
			byte[] bits = new byte[num];
			DecodeAlphaNumString(bits, num);
			uint num2 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ChecksumBits);
			MapNumberToBits(bits, num, 0u, ChecksumBits);
			if (num2 != CalculateChecksum(bits, num))
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.ChecksumFailed);
			}
			uint num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, NbDaysBits);
			m_Date = new DateTime(2001, 1, 1).AddDays(num3);
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, UniqueSeedBits);
			m_UniqueSeed = (ushort)num3;
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ProductCodeBits);
			m_Product = (LicenseProduct)num3;
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ProductVersionBits);
			m_VerMajor = (byte)(num3 / 10);
			m_VerMinor = (byte)(num3 % 10);
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, NbLicensesBits);
			m_NbLicenses = (byte)num3;
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, OriginCodeBits);
			m_Originator = (LicenseOrigin)num3;
		}

		private void DecodeReadablePart()
		{
			if (!m_Key.StartsWith(ProductIds[(int)m_Product]))
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.ProductMismatch);
			}
			if (Convert.ToByte(m_Key.Substring(3, 1), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) != m_VerMajor || Convert.ToByte(m_Key.Substring(4, 1), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) != m_VerMinor)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode.VersionMismatch);
			}
		}

		private void DecodeAlphaNumString(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen)
		{
			ushort num = 11;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			uint num3 = 0u;
			uint num4 = 5u;
			while (num2 < bitsLen && num3 < 65)
			{
				ushort alphaNumValue = GetAlphaNumValue(m_Key[(int)num4++]);
				alphaNumValue = (ushort)(alphaNumValue << (int)num);
				bits[num2] |= (byte)((alphaNumValue & 0xFF00) >> 8);
				bits[num2 + 1] |= (byte)(alphaNumValue & 0xFF);
				if (num < 8)
				{
					num += 3;
					num2++;
				}
				else
				{
					num -= 5;
				}
				num3 += 5;
			}
		}

		private static uint MapBitsToNumber(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen, byte[] bitMapping)
		{
			if (bits == null || bitsLen == 0)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			if (bitMapping == null)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			uint num = 0u;
			for (uint num2 = 0u; bitMapping[num2] != byte.MaxValue; num2++)
			{
				byte b = bitMapping[num2];
				byte b2 = (byte)(1 << 7 - b % 8);
				if ((bits[b / 8] & b2) != 0)
				{
					num |= (uint)(1 << (int)num2);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static void MapNumberToBits(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen, uint number, byte[] bitMapping)
		{
			if (bits == null || bitsLen == 0)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			if (bitMapping == null)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			int num = 0;
			byte b = 0;
			for (num = 0; bitMapping[num] != byte.MaxValue; num++)
			{
				if (bitMapping[num] > b)
				{
					b = bitMapping[num];
				}
			}
			if (number >= (uint)(1 << num))
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			if (bitsLen * 8 <= b)
			{
				throw new XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				bool flag = (number & (1 << i)) != 0;
				byte b2 = bitMapping[i];
				byte b3 = (byte)(1 << 7 - b2 % 8);
				if (flag)
				{
					bits[b2 / 8] |= b3;
				}
				else
				{
					bits[b2 / 8] &= (byte)(~b3);
				}
			}
		}

		private static ushort GetAlphaNumValue(char alphaNum)
		{
			for (ushort num = 0; num < 32; num++)
			{
				if ("ABJCKTDL4UEMW71FNX52YGP98Z63HRS0"[num] == alphaNum)
				{
					return num;
				}
			}
			return ushort.MaxValue;
		}

		private void Reset()
		{
			m_Key = "";
			m_Product = LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct;
			m_VerMajor = 0;
			m_VerMinor = 0;
			m_Originator = LicenseOrigin.InvalidOrigin;
			m_NbLicenses = 0;
			m_UniqueSeed = 0;
			m_Date = DateTime.Now;
		}

		private byte CalculateChecksum(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen)
		{
			uint adler = Adler32(0u, m_Key.ToCharArray(), 5u);
			adler = Adler32(adler, bits, bitsLen);
			ushort num = (ushort)(((adler & 0xFFFF0000u) >> 16) ^ (adler & 0xFFFF));
			return (byte)(((num & 0xFF00) >> 8) ^ (num & 0xFF));
		}

		private static uint Adler32(uint adler, Array buffer, uint bufLen)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				return 1u;
			}
			uint num = adler & 0xFFFF;
			uint num2 = (adler >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
			int num3 = 0;
			while (bufLen != 0)
			{
				uint num4 = ((bufLen < 5552) ? bufLen : 5552u);
				bufLen -= num4;
				while (true)
				{
					switch (num4)
					{
					default:
					{
						int num5 = 0;
						while (num5 < 16)
						{
							num += Convert.ToUInt32(buffer.GetValue(num3), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
							num2 += num;
							num5++;
							num3++;
						}
						num4 -= 16;
						continue;
					}
					case 1u:
					case 2u:
					case 3u:
					case 4u:
					case 5u:
					case 6u:
					case 7u:
					case 8u:
					case 9u:
					case 10u:
					case 11u:
					case 12u:
					case 13u:
					case 14u:
					case 15u:
						do
						{
							num += Convert.ToUInt32(buffer.GetValue(num3), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
							num3++;
							num2 += num;
						}
						while (--num4 != 0);
						break;
					case 0u:
						break;
					}
					break;
				}
				num %= 65521;
				num2 %= 65521;
			}
			return (num2 << 16) | num;
		}
	}
	internal class XceedLicenseManagerException : ApplicationException
	{
		public XceedLicenseManagerException()
			: base("Error reading the license key")
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(string message, Exception inner)
			: base(message, inner)
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(LicenseErrorCode errorCode)
			: base(errorCode.ToString())
		{
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Streams
{
	internal class CombinedStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private byte[] m_leadingBytes;

		private long m_initialPosition;

		private int m_leadingBytesRead;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite => false;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_innerStream.Length < m_initialPosition)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The length of the inner stream must be greater than its initial position.");
				}
				return m_leadingBytes.Length + m_innerStream.Length - m_initialPosition;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				long position = m_innerStream.Position;
				if (position < m_initialPosition)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream's current position must be greater than its initial position.");
				}
				if (m_leadingBytesRead != m_leadingBytes.Length)
				{
					return m_leadingBytesRead;
				}
				return m_leadingBytesRead + position - m_initialPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (!CanSeek)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream is not seekable");
				}
				if (value < 0)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot seek before the beginning of the stream.");
				}
				if (value < m_leadingBytes.Length)
				{
					m_leadingBytesRead = (int)value;
					return;
				}
				m_leadingBytesRead = m_leadingBytes.Length;
				m_innerStream.Position = value - m_leadingBytes.Length + m_initialPosition;
			}
		}

		public CombinedStream(byte[] buffer, Stream inner)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			try
			{
				m_initialPosition = inner.Position;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			m_leadingBytes = buffer;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanSeek)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream is not seekable");
			}
			switch (origin)
			{
			case SeekOrigin.Begin:
				Position = offset;
				break;
			case SeekOrigin.End:
				Position = Length + offset;
				break;
			case SeekOrigin.Current:
				Position += offset;
				break;
			}
			return Position;
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (value < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", value, "The length of the stream must be greater than zero.");
			}
			if (value < m_leadingBytes.Length)
			{
				m_innerStream.SetLength(m_initialPosition);
				byte[] array = new byte[value];
				if (value > 0)
				{
					Array.Copy(m_leadingBytes, 0, array, 0, (int)value);
				}
				m_leadingBytes = array;
				if (m_leadingBytesRead > value)
				{
					m_leadingBytesRead = (int)value;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				m_innerStream.SetLength(value - m_leadingBytes.Length + m_initialPosition);
			}
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream does not support reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset must be greater than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "Count must be greater than zero.");
			}
			if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
			{
				ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "Count must be less than the buffer's remaining length after the specified offset.");
			}
			if (m_leadingBytesRead < m_leadingBytes.Length)
			{
				if (m_leadingBytes.Length - m_leadingBytesRead < count)
				{
					count = m_leadingBytes.Length - m_leadingBytesRead;
				}
				Array.Copy(m_leadingBytes, m_leadingBytesRead, buffer, offset, count);
				m_leadingBytesRead += count;
				return count;
			}
			return m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("A CombinedStream object does not support writing.");
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				try
				{
					Flush();
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (disposing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_leadingBytes = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("CombinedStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private CombinedStream()
		{
		}

		~CombinedStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Compression.CompressionEngine.Managed
{
	internal class InfBlock
	{
		internal enum inflate_block_mode
		{
			TYPE,
			LENS,
			STORED,
			TABLE,
			BTREE,
			DTREE,
			CODES,
			DRY,
			DONE,
			BAD
		}

		internal class inflate_blocks_state
		{
			internal inflate_block_mode mode;

			internal uint left;

			internal uint table;

			internal uint index;

			internal uint[] blens;

			internal uint bb;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tb;

			internal InfCodes.inflate_codes_state codes;

			internal uint treetype;

			internal uint bl;

			internal uint bd;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tl;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] td;

			internal uint last;

			internal uint bitk;

			internal uint bitb;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] hufts;

			internal byte[] window;

			internal uint end;

			internal uint read;

			internal uint write;

			internal bool checkfn;

			internal uint check;
		}

		private static uint[] border = new uint[19]
		{
			16u, 17u, 18u, 0u, 8u, 7u, 9u, 6u, 10u, 5u,
			11u, 4u, 12u, 3u, 13u, 2u, 14u, 1u, 15u
		};

		internal static uint[] inflate_mask = new uint[17]
		{
			0u, 1u, 3u, 7u, 15u, 31u, 63u, 127u, 255u, 511u,
			1023u, 2047u, 4095u, 8191u, 16383u, 32767u, 65535u
		};

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_blocks(ref inflate_blocks_state s, ref z_stream_s z, ReturnCode r)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			uint b = 0u;
			uint k = 0u;
			uint p = 0u;
			uint n = 0u;
			uint q = 0u;
			uint m = 0u;
			ReturnCode returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			LOAD(ref p, ref n, ref b, ref k, ref q, ref m, z, s);
			while (true)
			{
				switch (s.mode)
				{
				case inflate_block_mode.TYPE:
					if (!NEEDBITS(3u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					num = b & 7;
					s.last = num & 1;
					switch (num >> 1)
					{
					case 0u:
						DUMPBITS(3u, ref b, ref k);
						num = k & 7;
						DUMPBITS(num, ref b, ref k);
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.LENS;
						break;
					case 1u:
						if (s.treetype != ((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod)
						{
							s.treetype = (uint)((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod;
							InfTrees.inflate_trees_fixed(((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod, ref s.bl, ref s.bd, ref s.tl, ref s.td, s.hufts);
						}
						s.codes = InfCodes.inflate_codes_new(s.bl, s.bd, s.tl, s.td);
						if (s.codes == null)
						{
							r = ReturnCode.Z_MEM_ERROR;
							return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
						}
						DUMPBITS(3u, ref b, ref k);
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.CODES;
						break;
					case 2u:
						DUMPBITS(3u, ref b, ref k);
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.TABLE;
						break;
					case 3u:
						DUMPBITS(3u, ref b, ref k);
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "invalid block type";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.LENS:
					if (!NEEDBITS(32u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					if (((~b >> 16) & 0xFFFF) != (b & 0xFFFF))
					{
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "invalid stored block lengths";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					s.left = b & 0xFFFF;
					b = (k = 0u);
					s.mode = ((s.left != 0) ? inflate_block_mode.STORED : ((s.last != 0) ? inflate_block_mode.DRY : inflate_block_mode.TYPE));
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.STORED:
					if (n == 0)
					{
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					if (m == 0)
					{
						WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
						if (m == 0)
						{
							FLUSH(s, z, ref m, ref q, ref r);
							WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
							if (m == 0)
							{
								UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
								return inflate_flush(s, z, r);
							}
						}
					}
					r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
					num = s.left;
					if (num > n)
					{
						num = n;
					}
					if (num > m)
					{
						num = m;
					}
					try
					{
						Array.Copy(z.next_in, (int)p, s.window, (int)q, (int)num);
					}
					catch (ArgumentOutOfRangeException)
					{
						z.msg = "Out of range exception";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_INTERNAL_ERROR;
						LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					catch (ArgumentException)
					{
						z.msg = "Argument exception";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_INTERNAL_ERROR;
						LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						z.msg = "Unknown exception";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_INTERNAL_ERROR;
						LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					p += num;
					n -= num;
					q += num;
					m -= num;
					if ((s.left -= num) == 0)
					{
						s.mode = ((s.last != 0) ? inflate_block_mode.DRY : inflate_block_mode.TYPE);
					}
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.TABLE:
					if (!NEEDBITS(14u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					num = (s.table = b & 0x3FFF);
					if ((num & 0x1F) > 29)
					{
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "too many length or distance symbols";
						r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					num = 258 + (num & 0x1F) + ((num >> 5) & 0x1F);
					s.blens = new uint[num];
					DUMPBITS(14u, ref b, ref k);
					s.index = 0u;
					s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BTREE;
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.BTREE:
					while (s.index < 4 + (s.table >> 10))
					{
						if (!NEEDBITS(3u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
						{
							return r;
						}
						s.blens[border[s.index++]] = b & 7;
						DUMPBITS(3u, ref b, ref k);
					}
					while (s.index < 19)
					{
						s.blens[border[s.index++]] = 0u;
					}
					s.bb = 7u;
					returnCode = InfTrees.inflate_trees_bits(s.blens, ref s.bb, ref s.tb, s.hufts, z);
					if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
					{
						r = returnCode;
						if (r == ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR)
						{
							s.blens = null;
							s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
						}
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					s.index = 0u;
					s.mode = inflate_block_mode.DTREE;
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.DTREE:
				{
					for (num = s.table; s.index < 258 + (num & 0x1F) + ((num >> 5) & 0x1F); num = s.table)
					{
						InfTrees.inflate_huft inflate_huft = null;
						num = s.bb;
						if (!NEEDBITS(num, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
						{
							return r;
						}
						inflate_huft = s.tb[b & inflate_mask[num]];
						num = inflate_huft.bits;
						uint num2 = inflate_huft._base;
						int num3;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 0u:
						case 1u:
						case 2u:
						case 3u:
						case 4u:
						case 5u:
						case 6u:
						case 7u:
						case 8u:
						case 9u:
						case 10u:
						case 11u:
						case 12u:
						case 13u:
						case 14u:
						case 15u:
							DUMPBITS(num, ref b, ref k);
							s.blens[s.index++] = num2;
							continue;
						default:
							num3 = (int)(num2 - 14);
							break;
						case 18u:
							num3 = 7;
							break;
						}
						uint num4 = (uint)num3;
						uint num5 = ((num2 == 18) ? 11u : 3u);
						if (!NEEDBITS(num + num4, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
						{
							return r;
						}
						DUMPBITS(num, ref b, ref k);
						num5 += b & inflate_mask[num4];
						DUMPBITS(num4, ref b, ref k);
						num4 = s.index;
						num = s.table;
						if (num4 + num5 > 258 + (num & 0x1F) + ((num >> 5) & 0x1F) || (num2 == 16 && num4 < 1))
						{
							s.blens = null;
							s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
							z.msg = "invalid bit length repeat";
							r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
							return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
						}
						num2 = ((num2 == 16) ? s.blens[num4 - 1] : 0u);
						do
						{
							s.blens[num4++] = num2;
						}
						while (--num5 != 0);
						s.index = num4;
					}
					s.tb = null;
					InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tl = null;
					InfTrees.inflate_huft[] td = null;
					InfCodes.inflate_codes_state inflate_codes_state = null;
					uint bl = 9u;
					uint bd = 6u;
					num = s.table;
					s.treetype = 0u;
					returnCode = InfTrees.inflate_trees_dynamic(((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod, 257 + (num & 0x1F), 1 + ((num >> 5) & 0x1F), ref s.blens, ref bl, ref bd, ref tl, ref td, ref s.hufts, z);
					if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
					{
						if (returnCode == ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR)
						{
							s.blens = null;
							s.mode = inflate_block_mode.BAD;
						}
						r = returnCode;
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					if ((inflate_codes_state = InfCodes.inflate_codes_new(bl, bd, tl, td)) == null)
					{
						r = ReturnCode.Z_MEM_ERROR;
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					s.codes = inflate_codes_state;
					s.blens = null;
					s.mode = inflate_block_mode.CODES;
					break;
				}
				case inflate_block_mode.CODES:
					UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
					if ((r = InfCodes.inflate_codes(s, z, r)) != ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END)
					{
						return inflate_flush(s, z, r);
					}
					r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
					InfCodes.inflate_codes_free(ref s.codes);
					LOAD(ref p, ref n, ref b, ref k, ref q, ref m, z, s);
					if (s.last == 0)
					{
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.TYPE;
					}
					else
					{
						s.mode = inflate_block_mode.DRY;
					}
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.DRY:
					FLUSH(s, z, ref m, ref q, ref r);
					if (s.read != s.write)
					{
						return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					s.mode = inflate_block_mode.DONE;
					break;
				case inflate_block_mode.DONE:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
					return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				case inflate_block_mode.BAD:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				default:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
					return LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_blocks_free(ref inflate_blocks_state s, ref z_stream_s z)
		{
			uint c = 0u;
			inflate_blocks_reset(s, ref z, ref c);
			s.window = null;
			s.hufts = null;
			s = null;
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static void inflate_blocks_reset(inflate_blocks_state s, ref z_stream_s z, ref uint c)
		{
			c = s.check;
			if (s.mode == inflate_block_mode.BTREE || s.mode == inflate_block_mode.DTREE)
			{
				s.blens = null;
			}
			if (s.mode == inflate_block_mode.CODES)
			{
				InfCodes.inflate_codes_free(ref s.codes);
			}
			s.mode = inflate_block_mode.TYPE;
			s.treetype = 0u;
			s.bitk = 0u;
			s.bitb = 0u;
			s.read = (s.write = 0u);
			if (s.checkfn)
			{
				z.adler = (s.check = XZLib.Adler32(0u, null, 0u, 0u));
			}
		}

		internal static inflate_blocks_state inflate_blocks_new(ref z_stream_s z, bool check, uint w)
		{
			inflate_blocks_state inflate_blocks_state = null;
			inflate_blocks_state = new inflate_blocks_state();
			inflate_blocks_state.hufts = new InfTrees.inflate_huft[1440];
			inflate_blocks_state.window = new byte[w];
			inflate_blocks_state.end = w;
			inflate_blocks_state.checkfn = check;
			inflate_blocks_state.mode = inflate_block_mode.TYPE;
			uint c = 0u;
			inflate_blocks_reset(inflate_blocks_state, ref z, ref c);
			return inflate_blocks_state;
		}

		internal static void LOAD(ref uint p, ref uint n, ref uint b, ref uint k, ref uint q, ref uint m, z_stream_s z, inflate_blocks_state s)
		{
			p = z.next_in_index;
			n = z.avail_in;
			b = s.bitb;
			k = s.bitk;
			q = s.write;
			m = ((q < s.read) ? (s.read - q - 1) : (s.end - q));
		}

		internal static void UPDATE(inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, uint b, uint k, uint n, uint p, uint q)
		{
			s.bitb = b;
			s.bitk = k;
			z.avail_in = n;
			z.total_in += p - z.next_in_index;
			z.next_in_index = p;
			s.write = q;
		}

		internal static bool NEEDBITS(uint j, ref uint k, ref uint b, ref uint n, ref uint p, uint q, inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, ref ReturnCode r)
		{
			while (k < j)
			{
				if (n != 0)
				{
					r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
					b |= (uint)(NEXTBYTE(ref n, ref p, z) << (int)k);
					k += 8u;
					continue;
				}
				r = LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal static void DUMPBITS(uint j, ref uint b, ref uint k)
		{
			b >>= (int)j;
			k -= j;
		}

		internal static byte NEXTBYTE(ref uint n, ref uint p, z_stream_s z)
		{
			n--;
			return z.next_in[p++];
		}

		internal static void GRABBITS(uint j, ref uint k, ref uint b, ref uint n, ref uint p, z_stream_s z)
		{
			while (k < j)
			{
				b |= (uint)(NEXTBYTE(ref n, ref p, z) << (int)k);
				k += 8u;
			}
		}

		internal static void UNGRAB(ref uint c, ref uint n, ref uint k, ref uint p, z_stream_s z)
		{
			c = z.avail_in - n;
			c = ((k >> 3 < c) ? (k >> 3) : c);
			n += c;
			p -= c;
			k -= c << 3;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode LEAVE(uint b, uint k, uint n, uint p, uint q, inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, ReturnCode r)
		{
			UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
			return inflate_flush(s, z, r);
		}

		internal static void WRAP(ref uint q, ref uint m, inflate_blocks_state s)
		{
			if (q == s.end && s.read != 0)
			{
				q = 0u;
				m = ((q < s.read) ? (s.read - q - 1) : (s.end - q));
			}
		}

		internal static void FLUSH(inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, ref uint m, ref uint q, ref ReturnCode r)
		{
			s.write = q;
			r = inflate_flush(s, z, r);
			q = s.write;
			m = ((q < s.read) ? (s.read - q - 1) : (s.end - q));
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_flush(inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, ReturnCode r)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			num = z.next_out_index;
			num2 = s.read;
			uint num3 = ((num2 <= s.write) ? s.write : s.end) - num2;
			if (num3 > z.avail_out)
			{
				num3 = z.avail_out;
			}
			if (num3 != 0 && r == ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR)
			{
				r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			}
			z.avail_out -= num3;
			z.total_out += num3;
			if (s.checkfn)
			{
				z.adler = (s.check = XZLib.Adler32(s.check, s.window, num2, num3));
			}
			Array.Copy(s.window, (int)num2, z.next_out, (int)num, (int)num3);
			num += num3;
			num2 += num3;
			if (num2 == s.end)
			{
				num2 = 0u;
				if (s.write == s.end)
				{
					s.write = 0u;
				}
				num3 = s.write - num2;
				if (num3 > z.avail_out)
				{
					num3 = z.avail_out;
				}
				if (num3 != 0 && r == ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR)
				{
					r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
				}
				z.avail_out -= num3;
				z.total_out += num3;
				if (s.checkfn)
				{
					z.adler = (s.check = XZLib.Adler32(s.check, s.window, num2, num3));
				}
				Array.Copy(s.window, (int)num2, z.next_out, (int)num, (int)num3);
				num += num3;
				num2 += num3;
			}
			z.next_out_index = num;
			s.read = num2;
			return r;
		}
	}
	internal class InfCodes
	{
		internal enum inflate_codes_mode
		{
			START,
			LEN,
			LENEXT,
			DIST,
			DISTEXT,
			COPY,
			LIT,
			WASH,
			END,
			BADCODE
		}

		internal class inflate_codes_state
		{
			internal inflate_codes_mode mode;

			internal uint len;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tree;

			internal uint tree_ndx;

			internal uint need;

			internal uint lit;

			internal uint _get;

			internal uint dist;

			internal byte lbits;

			internal byte dbits;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] ltree;

			internal uint ltree_ndx;

			internal InfTrees.inflate_huft[] dtree;

			internal uint dtree_ndx;
		}

		internal static void inflate_codes_free(ref inflate_codes_state c)
		{
			c = null;
		}

		internal static inflate_codes_state inflate_codes_new(uint bl, uint bd, InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tl, InfTrees.inflate_huft[] td)
		{
			inflate_codes_state inflate_codes_state = null;
			inflate_codes_state = new inflate_codes_state();
			inflate_codes_state.mode = inflate_codes_mode.START;
			inflate_codes_state.lbits = (byte)bl;
			inflate_codes_state.dbits = (byte)bd;
			inflate_codes_state.ltree = tl;
			inflate_codes_state.ltree_ndx = 0u;
			inflate_codes_state.dtree = td;
			inflate_codes_state.dtree_ndx = 0u;
			return inflate_codes_state;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_codes(InfBlock.inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z, ReturnCode r)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			int num2 = 0;
			uint num3 = 0u;
			uint b = 0u;
			uint k = 0u;
			uint p = 0u;
			uint n = 0u;
			uint q = 0u;
			uint m = 0u;
			int num4 = 0;
			inflate_codes_state codes = s.codes;
			uint num5 = 258u;
			if (((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod == 9)
			{
				num5 = 65535u;
			}
			InfBlock.LOAD(ref p, ref n, ref b, ref k, ref q, ref m, z, s);
			while (true)
			{
				switch (codes.mode)
				{
				case inflate_codes_mode.START:
					if (m >= num5 && n >= 10)
					{
						InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
						r = inflate_fast(codes.lbits, codes.dbits, codes.ltree, codes.ltree_ndx, codes.dtree, codes.dtree_ndx, s, z);
						InfBlock.LOAD(ref p, ref n, ref b, ref k, ref q, ref m, z, s);
						if (r != ReturnCode.Z_OK)
						{
							codes.mode = ((r == ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END) ? inflate_codes_mode.WASH : inflate_codes_mode.BADCODE);
							break;
						}
					}
					codes.need = codes.lbits;
					codes.tree = codes.ltree;
					codes.tree_ndx = codes.ltree_ndx;
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.LEN;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.LEN:
					num = codes.need;
					if (!InfBlock.NEEDBITS(num, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					num2 = (int)(codes.tree_ndx + (b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num]));
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(codes.tree[num2].bits, ref b, ref k);
					num3 = codes.tree[num2].exop;
					if (num3 == 0)
					{
						codes.lit = codes.tree[num2]._base;
						codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.LIT;
						break;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0x20) != 0)
					{
						codes._get = num3 & 0x1F;
						codes.len = codes.tree[num2]._base;
						codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.LENEXT;
						break;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0xE0) == 0)
					{
						codes.need = num3;
						codes.tree_ndx = (uint)num2 + codes.tree[num2]._base;
						break;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0x40) != 0)
					{
						codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.WASH;
						break;
					}
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.BADCODE;
					z.msg = "invalid literal/length code";
					r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				case inflate_codes_mode.LENEXT:
					num = codes._get;
					if (!InfBlock.NEEDBITS(num, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					codes.len += b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num];
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(num, ref b, ref k);
					codes.need = codes.dbits;
					codes.tree = codes.dtree;
					codes.tree_ndx = codes.dtree_ndx;
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.DIST;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.DIST:
					num = codes.need;
					if (!InfBlock.NEEDBITS(num, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					num2 = (int)(codes.tree_ndx + (b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num]));
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(codes.tree[num2].bits, ref b, ref k);
					num3 = codes.tree[num2].exop;
					if ((num3 & 0x20) != 0)
					{
						codes._get = num3 & 0x1F;
						codes.dist = codes.tree[num2]._base;
						codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.DISTEXT;
						break;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0xE0) == 0)
					{
						codes.need = num3;
						codes.tree_ndx = (uint)num2 + codes.tree[num2]._base;
						break;
					}
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.BADCODE;
					z.msg = "invalid distance code";
					r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				case inflate_codes_mode.DISTEXT:
					num = codes._get;
					if (!InfBlock.NEEDBITS(num, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, q, s, z, ref r))
					{
						return r;
					}
					codes.dist += b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num];
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(num, ref b, ref k);
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.COPY;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.COPY:
					for (num4 = (int)(q - codes.dist); num4 < 0; num4 += (int)s.end)
					{
					}
					while (codes.len != 0)
					{
						if (m == 0)
						{
							InfBlock.WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
							if (m == 0)
							{
								InfBlock.FLUSH(s, z, ref m, ref q, ref r);
								InfBlock.WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
								if (m == 0)
								{
									InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
									return InfBlock.inflate_flush(s, z, r);
								}
							}
						}
						r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
						s.window[q++] = s.window[num4++];
						m--;
						if (num4 == s.end)
						{
							num4 = 0;
						}
						codes.len--;
					}
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.START;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.LIT:
					if (m == 0)
					{
						InfBlock.WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
						if (m == 0)
						{
							InfBlock.FLUSH(s, z, ref m, ref q, ref r);
							InfBlock.WRAP(ref q, ref m, s);
							if (m == 0)
							{
								InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
								return InfBlock.inflate_flush(s, z, r);
							}
						}
					}
					r = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
					s.window[q++] = (byte)codes.lit;
					m--;
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.START;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.WASH:
					if (k > 7)
					{
						k -= 8;
						n++;
						p--;
					}
					InfBlock.FLUSH(s, z, ref m, ref q, ref r);
					if (s.read != s.write)
					{
						return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
					}
					codes.mode = inflate_codes_mode.END;
					break;
				case inflate_codes_mode.END:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
					return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				case inflate_codes_mode.BADCODE:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				default:
					r = ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
					return InfBlock.LEAVE(b, k, n, p, q, s, z, r);
				}
			}
		}

		private static ReturnCode inflate_fast(uint bl, uint bd, InfTrees.inflate_huft[] tl, uint tl_ndx, InfTrees.inflate_huft[] td, uint td_ndx, InfBlock.inflate_blocks_state s, z_stream_s z)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			InfTrees.inflate_huft[] array = null;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			uint num3 = 0u;
			uint b = 0u;
			uint k = 0u;
			uint p = 0u;
			uint n = 0u;
			uint q = 0u;
			uint m = 0u;
			uint num4 = 0u;
			uint num5 = 0u;
			uint c = 0u;
			uint num6 = 0u;
			int num7 = 0;
			InfBlock.LOAD(ref p, ref n, ref b, ref k, ref q, ref m, z, s);
			num4 = InfBlock.inflate_mask[bl];
			num5 = InfBlock.inflate_mask[bd];
			uint num8 = 258u;
			if (((XInflate.internal_state)z.state).compMethod == 9)
			{
				num8 = 65535u;
			}
			do
			{
				InfBlock.GRABBITS(15u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, z);
				num = b & num4;
				array = tl;
				num2 = tl_ndx;
				if ((num3 = array[num2 + num].exop) == 0)
				{
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(array[num2 + num].bits, ref b, ref k);
					s.window[q++] = (byte)array[num2 + num]._base;
					m--;
					continue;
				}
				while (true)
				{
					InfBlock.DUMPBITS(array[num2 + num].bits, ref b, ref k);
					if ((num3 & 0x20) != 0)
					{
						num3 &= 0x1F;
						InfBlock.GRABBITS(num3, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, z);
						c = array[num2 + num]._base + (b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num3]);
						InfBlock.DUMPBITS(num3, ref b, ref k);
						InfBlock.GRABBITS(15u, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, z);
						num = b & num5;
						array = td;
						num2 = td_ndx;
						num3 = array[num2 + num].exop;
						while (true)
						{
							InfBlock.DUMPBITS(array[num2 + num].bits, ref b, ref k);
							if ((num3 & 0x20) != 0)
							{
								break;
							}
							if ((num3 & 0xE0) == 0)
							{
								num += array[num2 + num]._base;
								num += b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num3];
								num3 = array[num2 + num].exop;
								continue;
							}
							z.msg = "invalid distance code";
							InfBlock.UNGRAB(ref c, ref n, ref k, ref p, z);
							InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
							return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						}
						num3 &= 0x1F;
						InfBlock.GRABBITS(num3, ref k, ref b, ref n, ref p, z);
						num6 = array[num2 + num]._base + (b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num3]);
						InfBlock.DUMPBITS(num3, ref b, ref k);
						m -= c;
						num7 = (int)(q - num6);
						if (num7 < 0)
						{
							do
							{
								num7 += (int)s.end;
							}
							while (num7 < 0);
							num3 = s.end - (uint)num7;
							if (c > num3)
							{
								c -= num3;
								do
								{
									s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
								}
								while (--num3 != 0);
								num7 = 0;
								do
								{
									s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
								}
								while (--c != 0);
							}
							else
							{
								s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
								c--;
								s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
								c--;
								do
								{
									s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
								}
								while (--c != 0);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
							c--;
							s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
							c--;
							do
							{
								s.window[q++] = s.window[num7++];
							}
							while (--c != 0);
						}
						break;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0xE0) == 0)
					{
						num += array[num2 + num]._base;
						num += b & InfBlock.inflate_mask[num3];
						if ((num3 = array[num2 + num].exop) == 0)
						{
							InfBlock.DUMPBITS(array[num2 + num].bits, ref b, ref k);
							s.window[q++] = (byte)array[num2 + num]._base;
							m--;
							break;
						}
						continue;
					}
					if ((num3 & 0x40) != 0)
					{
						InfBlock.UNGRAB(ref c, ref n, ref k, ref p, z);
						InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
						return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
					}
					z.msg = "invalid literal/length code";
					InfBlock.UNGRAB(ref c, ref n, ref k, ref p, z);
					InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
					return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
				}
			}
			while (m >= num8 && n >= 10);
			InfBlock.UNGRAB(ref c, ref n, ref k, ref p, z);
			InfBlock.UPDATE(s, z, b, k, n, p, q);
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}
	}
	internal class InfTrees
	{
		internal enum Constant
		{
			MANY = 1440,
			FIXEDH = 544,
			BMAX = 15
		}

		internal class inflate_huft
		{
			internal byte exop;

			internal byte bits;

			internal uint _base;

			internal inflate_huft()
			{
				exop = 0;
				bits = 0;
				_base = 0u;
			}

			internal inflate_huft(inflate_huft source)
			{
				exop = source.exop;
				bits = source.bits;
				_base = source._base;
			}
		}

		private static inflate_huft[] fixed_mem = null;

		private static uint[] cplens = new uint[31]
		{
			3u, 4u, 5u, 6u, 7u, 8u, 9u, 10u, 11u, 13u,
			15u, 17u, 19u, 23u, 27u, 31u, 35u, 43u, 51u, 59u,
			67u, 83u, 99u, 115u, 131u, 163u, 195u, 227u, 258u, 0u,
			0u
		};

		private static uint[] cplext = new uint[31]
		{
			0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 1u, 1u,
			1u, 1u, 2u, 2u, 2u, 2u, 3u, 3u, 3u, 3u,
			4u, 4u, 4u, 4u, 5u, 5u, 5u, 5u, 0u, 128u,
			128u
		};

		private static uint[] cplens64 = new uint[31]
		{
			3u, 4u, 5u, 6u, 7u, 8u, 9u, 10u, 11u, 13u,
			15u, 17u, 19u, 23u, 27u, 31u, 35u, 43u, 51u, 59u,
			67u, 83u, 99u, 115u, 131u, 163u, 195u, 227u, 3u, 0u,
			0u
		};

		private static uint[] cplext64 = new uint[31]
		{
			0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 1u, 1u,
			1u, 1u, 2u, 2u, 2u, 2u, 3u, 3u, 3u, 3u,
			4u, 4u, 4u, 4u, 5u, 5u, 5u, 5u, 16u, 128u,
			128u
		};

		private static uint[] cpdist = new uint[32]
		{
			1u, 2u, 3u, 4u, 5u, 7u, 9u, 13u, 17u, 25u,
			33u, 49u, 65u, 97u, 129u, 193u, 257u, 385u, 513u, 769u,
			1025u, 1537u, 2049u, 3073u, 4097u, 6145u, 8193u, 12289u, 16385u, 24577u,
			32769u, 49153u
		};

		private static uint[] cpdext = new uint[32]
		{
			0u, 0u, 0u, 0u, 1u, 1u, 2u, 2u, 3u, 3u,
			4u, 4u, 5u, 5u, 6u, 6u, 7u, 7u, 8u, 8u,
			9u, 9u, 10u, 10u, 11u, 11u, 12u, 12u, 13u, 13u,
			14u, 14u
		};

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_trees_fixed(int method, ref uint bl, ref uint bd, ref inflate_huft[] tl, ref inflate_huft[] td, inflate_huft[] hp)
		{
			int num = 0;
			uint num2 = 30u;
			uint hn = 0u;
			uint[] array = null;
			uint[] array2 = null;
			array = new uint[288];
			array2 = new uint[288];
			fixed_mem = new inflate_huft[544];
			for (num = 0; num < 144; num++)
			{
				array[num] = 8u;
			}
			for (; num < 256; num++)
			{
				array[num] = 9u;
			}
			for (; num < 280; num++)
			{
				array[num] = 7u;
			}
			for (; num < 288; num++)
			{
				array[num] = 8u;
			}
			bl = 9u;
			huft_build(array, 0u, 288u, 257u, (method == 8) ? cplens : cplens64, (method == 8) ? cplext : cplext64, ref tl, ref bl, hp, ref hn, array2);
			if (method == 9)
			{
				num2 = 32u;
			}
			for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
			{
				array[num] = 5u;
			}
			bd = 5u;
			huft_build(array, 0u, num2, 0u, cpdist, cpdext, ref td, ref bd, hp, ref hn, array2);
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_trees_bits(uint[] c, ref uint bb, ref inflate_huft[] tb, inflate_huft[] hp, z_stream_s z)
		{
			uint hn = 0u;
			uint[] array = null;
			array = new uint[19];
			ReturnCode returnCode = huft_build(c, 0u, 19u, 19u, null, null, ref tb, ref bb, hp, ref hn, array);
			if (returnCode == ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR)
			{
				z.msg = "oversubscribed dynamic bit lengths tree";
			}
			else if (returnCode == ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR || bb == 0)
			{
				z.msg = "incomplete dynamic bit lengths tree";
				returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
			}
			return returnCode;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate_trees_dynamic(int method, uint nl, uint nd, ref uint[] c, ref uint bl, ref uint bd, ref inflate_huft[] tl, ref inflate_huft[] td, ref inflate_huft[] hp, z_stream_s z)
		{
			uint hn = 0u;
			uint[] array = null;
			array = new uint[288];
			ReturnCode returnCode = huft_build(c, 0u, nl, 257u, (method == 8) ? cplens : cplens64, (method == 8) ? cplext : cplext64, ref tl, ref bl, hp, ref hn, array);
			if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_OK || bl == 0)
			{
				switch (returnCode)
				{
				case ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR:
					z.msg = "oversubscribed literal/length tree";
					break;
				default:
					z.msg = "incomplete literal/length tree";
					returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					break;
				case ReturnCode.Z_MEM_ERROR:
					break;
				}
				return returnCode;
			}
			returnCode = huft_build(c, nl, nd, 0u, cpdist, cpdext, ref td, ref bd, hp, ref hn, array);
			if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_OK || (bd == 0 && nl > 257))
			{
				switch (returnCode)
				{
				case ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR:
					z.msg = "oversubscribed distance tree";
					break;
				case ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR:
					z.msg = "incomplete distance tree";
					returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					break;
				default:
					z.msg = "empty distance tree with lengths";
					returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
					break;
				case ReturnCode.Z_MEM_ERROR:
					break;
				}
				return returnCode;
			}
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		private static ReturnCode huft_build(uint[] b, uint startindex, uint n, uint s, uint[] d, uint[] e, ref inflate_huft[] t, ref uint m, inflate_huft[] hp, ref uint hn, uint[] v)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			uint[] array = new uint[16];
			uint num2 = 0u;
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			uint num5 = 0u;
			uint num6 = 0u;
			int num7 = 0;
			uint num8 = 0u;
			uint num9 = 0u;
			uint num10 = 0u;
			inflate_huft inflate_huft = new inflate_huft();
			uint[] array2 = new uint[15];
			int num11 = 0;
			uint[] array3 = new uint[16];
			uint num12 = 0u;
			int num13 = 0;
			uint num14 = 0u;
			uint num15 = 0u;
			for (uint num16 = 0u; num16 < 16; num16++)
			{
				array[num16] = 0u;
			}
			num9 = 0u;
			num5 = n;
			do
			{
				array[b[startindex + num9]]++;
				num9++;
			}
			while (--num5 != 0);
			if (array[0] == n)
			{
				t = null;
				m = 0u;
				return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			}
			int num17 = (int)m;
			for (num6 = 1u; num6 <= 15 && array[num6] == 0; num6++)
			{
			}
			num7 = (int)num6;
			if ((uint)num17 < num6)
			{
				num17 = (int)num6;
			}
			num5 = 15u;
			while (num5 != 0 && array[num5] == 0)
			{
				num5--;
			}
			num3 = (int)num5;
			if ((uint)num17 > num5)
			{
				num17 = (int)num5;
			}
			m = (uint)num17;
			num13 = 1 << (int)num6;
			while (num6 < num5)
			{
				if ((num13 -= (int)array[num6]) < 0)
				{
					return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
				}
				num6++;
				num13 <<= 1;
			}
			if ((num13 -= (int)array[num5]) < 0)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
			}
			array[num5] += (uint)num13;
			num6 = (array3[1] = 0u);
			num9 = 1u;
			num12 = 2u;
			while (--num5 != 0)
			{
				num6 = (array3[num12++] = num6 + array[num9++]);
			}
			num9 = 0u;
			num5 = 0u;
			do
			{
				if ((num6 = b[startindex + num9]) != 0)
				{
					v[array3[num6]++] = num5;
				}
				num9++;
			}
			while (++num5 < n);
			n = array3[num3];
			num5 = (array3[0] = 0u);
			num9 = 0u;
			num4 = -1;
			num11 = -num17;
			array2[0] = 0u;
			num10 = 0u;
			num14 = 0u;
			for (; num7 <= num3; num7++)
			{
				num = array[num7];
				while (num != 0)
				{
					num--;
					while (num7 > num11 + num17)
					{
						num4++;
						num11 += num17;
						num14 = (uint)(num3 - num11);
						num14 = ((num14 > num17) ? ((uint)num17) : num14);
						if ((num2 = (uint)(1 << (int)(num6 = (uint)(num7 - num11)))) > num + 1)
						{
							num2 -= num + 1;
							num12 = (uint)num7;
							if (num6 < num14)
							{
								while (++num6 < num14 && (num2 <<= 1) > array[++num12])
								{
									num2 -= array[num12];
								}
							}
						}
						num14 = (uint)(1 << (int)num6);
						if (hn + num14 > 1440)
						{
							return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
						}
						num10 = (array2[num4] = hn);
						hn += num14;
						if (num4 != 0)
						{
							array3[num4] = num5;
							inflate_huft.bits = (byte)num17;
							inflate_huft.exop = (byte)num6;
							num6 = num5 >> num11 - num17;
							inflate_huft._base = num10 - array2[num4 - 1] - num6;
							hp[array2[num4 - 1] + num6] = new inflate_huft(inflate_huft);
						}
						else
						{
							num15 = num10;
						}
					}
					inflate_huft.bits = (byte)(num7 - num11);
					if (num9 >= n)
					{
						inflate_huft.exop = 128;
					}
					else if (v[num9] < s)
					{
						inflate_huft.exop = (byte)((v[num9] >= 256) ? 64u : 0u);
						inflate_huft._base = v[num9++];
					}
					else
					{
						inflate_huft.exop = (byte)(e[v[num9] - s] + 32);
						inflate_huft._base = d[v[num9++] - s];
					}
					num2 = (uint)(1 << num7 - num11);
					for (num6 = num5 >> num11; num6 < num14; num6 += num2)
					{
						hp[num10 + num6] = new inflate_huft(inflate_huft);
					}
					num6 = (uint)(1 << num7 - 1);
					while ((num5 & num6) != 0)
					{
						num5 ^= num6;
						num6 >>= 1;
					}
					num5 ^= num6;
					num8 = (uint)((1 << num11) - 1);
					while ((num5 & num8) != array3[num4])
					{
						num4--;
						num11 -= num17;
						num8 = (uint)((1 << num11) - 1);
					}
				}
			}
			t = new inflate_huft[hp.Length - num15];
			Array.Copy(hp, (int)num15, t, 0, (int)(hp.Length - num15));
			if (num13 == 0 || num3 == 1)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			}
			return ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR;
		}
	}
