		private static static_tree_desc static_l_desc = new static_tree_desc(static_ltree, extra_lbits, 257, 286, 15);

		private static static_tree_desc static_l_desc64 = new static_tree_desc(static_ltree, extra_lbits64, 257, 286, 15);

		private static static_tree_desc static_d_desc = new static_tree_desc(static_dtree, extra_dbits, 0, 32, 15);

		private static static_tree_desc static_bl_desc = new static_tree_desc(null, extra_blbits, 0, 19, 7);

		internal Tree()
		{
		}

		internal static void _tr_init(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			s.l_desc.dyn_tree = s.dyn_ltree;
			s.l_desc.stat_desc = static_l_desc;
			if (s.method == 9)
			{
				s.l_desc.stat_desc = static_l_desc64;
			}
			s.d_desc.dyn_tree = s.dyn_dtree;
			s.d_desc.stat_desc = static_d_desc;
			s.bl_desc.dyn_tree = s.bl_tree;
			s.bl_desc.stat_desc = static_bl_desc;
			s.bi_buf = 0;
			s.bi_valid = 0;
			s.last_eob_len = 8;
			init_block(s);
		}

		internal static void _tr_align(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			send_bits(s, 2, 3);
			send_code(s, 256u, static_ltree);
			bi_flush(s);
			if (1 + s.last_eob_len + 10 - s.bi_valid < 9)
			{
				send_bits(s, 2, 3);
				send_code(s, 256u, static_ltree);
				bi_flush(s);
			}
			s.last_eob_len = 7;
		}

		internal static void put_byte(XDeflate.internal_state s, byte c)
		{
			s.pending_buf[s.pending++] = c;
		}

		internal static byte d_code(uint dist)
		{
			if (dist >= 256)
			{
				return _dist_code[256 + (dist >> 7)];
			}
			return _dist_code[dist];
		}

		internal static void send_bits(XDeflate.internal_state s, int _value, int length)
		{
			if (s.bi_valid > 16 - length)
			{
				s.bi_buf |= (ushort)(_value << s.bi_valid);
				put_short(s, s.bi_buf);
				s.bi_buf = (ushort)(_value >> 16 - s.bi_valid);
				s.bi_valid += length - 16;
			}
			else
			{
				s.bi_buf |= (ushort)(_value << s.bi_valid);
				s.bi_valid += length;
			}
		}

		internal static void copy_block(XDeflate.internal_state s, byte[] buf, int startindex, uint len, bool header)
		{
			bi_windup(s);
			s.last_eob_len = 8;
			if (header)
			{
				put_short(s, (ushort)len);
				put_short(s, (ushort)(~len));
			}
			int num = 0;
			while (len != 0)
			{
				put_byte(s, buf[startindex + num++]);
				len--;
			}
		}

		internal static void set_data_type(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			int num = 0;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			uint num3 = 0u;
			while (num < 7)
			{
				num3 += s.dyn_ltree[num++].freq_code;
			}
			while (num < 128)
			{
				num2 += s.dyn_ltree[num++].freq_code;
			}
			while (num < 256)
			{
				num3 += s.dyn_ltree[num++].freq_code;
			}
			s.data_type = ((num3 <= num2 >> 2) ? DataTypeValue.Z_ASCII : DataTypeValue.Z_BINARY);
		}

		internal static void _tr_flush_block(XDeflate.internal_state s, byte[] buf, int startindex, uint stored_len, bool eof)
		{
			int num = 0;
			uint num2;
			uint num3;
			if (s.level > 0)
			{
				if (s.data_type == DataTypeValue.Z_UNKNOWN)
				{
					set_data_type(s);
				}
				build_tree(s, s.l_desc);
				build_tree(s, s.d_desc);
				num = build_bl_tree(s);
				num2 = s.opt_len + 3 + 7 >> 3;
				num3 = s.static_len + 3 + 7 >> 3;
				if (num3 <= num2)
				{
					num2 = num3;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = (num3 = stored_len + 5);
			}
			if (stored_len + 4 <= num2 && buf != null)
			{
				_tr_stored_block(s, buf, startindex, stored_len, eof);
			}
			else if (num3 == num2)
			{
				send_bits(s, 2 + (eof ? 1 : 0), 3);
				compress_block(s, static_ltree, static_dtree);
			}
			else
			{
				send_bits(s, 4 + (eof ? 1 : 0), 3);
				send_all_trees(s, s.l_desc.max_code + 1, s.d_desc.max_code + 1, num + 1);
				compress_block(s, s.dyn_ltree, s.dyn_dtree);
			}
			init_block(s);
			if (eof)
			{
				bi_windup(s);
			}
		}

		internal static void _tr_stored_block(XDeflate.internal_state s, byte[] buf, int startindex, uint stored_len, bool eof)
		{
			send_bits(s, eof ? 1 : 0, 3);
			copy_block(s, buf, startindex, stored_len, header: true);
		}

		private static void init_block(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 286; i++)
			{
				s.dyn_ltree[i].freq_code = 0;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++)
			{
				s.dyn_dtree[i].freq_code = 0;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 19; i++)
			{
				s.bl_tree[i].freq_code = 0;
			}
			s.dyn_ltree[256].freq_code = 1;
			s.opt_len = (s.static_len = 0u);
			s.last_lit = (s.matches = 0u);
		}

		private static void send_code(XDeflate.internal_state s, uint c, ct_data[] tree)
		{
			send_bits(s, tree[c].freq_code, tree[c].dad_len);
		}

		private static void bi_flush(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			if (s.bi_valid == 16)
			{
				put_short(s, s.bi_buf);
				s.bi_buf = 0;
				s.bi_valid = 0;
			}
			else if (s.bi_valid >= 8)
			{
				put_byte(s, (byte)s.bi_buf);
				s.bi_buf >>= 8;
				s.bi_valid -= 8;
			}
		}

		private static void bi_windup(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			if (s.bi_valid > 8)
			{
				put_short(s, s.bi_buf);
			}
			else if (s.bi_valid > 0)
			{
				put_byte(s, (byte)s.bi_buf);
			}
			s.bi_buf = 0;
			s.bi_valid = 0;
		}

		private static void put_short(XDeflate.internal_state s, ushort w)
		{
			put_byte(s, (byte)(w & 0xFF));
			put_byte(s, (byte)(w >> 8));
		}

		private static void build_tree(XDeflate.internal_state s, tree_desc desc)
		{
			ct_data[] dyn_tree = desc.dyn_tree;
			ct_data[] static_tree = desc.stat_desc.static_tree;
			int elems = desc.stat_desc.elems;
			int num = -1;
			s.heap_len = 0;
			s.heap_max = 573;
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < elems; i++)
			{
				if (dyn_tree[i].freq_code != 0)
				{
					num = (s.heap[++s.heap_len] = i);
					s.depth[i] = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					dyn_tree[i].dad_len = 0;
				}
			}
			int num2;
			while (s.heap_len < 2)
			{
				num2 = (s.heap[++s.heap_len] = ((num < 2) ? (++num) : 0));
				dyn_tree[num2].freq_code = 1;
				s.depth[num2] = 0;
				s.opt_len--;
				if (static_tree != null)
				{
					s.static_len -= static_tree[num2].dad_len;
				}
			}
			desc.max_code = num;
			for (i = s.heap_len / 2; i >= 1; i--)
			{
				pqdownheap(s, dyn_tree, i);
			}
			num2 = elems;
			do
			{
				pqremove(s, dyn_tree, ref i);
				int num3 = s.heap[1];
				s.heap[--s.heap_max] = i;
				s.heap[--s.heap_max] = num3;
				dyn_tree[num2].freq_code = (ushort)(dyn_tree[i].freq_code + dyn_tree[num3].freq_code);
				s.depth[num2] = (byte)(Math.Max(s.depth[i], s.depth[num3]) + 1);
				dyn_tree[i].dad_len = (dyn_tree[num3].dad_len = (ushort)num2);
				s.heap[1] = num2++;
				pqdownheap(s, dyn_tree, 1);
			}
			while (s.heap_len >= 2);
			s.heap[--s.heap_max] = s.heap[1];
			gen_bitlen(s, desc);
			gen_codes(dyn_tree, num, s.bl_count);
		}

		private static int build_bl_tree(XDeflate.internal_state s)
		{
			scan_tree(s, s.dyn_ltree, s.l_desc.max_code);
			scan_tree(s, s.dyn_dtree, s.d_desc.max_code);
			build_tree(s, s.bl_desc);
			int num = 18;
			while (num >= 3 && s.bl_tree[bl_order[num]].dad_len == 0)
			{
				num--;
			}
			s.opt_len += (uint)(3 * (num + 1) + 5 + 5 + 4);
			return num;
		}

		private static void compress_block(XDeflate.internal_state s, ct_data[] ltree, ct_data[] dtree)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			if (s.last_lit != 0)
			{
				do
				{
					uint num2 = (uint)(((s.pending_buf[s.d_buf + num * 2 + 1] << 8) & 0xFF00) | (s.pending_buf[s.d_buf + num * 2] & 0xFF));
					int num3 = ((s.pending_buf[s.l_buf + num * 2 + 1] << 8) & 0xFF00) | (s.pending_buf[s.l_buf + num * 2] & 0xFF);
					num++;
					if (num2 == 0)
					{
						send_code(s, (uint)num3, ltree);
						continue;
					}
					int num6;
					uint num4;
					int num5;
					if (s.method == 8)
					{
						num4 = _length_code[num3];
						num5 = extra_lbits[num4];
						num6 = base_length[num4];
					}
					else
					{
						num4 = 28u;
						num5 = 16;
						num6 = 0;
						if (num3 < 256)
						{
							num4 = 27u;
							if (num3 != 255)
							{
								num4 = _length_code[num3];
							}
							num5 = extra_lbits[num4];
							num6 = base_length[num4];
						}
					}
					send_code(s, num4 + 256 + 1, ltree);
					if (num5 != 0)
					{
						num3 -= num6;
						send_bits(s, num3, num5);
					}
					num2--;
					num4 = d_code(num2);
					send_code(s, num4, dtree);
					num5 = extra_dbits[num4];
					if (num5 != 0)
					{
						num2 -= (uint)base_dist[num4];
						send_bits(s, (int)num2, num5);
					}
				}
				while (num < s.last_lit);
			}
			send_code(s, 256u, ltree);
			s.last_eob_len = ltree[256].dad_len;
		}

		private static void send_all_trees(XDeflate.internal_state s, int lcodes, int dcodes, int blcodes)
		{
			send_bits(s, lcodes - 257, 5);
			send_bits(s, dcodes - 1, 5);
			send_bits(s, blcodes - 4, 4);
			for (int i = 0; i < blcodes; i++)
			{
				send_bits(s, s.bl_tree[bl_order[i]].dad_len, 3);
			}
			send_tree(s, s.dyn_ltree, lcodes - 1);
			send_tree(s, s.dyn_dtree, dcodes - 1);
		}

		private static void pqdownheap(XDeflate.internal_state s, ct_data[] tree, int k)
		{
			int num = s.heap[k];
			for (int num2 = k << 1; num2 <= s.heap_len; num2 <<= 1)
			{
				if (num2 < s.heap_len && smaller(tree, s.heap[num2 + 1], s.heap[num2], s.depth))
				{
					num2++;
				}
				if (smaller(tree, num, s.heap[num2], s.depth))
				{
					break;
				}
				s.heap[k] = s.heap[num2];
				k = num2;
			}
			s.heap[k] = num;
		}

		private static void pqremove(XDeflate.internal_state s, ct_data[] tree, ref int top)
		{
			top = s.heap[1];
			s.heap[1] = s.heap[s.heap_len--];
			pqdownheap(s, tree, 1);
		}

		private static void gen_bitlen(XDeflate.internal_state s, tree_desc desc)
		{
			ct_data[] dyn_tree = desc.dyn_tree;
			int max_code = desc.max_code;
			ct_data[] static_tree = desc.stat_desc.static_tree;
			int[] extra_bits = desc.stat_desc.extra_bits;
			int extra_base = desc.stat_desc.extra_base;
			int max_length = desc.stat_desc.max_length;
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 15; i++)
			{
				s.bl_count[i] = 0;
			}
			dyn_tree[s.heap[s.heap_max]].dad_len = 0;
			int j;
			for (j = s.heap_max + 1; j < 573; j++)
			{
				int num2 = s.heap[j];
				int i = dyn_tree[dyn_tree[num2].dad_len].dad_len + 1;
				if (i > max_length)
				{
					i = max_length;
					num++;
				}
				dyn_tree[num2].dad_len = (ushort)i;
				if (num2 <= max_code)
				{
					s.bl_count[i]++;
					int num3 = 0;
					if (num2 >= extra_base)
					{
						num3 = extra_bits[num2 - extra_base];
					}
					ushort freq_code = dyn_tree[num2].freq_code;
					s.opt_len += (uint)(freq_code * (i + num3));
					if (static_tree != null)
					{
						s.static_len += (uint)(freq_code * (static_tree[num2].dad_len + num3));
					}
				}
			}
			if (num == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			do
			{
				int i = max_length - 1;
				while (s.bl_count[i] == 0)
				{
					i--;
				}
				s.bl_count[i]--;
				s.bl_count[i + 1] += 2;
				s.bl_count[max_length]--;
				num -= 2;
			}
			while (num > 0);
			for (int i = max_length; i != 0; i--)
			{
				int num2 = s.bl_count[i];
				while (num2 != 0)
				{
					int num4 = s.heap[--j];
					if (num4 <= max_code)
					{
						if (dyn_tree[num4].dad_len != i)
						{
							s.opt_len += (uint)((i - dyn_tree[num4].dad_len) * dyn_tree[num4].freq_code);
							dyn_tree[num4].dad_len = (ushort)i;
						}
						num2--;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static void gen_codes(ct_data[] tree, int max_code, ushort[] bl_count)
		{
			ushort[] array = null;
			ushort num = 0;
			array = new ushort[16];
			for (int i = 1; i <= 15; i++)
			{
				num = (array[i] = (ushort)(num + bl_count[i - 1] << 1));
			}
			for (int j = 0; j <= max_code; j++)
			{
				int dad_len = tree[j].dad_len;
				if (dad_len != 0)
				{
					tree[j].freq_code = (ushort)bi_reverse(array[dad_len]++, dad_len);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void scan_tree(XDeflate.internal_state s, ct_data[] tree, int max_code)
		{
			int num = -1;
			int dad_len = tree[0].dad_len;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 7;
			int num4 = 4;
			if (dad_len == 0)
			{
				num3 = 138;
				num4 = 3;
			}
			tree[max_code + 1].dad_len = ushort.MaxValue;
			for (int i = 0; i <= max_code; i++)
			{
				int num5 = dad_len;
				dad_len = tree[i + 1].dad_len;
				if (++num2 < num3 && num5 == dad_len)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num2 < num4)
				{
					s.bl_tree[num5].freq_code += (ushort)num2;
				}
				else if (num5 != 0)
				{
					if (num5 != num)
					{
						s.bl_tree[num5].freq_code++;
					}
					s.bl_tree[16].freq_code++;
				}
				else if (num2 <= 10)
				{
					s.bl_tree[17].freq_code++;
				}
				else
				{
					s.bl_tree[18].freq_code++;
				}
				num2 = 0;
				num = num5;
				if (dad_len == 0)
				{
					num3 = 138;
					num4 = 3;
				}
				else if (num5 == dad_len)
				{
					num3 = 6;
					num4 = 3;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = 7;
					num4 = 4;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void send_tree(XDeflate.internal_state s, ct_data[] tree, int max_code)
		{
			int num = -1;
			int dad_len = tree[0].dad_len;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 7;
			int num4 = 4;
			if (dad_len == 0)
			{
				num3 = 138;
				num4 = 3;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i <= max_code; i++)
			{
				int num5 = dad_len;
				dad_len = tree[i + 1].dad_len;
				if (++num2 < num3 && num5 == dad_len)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num2 < num4)
				{
					do
					{
						send_code(s, (uint)num5, s.bl_tree);
					}
					while (--num2 != 0);
				}
				else if (num5 != 0)
				{
					if (num5 != num)
					{
						send_code(s, (uint)num5, s.bl_tree);
						num2--;
					}
					send_code(s, 16u, s.bl_tree);
					send_bits(s, num2 - 3, 2);
				}
				else if (num2 <= 10)
				{
					send_code(s, 17u, s.bl_tree);
					send_bits(s, num2 - 3, 3);
				}
				else
				{
					send_code(s, 18u, s.bl_tree);
					send_bits(s, num2 - 11, 7);
				}
				num2 = 0;
				num = num5;
				if (dad_len == 0)
				{
					num3 = 138;
					num4 = 3;
				}
				else if (num5 == dad_len)
				{
					num3 = 6;
					num4 = 3;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = 7;
					num4 = 4;
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool smaller(ct_data[] tree, int n, int m, byte[] depth)
		{
			if (tree[n].freq_code >= tree[m].freq_code)
			{
				if (tree[n].freq_code == tree[m].freq_code)
				{
					return depth[n] <= depth[m];
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static uint bi_reverse(uint code, int len)
		{
			uint num = 0u;
			do
			{
				num |= code & 1;
				code >>= 1;
				num <<= 1;
			}
			while (--len > 0);
			return num >> 1;
		}
	}
	internal class XDeflate
	{
		private enum Compress_func
		{
			deflate_stored,
			deflate_fast,
			deflate_slow
		}

		private enum block_state
		{
			need_more,
			block_done,
			finish_started,
			finish_done
		}

		internal enum Constant
		{
			LENGTH_CODES = 29,
			LITERALS = 256,
			L_CODES = 286,
			D_CODES = 32,
			BL_CODES = 19,
			HEAP_SIZE = 573,
			MAX_BITS = 15,
			MIN_LOOKAHEAD = 262,
			MIN_MATCH = 3,
			MAX_MATCH = 258,
			TOO_FAR = 4096
		}

		internal enum StreamStatus
		{
			INIT_STATE = 42,
			BUSY_STATE = 113,
			FINISH_STATE = 666
		}

		internal class internal_state : Xceed.Compression.CompressionEngine.Managed.internal_state
		{
			internal z_stream_s strm;

			internal StreamStatus status;

			internal byte[] pending_buf;

			internal uint pending_buf_size;

			internal uint pending_out;

			internal int pending;

			internal int noheader;

			internal DataTypeValue data_type;

			internal byte method;

			internal FlushValue last_flush;

			internal uint w_size;

			internal uint w_bits;

			internal uint w_mask;

			internal byte[] window;

			internal uint window_size;

			internal uint max_dist;

			internal uint max_match;

			internal uint min_lookahead;

			internal ushort[] prev;

			internal ushort[] head;

			internal uint[] prev64;

			internal uint[] head64;

			internal uint ins_h;

			internal uint hash_size;

			internal uint hash_bits;

			internal uint hash_mask;

			internal uint hash_shift;

			internal int block_start;

			internal uint match_length;

			internal uint prev_match;

			internal int match_available;

			internal uint strstart;

			internal uint match_start;

			internal uint lookahead;

			internal uint prev_length;

			internal uint max_chain_length;

			internal uint max_lazy_match;

			internal int level;

			internal CompressionStrategy strategy;

			internal uint good_match;

			internal int nice_match;

			internal Tree.ct_data[] dyn_ltree;

			internal Tree.ct_data[] dyn_dtree;

			internal Tree.ct_data[] bl_tree;

			internal Tree.tree_desc l_desc = new Tree.tree_desc();

			internal Tree.tree_desc d_desc = new Tree.tree_desc();

			internal Tree.tree_desc bl_desc = new Tree.tree_desc();

			internal ushort[] bl_count = new ushort[16];

			internal int[] heap = new int[573];

			internal int heap_len;

			internal int heap_max;

			internal byte[] depth = new byte[573];

			internal int l_buf;

			internal uint lit_bufsize;

			internal uint last_lit;

			internal int d_buf;

			internal uint opt_len;

			internal uint static_len;

			internal uint matches;

			internal int last_eob_len;

			internal ushort bi_buf;

			internal int bi_valid;

			internal internal_state()
			{
				dyn_ltree = new Tree.ct_data[573];
				for (int i = 0; i < dyn_ltree.Length; i++)
				{
					dyn_ltree[i] = new Tree.ct_data();
				}
				dyn_dtree = new Tree.ct_data[65];
				for (int j = 0; j < dyn_dtree.Length; j++)
				{
					dyn_dtree[j] = new Tree.ct_data();
				}
				bl_tree = new Tree.ct_data[39];
				for (int k = 0; k < bl_tree.Length; k++)
				{
					bl_tree[k] = new Tree.ct_data();
				}
			}
		}

		private class config
		{
			internal ushort good_length;

			internal ushort max_lazy;

			internal ushort nice_length;

			internal ushort max_chain;

			internal Compress_func func;

			internal config(ushort good_length, ushort max_lazy, ushort nice_length, ushort max_chain, Compress_func func)
			{
				this.good_length = good_length;
				this.max_lazy = max_lazy;
				this.nice_length = nice_length;
				this.max_chain = max_chain;
				this.func = func;
			}
		}

		internal static string[] z_errmsg = new string[10] { "need dictionary", "stream end", "", "file error", "stream error", "data error", "insufficient memory", "buffer error", "incompatible version", "internal error" };

		private static config[] configuration_table = new config[10]

			new config(32, 128, 258, 1024, Compress_func.deflate_slow),
			new config(32, 258, 258, 4096, Compress_func.deflate_slow)
		};

		private static byte[] m_adler16 = new byte[135]
		{

		internal static ReturnCode DeflateInit(z_stream_s strm, int level, int method, int windowBits, int memLevel, CompressionStrategy strategy)
		{
			int noheader = 0;
			if (strm == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			strm.msg = null;
			if (level == -1)
			{
				level = 6;
			}
			if (windowBits < 0)
			{
				noheader = 1;
				windowBits = -windowBits;
			}
			if (memLevel < 1 || memLevel > 9 || level < 0 || level > 9 || strategy < CompressionStrategy.Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY || strategy > CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			switch (method)
			{
			case 8:
				if (windowBits < 8 || windowBits > 15)
				{
					return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
				}
				break;
			case 9:
				if (windowBits < 8 || windowBits > 16)
				{
					return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
				}
				break;
			default:
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			internal_state internal_state2 = (internal_state)(strm.state = new internal_state());
			internal_state2.strm = strm;
			internal_state2.noheader = noheader;
			internal_state2.w_bits = (uint)windowBits;
			internal_state2.w_size = (uint)(1 << (int)internal_state2.w_bits);
			internal_state2.w_mask = internal_state2.w_size - 1;
			internal_state2.max_dist = MAX_DIST(internal_state2);
			internal_state2.max_match = 258u;
			internal_state2.min_lookahead = 262u;
			if (method == 9)
			{
				internal_state2.max_dist = internal_state2.w_size - 1;
				internal_state2.max_match = internal_state2.w_size - 14;
				internal_state2.min_lookahead = internal_state2.max_match + 3 + 1;
			}
			internal_state2.hash_bits = (uint)(memLevel + 7);
			internal_state2.hash_size = (uint)(1 << (int)internal_state2.hash_bits);
			internal_state2.hash_mask = internal_state2.hash_size - 1;
			internal_state2.hash_shift = (internal_state2.hash_bits + 3 - 1) / 3;
			internal_state2.window = ((method == 8) ? new byte[2 * internal_state2.w_size] : new byte[2 * internal_state2.w_size + internal_state2.min_lookahead]);
			if (method == 9)
			{
				internal_state2.prev64 = new uint[internal_state2.w_size];
				internal_state2.head64 = new uint[internal_state2.hash_size];
			}
			else
			{
				internal_state2.prev = new ushort[internal_state2.w_size];
				internal_state2.head = new ushort[internal_state2.hash_size];
			}
			internal_state2.lit_bufsize = (uint)(1 << memLevel + 6);
			internal_state2.pending_buf = new byte[internal_state2.lit_bufsize * 6];
			internal_state2.pending_buf_size = internal_state2.lit_bufsize * 6;
			internal_state2.d_buf = (int)(2 * internal_state2.lit_bufsize);
			internal_state2.l_buf = (int)(4 * internal_state2.lit_bufsize);
			internal_state2.level = level;
			internal_state2.strategy = strategy;
			internal_state2.method = (byte)method;
			return deflateReset(strm);
		}

		internal static ReturnCode deflateReset(z_stream_s strm)
		{
			if (strm == null || strm.state == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			strm.total_in = (strm.total_out = 0uL);
			strm.msg = null;
			strm.data_type = DataTypeValue.Z_UNKNOWN;
			internal_state internal_state2 = (internal_state)strm.state;
			internal_state2.pending = 0;
			internal_state2.pending_out = 0u;
			if (internal_state2.noheader < 0)
			{
				internal_state2.noheader = 0;
			}
			internal_state2.status = ((internal_state2.noheader != 0) ? StreamStatus.BUSY_STATE : StreamStatus.INIT_STATE);
			strm.adler = 1u;
			internal_state2.last_flush = FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH;
			Tree._tr_init(internal_state2);
			lm_init(internal_state2);
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode deflate(z_stream_s strm, FlushValue flush)
		{
			if (strm == null || strm.state == null || flush > FlushValue.Z_FINISH || flush < FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			internal_state internal_state2 = (internal_state)strm.state;
			if (strm.next_out == null || (strm.next_in == null && strm.avail_in != 0) || (internal_state2.status == StreamStatus.FINISH_STATE && flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH))
			{
				return ERR_RETURN(strm, ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR);
			}
			if (strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				return ERR_RETURN(strm, ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR);
			}
			internal_state2.strm = strm;
			FlushValue last_flush = internal_state2.last_flush;
			internal_state2.last_flush = flush;
			if (internal_state2.status == StreamStatus.INIT_STATE)
			{
				uint num = internal_state2.method + (internal_state2.w_bits - 8 << 4) << 8;
				uint num2 = (uint)(internal_state2.level - 1 >> 1);
				if (num2 > 3)
				{
					num2 = 3u;
				}
				num |= num2 << 6;
				if (internal_state2.strstart != 0)
				{
					num |= 0x20;
				}
				num += 31 - num % 31;
				internal_state2.status = StreamStatus.BUSY_STATE;
				putShortMSB(internal_state2, num);
				if (internal_state2.strstart != 0)
				{
					putShortMSB(internal_state2, strm.adler >> 16);
					putShortMSB(internal_state2, strm.adler & 0xFFFF);
				}
				strm.adler = 1u;
			}
			if (internal_state2.pending != 0)
			{
				flush_pending(strm);
				if (strm.avail_out == 0)
				{
					internal_state2.last_flush = (FlushValue)(-1);
					return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
				}
			}
			else if (strm.avail_in == 0 && flush <= last_flush && flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return ERR_RETURN(strm, ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR);
			}
			if (internal_state2.status == StreamStatus.FINISH_STATE && strm.avail_in != 0)
			{
				return ERR_RETURN(strm, ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR);
			}
			if (strm.avail_in != 0 || internal_state2.lookahead != 0 || (flush != FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH && internal_state2.status != StreamStatus.FINISH_STATE))
			{
				block_state block_state = (block_state)(-1);
				switch (configuration_table[internal_state2.level].func)
				{
				case Compress_func.deflate_stored:
					block_state = deflate_stored(internal_state2, flush);
					break;
				case Compress_func.deflate_fast:
					block_state = ((internal_state2.method != 9) ? deflate_fast(internal_state2, flush) : deflate_fast64(internal_state2, flush));
					break;
				case Compress_func.deflate_slow:
					block_state = ((internal_state2.method != 9) ? deflate_slow(internal_state2, flush) : deflate_slow64(internal_state2, flush));
					break;
				}
				if (block_state == block_state.finish_started || block_state == block_state.finish_done)
				{
					internal_state2.status = StreamStatus.FINISH_STATE;
				}
				switch (block_state)
				{
				case block_state.need_more:
				case block_state.finish_started:
					if (strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						internal_state2.last_flush = (FlushValue)(-1);
					}
					return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
				case block_state.block_done:
					if (flush == FlushValue.Z_PARTIAL_FLUSH)
					{
						Tree._tr_align(internal_state2);
					}
					else
					{
						Tree._tr_stored_block(internal_state2, null, 0, 0u, eof: false);
						if (flush == FlushValue.Z_FULL_FLUSH)
						{
							if (internal_state2.method == 9)
							{
								CLEAR_HASH64(internal_state2);
							}
							else
							{
								CLEAR_HASH(internal_state2);
							}
						}
					}
					flush_pending(strm);
					if (strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						internal_state2.last_flush = (FlushValue)(-1);
						return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			}
			if (internal_state2.noheader != 0)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
			}
			putShortMSB(internal_state2, strm.adler >> 16);
			putShortMSB(internal_state2, strm.adler & 0xFFFF);
			flush_pending(strm);
			internal_state2.noheader = -1;
			if (internal_state2.pending == 0)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
			}
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode deflateEnd(z_stream_s strm)
		{
			if (strm == null || strm.state == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			StreamStatus status = ((internal_state)strm.state).status;
			if (status != StreamStatus.INIT_STATE && status != StreamStatus.BUSY_STATE && status != StreamStatus.FINISH_STATE)
			{
				if (m_adler16[strm.avail_in % 32] == 0)
				{
					return ReturnCode.Z_INTERNAL_ERROR;
				}
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			((internal_state)strm.state).pending_buf = null;
			((internal_state)strm.state).head = null;
			((internal_state)strm.state).prev = null;
			((internal_state)strm.state).window = null;
			((internal_state)strm.state).head64 = null;
			((internal_state)strm.state).prev64 = null;
			strm.state = null;
			if (status != StreamStatus.BUSY_STATE)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			}
			return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode deflateParams(z_stream_s strm, int level, CompressionStrategy strategy)
		{
			ReturnCode result = ReturnCode.Z_OK;
			if (strm == null || strm.state == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			internal_state internal_state2 = (internal_state)strm.state;
			if (level == -1)
			{
				level = 6;
			}
			if (level < 0 || level > 9 || strategy < CompressionStrategy.Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY || strategy > CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			Compress_func func = configuration_table[internal_state2.level].func;
			if (func != configuration_table[level].func && strm.total_in != 0)
			{
				result = deflate(strm, FlushValue.Z_PARTIAL_FLUSH);
			}
			if (internal_state2.level != level)
			{
				internal_state2.level = level;
				internal_state2.max_lazy_match = configuration_table[level].max_lazy;
				internal_state2.good_match = configuration_table[level].good_length;
				internal_state2.nice_match = configuration_table[level].nice_length;
				internal_state2.max_chain_length = configuration_table[level].max_chain;
			}
			internal_state2.strategy = strategy;
			return result;
		}

		private static void lm_init(internal_state s)
		{
			s.window_size = 2 * s.w_size;
			if (s.method == 9)
			{
				s.window_size += s.min_lookahead;
				CLEAR_HASH64(s);
			}
			else
			{
				CLEAR_HASH(s);
			}
			s.max_lazy_match = configuration_table[s.level].max_lazy;
			s.good_match = configuration_table[s.level].good_length;
			s.nice_match = configuration_table[s.level].nice_length;
			s.max_chain_length = configuration_table[s.level].max_chain;
			s.strstart = 0u;
			s.block_start = 0;
			s.lookahead = 0u;
			s.match_length = (s.prev_length = 2u);
			s.match_available = 0;
			s.ins_h = 0u;
		}

		private static ReturnCode ERR_RETURN(z_stream_s strm, ReturnCode err)
		{
			strm.msg = ERR_MSG(err);
			return err;
		}

		private static void putShortMSB(internal_state s, uint b)
		{
			Tree.put_byte(s, (byte)(b >> 8));
			Tree.put_byte(s, (byte)(b & 0xFF));
		}

		private static void flush_pending(z_stream_s strm)
		{
			internal_state internal_state2 = (internal_state)strm.state;
			uint num = (uint)internal_state2.pending;
			if (num > strm.avail_out)
			{
				num = strm.avail_out;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				Array.Copy(internal_state2.pending_buf, (int)internal_state2.pending_out, strm.next_out, (int)strm.next_out_index, (int)num);
				strm.next_out_index += num;
				internal_state2.pending_out += num;
				strm.total_out += num;
				strm.avail_out -= num;
				internal_state2.pending -= (int)num;
				if (internal_state2.pending == 0)
				{
					internal_state2.pending_out = 0u;
				}
			}
		}

		private static block_state deflate_stored(internal_state s, FlushValue flush)
		{
			uint num = 65535u;
			if (num > s.pending_buf_size - 5)
			{
				num = s.pending_buf_size - 5;
			}
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.lookahead <= 1)
				{
					if (s.method == 9)
					{
						fill_window64(s);
					}
					else
					{
						fill_window(s);
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0 && flush == FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				s.strstart += s.lookahead;
				s.lookahead = 0u;
				uint num2 = (uint)s.block_start + num;
				if (s.strstart == 0 || s.strstart >= num2)
				{
					s.lookahead = s.strstart - num2;
					s.strstart = num2;
					FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
				if (s.strstart - s.block_start >= s.max_dist)
				{
					FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
			}
			FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, flush == FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
			if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
				{
					return block_state.need_more;
				}
				return block_state.finish_started;
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return block_state.block_done;
			}
			return block_state.finish_done;
		}

		private static block_state deflate_fast(internal_state s, FlushValue flush)
		{
			uint match_head = 0u;
			bool flush2 = false;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead)
				{
					fill_window(s);
					if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead && flush == FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					INSERT_STRING(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
				}
				if (match_head != 0 && s.strstart - match_head <= s.max_dist && s.strategy != CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
				{
					s.match_length = longest_match(s, match_head);
				}
				if (s.match_length >= 3)
				{
					_tr_tally_dist(s, s.strstart - s.match_start, s.match_length - 3, ref flush2);
					s.lookahead -= s.match_length;
					if (s.match_length <= s.max_lazy_match && s.lookahead >= 3)
					{
						s.match_length--;
						do
						{
							s.strstart++;
							INSERT_STRING(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
						}
						while (--s.match_length != 0);
						s.strstart++;
					}
					else
					{
						s.strstart += s.match_length;
						s.match_length = 0u;
						s.ins_h = s.window[s.strstart];
						UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[s.strstart + 1]);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart], ref flush2);
					s.lookahead--;
					s.strstart++;
				}
				if (flush2)
				{
					FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
			}
			FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, flush == FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
			if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
				{
					return block_state.need_more;
				}
				return block_state.finish_started;
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return block_state.block_done;
			}
			return block_state.finish_done;
		}

		private static block_state deflate_fast64(internal_state s, FlushValue flush)
		{
			uint match_head = 0u;
			bool flush2 = false;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead)
				{
					fill_window64(s);
					if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead && flush == FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					INSERT_STRING64(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
				}
				if (match_head != 0 && s.strstart - match_head <= s.max_dist && s.strategy != CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
				{
					s.match_length = longest_match64(s, match_head);
				}
				if (s.match_length >= 3)
				{
					_tr_tally_dist(s, s.strstart - s.match_start, s.match_length - 3, ref flush2);
					s.lookahead -= s.match_length;
					if (s.match_length <= s.max_lazy_match && s.lookahead >= 3)
					{
						s.match_length--;
						do
						{
							s.strstart++;
							INSERT_STRING64(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
						}
						while (--s.match_length != 0);
						s.strstart++;
					}
					else
					{
						s.strstart += s.match_length;
						s.match_length = 0u;
						s.ins_h = s.window[s.strstart];
						UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[s.strstart + 1]);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart], ref flush2);
					s.lookahead--;
					s.strstart++;
				}
				if (flush2)
				{
					FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
			}
			FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, flush == FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
			if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
				{
					return block_state.need_more;
				}
				return block_state.finish_started;
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return block_state.block_done;
			}
			return block_state.finish_done;
		}

		private static block_state deflate_slow(internal_state s, FlushValue flush)
		{
			uint match_head = 0u;
			bool flush2 = false;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead)
				{
					fill_window(s);
					if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead && flush == FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					INSERT_STRING(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
				}
				s.prev_length = s.match_length;
				s.prev_match = s.match_start;
				s.match_length = 2u;
				if (match_head != 0 && s.prev_length < s.max_lazy_match && s.strstart - match_head <= s.max_dist)
				{
					if (s.strategy != CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
					{
						s.match_length = longest_match(s, match_head);
					}
					if (s.match_length <= 5 && (s.strategy == CompressionStrategy.Z_FILTERED || (s.match_length == 3 && s.strstart - s.match_start > 4096)))
					{
						s.match_length = 2u;
					}
				}
				if (s.prev_length >= 3 && s.match_length <= s.prev_length)
				{
					uint num = s.strstart + s.lookahead - 3;
					_tr_tally_dist(s, s.strstart - 1 - s.prev_match, s.prev_length - 3, ref flush2);
					s.lookahead -= s.prev_length - 1;
					s.prev_length -= 2u;
					do
					{
						if (++s.strstart <= num)
						{
							INSERT_STRING(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
						}
					}
					while (--s.prev_length != 0);
					s.match_available = 0;
					s.match_length = 2u;
					s.strstart++;
					if (flush2)
					{
						FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
						if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
						{
							return block_state.need_more;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (s.match_available != 0)
				{
					_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart - 1], ref flush2);
					if (flush2)
					{
						FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					}
					s.strstart++;
					s.lookahead--;
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					s.match_available = 1;
					s.strstart++;
					s.lookahead--;
				}
			}
			if (s.match_available != 0)
			{
				_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart - 1], ref flush2);
				s.match_available = 0;
			}
			FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, flush == FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
			if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
				{
					return block_state.need_more;
				}
				return block_state.finish_started;
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return block_state.block_done;
			}
			return block_state.finish_done;
		}

		private static block_state deflate_slow64(internal_state s, FlushValue flush)
		{
			uint match_head = 0u;
			bool flush2 = false;
			while (true)
			{
				if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead)
				{
					fill_window64(s);
					if (s.lookahead < s.min_lookahead && flush == FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
					if (s.lookahead == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					INSERT_STRING64(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
				}
				s.prev_length = s.match_length;
				s.prev_match = s.match_start;
				s.match_length = 2u;
				if (match_head != 0 && s.prev_length < s.max_lazy_match && s.strstart - match_head <= s.max_dist)
				{
					if (s.strategy != CompressionStrategy.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY)
					{
						s.match_length = longest_match64(s, match_head);
					}
					if (s.match_length <= 5 && (s.strategy == CompressionStrategy.Z_FILTERED || (s.match_length == 3 && s.strstart - s.match_start > 4096)))
					{
						s.match_length = 2u;
					}
				}
				if (s.prev_length >= 3 && s.match_length <= s.prev_length)
				{
					uint num = s.strstart + s.lookahead - 3;
					_tr_tally_dist(s, s.strstart - 1 - s.prev_match, s.prev_length - 3, ref flush2);
					s.lookahead -= s.prev_length - 1;
					s.prev_length -= 2u;
					do
					{
						if (++s.strstart <= num)
						{
							INSERT_STRING64(s, s.strstart, ref match_head);
						}
					}
					while (--s.prev_length != 0);
					s.match_available = 0;
					s.match_length = 2u;
					s.strstart++;
					if (flush2)
					{
						FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
						if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
						{
							return block_state.need_more;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (s.match_available != 0)
				{
					_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart - 1], ref flush2);
					if (flush2)
					{
						FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, eof: false);
					}
					s.strstart++;
					s.lookahead--;
					if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
					{
						return block_state.need_more;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					s.match_available = 1;
					s.strstart++;
					s.lookahead--;
				}
			}
			if (s.match_available != 0)
			{
				_tr_tally_lit(s, s.window[s.strstart - 1], ref flush2);
				s.match_available = 0;
			}
			FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(s, flush == FlushValue.Z_FINISH);
			if (s.strm.avail_out == 0)
			{
				if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
				{
					return block_state.need_more;
				}
				return block_state.finish_started;
			}
			if (flush != FlushValue.Z_FINISH)
			{
				return block_state.block_done;
			}
			return block_state.finish_done;
		}

		private static void fill_window(internal_state s)
		{
			uint w_size = s.w_size;
			do
			{
				uint num = s.window_size - s.lookahead - s.strstart;
				uint num2;
				if (num == 0 && s.strstart == 0 && s.lookahead == 0)
				{
					num = s.w_size;
				}
				else if (num == uint.MaxValue)
				{
					num--;
				}
				else if (s.strstart >= s.w_size + s.max_dist)
				{
					Array.Copy(s.window, (int)s.w_size, s.window, 0, (int)w_size);
					s.match_start -= s.w_size;
					s.strstart -= s.w_size;
					s.block_start -= (int)s.w_size;
					num2 = s.hash_size;
					uint num3 = num2;
					do
					{
						uint num4 = s.head[--num3];
						s.head[num3] = (ushort)((num4 >= s.w_size) ? (num4 - s.w_size) : 0u);
					}
					while (--num2 != 0);
					num2 = s.w_size;
					num3 = num2;
					do
					{
						uint num4 = s.prev[--num3];
						s.prev[num3] = (ushort)((num4 >= s.w_size) ? (num4 - s.w_size) : 0u);
					}
					while (--num2 != 0);
					num += s.w_size;
				}
				if (s.strm.avail_in == 0)
				{
					break;
				}
				num2 = (uint)read_buf(s.strm, s.window, s.strstart + s.lookahead, num);
				s.lookahead += num2;
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					s.ins_h = s.window[s.strstart];
					UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[s.strstart + 1]);
				}
			}
			while (s.lookahead < (int)s.min_lookahead && s.strm.avail_in != 0);
		}

		private static void fill_window64(internal_state s)
		{
			uint length = s.w_size + s.min_lookahead;
			do
			{
				uint num = s.window_size - s.lookahead - s.strstart;
				uint num2;
				if (num == 0 && s.strstart == 0 && s.lookahead == 0)
				{
					num = s.w_size;
				}
				else if (num == uint.MaxValue)
				{
					num--;
				}
				else if (s.strstart >= s.w_size + s.max_dist)
				{
					Array.Copy(s.window, (int)s.w_size, s.window, 0, (int)length);
					s.match_start -= s.w_size;
					s.strstart -= s.w_size;
					s.block_start -= (int)s.w_size;
					num2 = s.hash_size;
					uint num3 = num2;
					do
					{
						uint num4 = s.head64[--num3];
						s.head64[num3] = ((num4 >= s.w_size) ? (num4 - s.w_size) : 0u);
					}
					while (--num2 != 0);
					num2 = s.w_size;
					num3 = num2;
					do
					{
						uint num4 = s.prev64[--num3];
						s.prev64[num3] = ((num4 >= s.w_size) ? (num4 - s.w_size) : 0u);
					}
					while (--num2 != 0);
					num += s.w_size;
				}
				if (s.strm.avail_in == 0)
				{
					break;
				}
				num2 = (uint)read_buf(s.strm, s.window, s.strstart + s.lookahead, num);
				s.lookahead += num2;
				if (s.lookahead >= 3)
				{
					s.ins_h = s.window[s.strstart];
					UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[s.strstart + 1]);
				}
			}
			while (s.lookahead < (int)s.min_lookahead && s.strm.avail_in != 0);
		}

		private static bool _tr_tally(internal_state s, uint dist, uint lc)
		{
			s.pending_buf[s.d_buf + s.last_lit * 2 + 1] = (byte)(dist >> 8);
			s.pending_buf[s.d_buf + s.last_lit * 2] = (byte)(dist & 0xFF);
			s.pending_buf[s.l_buf + s.last_lit * 2 + 1] = (byte)(lc >> 8);
			s.pending_buf[s.l_buf + s.last_lit * 2] = (byte)(lc & 0xFF);
			s.last_lit++;
			if (dist == 0)
			{
				s.dyn_ltree[lc].freq_code++;
			}
			else
			{
				s.matches++;
				dist--;
				int num;
				if (s.method == 8)
				{
					num = Tree._length_code[lc];
				}
				else
				{
					num = 28;
					if (lc < 256)
					{
						num = 27;
						if (lc != 255)
						{
							num = Tree._length_code[lc];
						}
					}
				}
				s.dyn_ltree[num + 256 + 1].freq_code++;
				s.dyn_dtree[Tree.d_code(dist)].freq_code++;
			}
			return s.last_lit == s.lit_bufsize - 1;
		}

		private static uint longest_match(internal_state s, uint cur_match)
		{
			uint num = s.max_chain_length;
			uint num2 = s.strstart;
			int num3 = (int)s.prev_length;
			int num4 = s.nice_match;
			uint num5 = ((s.strstart > s.max_dist) ? (s.strstart - s.max_dist) : 0u);
			uint w_mask = s.w_mask;
			uint num6 = s.strstart + s.max_match;
			byte b = s.window[num2 + num3 - 1];
			byte b2 = s.window[num2 + num3];
			if (s.prev_length >= s.good_match)
			{
				num >>= 2;
			}
			if (num4 > s.lookahead)
			{
				num4 = (int)s.lookahead;
			}
			do
			{
				uint num7 = cur_match;
				if (s.window[num7 + num3] != b2 || s.window[num7 + num3 - 1] != b || s.window[num7] != s.window[num2] || s.window[++num7] != s.window[num2 + 1])
				{
					continue;
				}
				num2 += 2;
				num7++;
				while (s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && num2 < num6)
				{
				}
				int num8 = (int)(s.max_match - (num6 - num2));
				num2 = num6 - s.max_match;
				if (num8 > num3)
				{
					s.match_start = cur_match;
					num3 = num8;
					if (num8 >= num4)
					{
						break;
					}
					b = s.window[num2 + num3 - 1];
					b2 = s.window[num2 + num3];
				}
			}
			while ((cur_match = s.prev[cur_match & w_mask]) > num5 && --num != 0);
			if (num3 <= s.lookahead)
			{
				return (uint)num3;
			}
			return s.lookahead;
		}

		private static uint longest_match64(internal_state s, uint cur_match)
		{
			uint num = s.max_chain_length;
			uint num2 = s.strstart;
			int num3 = (int)s.prev_length;
			int num4 = s.nice_match;
			uint num5 = ((s.strstart > s.max_dist) ? (s.strstart - s.max_dist) : 0u);
			uint w_mask = s.w_mask;
			uint num6 = s.strstart + s.max_match;
			byte b = s.window[num2 + num3 - 1];
			byte b2 = s.window[num2 + num3];
			if (s.prev_length >= s.good_match)
			{
				num >>= 2;
			}
			if (num4 > s.lookahead)
			{
				num4 = (int)s.lookahead;
			}
			do
			{
				uint num7 = cur_match;
				if (s.window[num7 + num3] != b2 || s.window[num7 + num3 - 1] != b || s.window[num7] != s.window[num2] || s.window[++num7] != s.window[num2 + 1])
				{
					continue;
				}
				num2 += 2;
				num7++;
				while (s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && s.window[++num2] == s.window[++num7] && num2 < num6)
				{
				}
				int num8 = (int)(s.max_match - (num6 - num2));
				num2 = num6 - s.max_match;
				if (num8 > num3)
				{
					s.match_start = cur_match;
					num3 = num8;
					if (num8 >= num4)
					{
						break;
					}
					b = s.window[num2 + num3 - 1];
					b2 = s.window[num2 + num3];
				}
			}
			while ((cur_match = s.prev64[cur_match & w_mask]) > num5 && --num != 0);
			if (num3 <= s.lookahead)
			{
				return (uint)num3;
			}
			return s.lookahead;
		}

		private static int read_buf(z_stream_s strm, byte[] buf, uint startindex, uint size)
		{
			uint num = strm.avail_in;
			if (num > size)
			{
				num = size;
			}
			if (num == 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			strm.avail_in -= num;
			if (((internal_state)strm.state).noheader == 0)
			{
				strm.adler = XZLib.Adler32(strm.adler, strm.next_in, strm.next_in_index, num);
			}
			Array.Copy(strm.next_in, (int)strm.next_in_index, buf, (int)startindex, (int)num);
			strm.next_in_index += num;
			strm.total_in += num;
			return (int)num;
		}

		private static void CLEAR_HASH(internal_state s)
		{
			s.head[s.hash_size - 1] = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < s.head.Length; i++)
			{
				s.head[i] = 0;
			}
		}

		private static void CLEAR_HASH64(internal_state s)
		{
			s.head64[s.hash_size - 1] = 0u;
			for (int i = 0; i < s.head64.Length; i++)
			{
				s.head64[i] = 0u;
			}
		}

		private static void FLUSH_BLOCK_ONLY(internal_state s, bool eof)
		{
			Tree._tr_flush_block(s, (s.block_start >= 0) ? s.window : null, s.block_start, (uint)(s.strstart - s.block_start), eof);
			s.block_start = (int)s.strstart;
			flush_pending(s.strm);
		}

		private static uint MAX_DIST(internal_state s)
		{
			return s.w_size - 262;
		}

		private static void UPDATE_HASH(internal_state s, ref uint h, byte c)
		{
			h = ((h << (int)s.hash_shift) ^ c) & s.hash_mask;
		}

		private static void _tr_tally_lit(internal_state s, byte c, ref bool flush)
		{
			flush = _tr_tally(s, 0u, c);
		}

		private static void _tr_tally_dist(internal_state s, uint distance, uint length, ref bool flush)
		{
			flush = _tr_tally(s, distance, length);
		}

		private static string ERR_MSG(ReturnCode err)
		{
			return z_errmsg[(int)(2 - err)];
		}

		private static uint INSERT_STRING(internal_state s, uint str, ref uint match_head)
		{
			UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[str + 2]);
			match_head = (s.prev[str & s.w_mask] = s.head[s.ins_h]);
			return s.head[s.ins_h] = (ushort)str;
		}

		private static uint INSERT_STRING64(internal_state s, uint str, ref uint match_head)
		{
			UPDATE_HASH(s, ref s.ins_h, s.window[str + 2]);
			match_head = (s.prev64[str & s.w_mask] = s.head64[s.ins_h]);
			return s.head64[s.ins_h] = str;
		}
	}
	internal abstract class internal_state
	{
	}
	internal class XInflate
	{
		internal enum inflate_mode
		{
			METHOD,
			FLAG,
			DICT4,
			DICT3,
			DICT2,
			DICT1,
			DICT0,
			BLOCKS,
			CHECK4,
			CHECK3,
			CHECK2,
			CHECK1,
			DONE,
			BAD
		}

		internal class internal_state : Xceed.Compression.CompressionEngine.Managed.internal_state
		{
			internal inflate_mode mode;

			internal uint method;

			internal uint was;

			internal uint need;

			internal uint marker;

			internal int compMethod;

			internal int nowrap;

			internal uint wbits;

			internal InfBlock.inflate_blocks_state blocks;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode InflateInit(ref z_stream_s z, int method, int w)
		{
			if (method != 8 && method != 9)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			z.msg = null;
			z.state = new internal_state();
			((internal_state)z.state).blocks = null;
			((internal_state)z.state).nowrap = 0;
			if (w < 0)
			{
				w = -w;
				((internal_state)z.state).nowrap = 1;
			}
			if (w < 8 || w > 16)
			{
				InflateEnd(ref z);
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			((internal_state)z.state).compMethod = method;
			((internal_state)z.state).wbits = (uint)w;
			if ((((internal_state)z.state).blocks = InfBlock.inflate_blocks_new(ref z, ((internal_state)z.state).nowrap == 0, (uint)(1 << w))) == null)
			{
				InflateEnd(ref z);
				return ReturnCode.Z_MEM_ERROR;
			}
			InflateReset(ref z);
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode InflateReset(ref z_stream_s z)
		{
			if (z == null || z.state == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			z.total_in = (z.total_out = 0uL);
			z.msg = null;
			((internal_state)z.state).mode = ((((internal_state)z.state).nowrap != 0) ? inflate_mode.BLOCKS : inflate_mode.METHOD);
			uint c = 0u;
			InfBlock.inflate_blocks_reset(((internal_state)z.state).blocks, ref z, ref c);
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode InflateEnd(ref z_stream_s z)
		{
			if (z == null || z.state == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			if (((internal_state)z.state).blocks != null)
			{
				InfBlock.inflate_blocks_free(ref ((internal_state)z.state).blocks, ref z);
			}
			z.state = null;
			return ReturnCode.Z_OK;
		}

		internal static ReturnCode inflate(ref z_stream_s z, FlushValue f)
		{
			internal_state internal_state2 = null;
			if (z == null || z.state == null || z.next_in == null)
			{
				return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
			}
			internal_state2 = (internal_state)z.state;
			f = ((f == FlushValue.Z_FINISH) ? ((FlushValue)(-5)) : FlushValue.Z_NO_FLUSH);
			ReturnCode returnCode = ReturnCode.Z_BUF_ERROR;
			while (true)
			{
				switch (internal_state2.mode)
				{
				case inflate_mode.METHOD:
				{
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.method = NEXTBYTE(z);
					byte b = (byte)(internal_state2.method & 0xF);
					if (b != 8 && b != 9)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "unknown compression method";
						internal_state2.marker = 5u;
						break;
					}
					((internal_state)z.state).compMethod = b;
					if ((internal_state2.method >> 4) + 8 > internal_state2.wbits)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "invalid window size";
						internal_state2.marker = 5u;
					}
					else
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.FLAG;
					}
					break;
				}
				case inflate_mode.FLAG:
				{
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					byte b = NEXTBYTE(z);
					if (((internal_state2.method << 8) + b) % 31 != 0)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "incorrect header check";
						internal_state2.marker = 5u;
					}
					else if ((b & 0x20) == 0)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BLOCKS;
					}
					else
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DICT4;
					}
					break;
				}
				case inflate_mode.DICT4:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need = (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 24);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DICT3;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.DICT3:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 16);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DICT2;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.DICT2:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 8);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DICT1;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.DICT1:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += NEXTBYTE(z);
					z.adler = internal_state2.need;
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DICT0;
					return ReturnCode.Z_NEED_DICT;
				case inflate_mode.DICT0:
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
					z.msg = "need dictionary";
					internal_state2.marker = 0u;
					return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
				case inflate_mode.BLOCKS:
					returnCode = InfBlock.inflate_blocks(ref internal_state2.blocks, ref z, returnCode);
					switch (returnCode)
					{
					case ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR:
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
						internal_state2.marker = 0u;
						goto end_IL_003e;
					case ReturnCode.Z_OK:
						returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
						break;
					}
					if (returnCode != ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					InfBlock.inflate_blocks_reset(internal_state2.blocks, ref z, ref internal_state2.was);
					if (internal_state2.nowrap != 0)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DONE;
					}
					else
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.CHECK4;
					}
					break;
				case inflate_mode.CHECK4:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need = (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 24);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.CHECK3;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.CHECK3:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 16);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.CHECK2;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.CHECK2:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += (uint)(NEXTBYTE(z) << 8);
					internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.CHECK1;
					break;
				case inflate_mode.CHECK1:
					if (z.avail_in == 0)
					{
						return returnCode;
					}
					returnCode = (ReturnCode)f;
					internal_state2.need += NEXTBYTE(z);
					if (internal_state2.was != internal_state2.need)
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.BAD;
						z.msg = "incorrect data check";
						internal_state2.marker = 5u;
					}
					else
					{
						internal_state2.mode = inflate_mode.DONE;
					}
					break;
				case inflate_mode.DONE:
					return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_END;
				case inflate_mode.BAD:
					return ReturnCode.Z_DATA_ERROR;
				default:
					{
						return ReturnCode.Z_STREAM_ERROR;
					}
					end_IL_003e:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private static byte NEXTBYTE(z_stream_s z)
		{
			z.avail_in--;
			z.total_in++;
			return z.next_in[z.next_in_index++];
		}
	}
	internal enum FlushValue
	{
		Z_NO_FLUSH,
		Z_PARTIAL_FLUSH,
		Z_SYNC_FLUSH,
		Z_FULL_FLUSH,
		Z_FINISH
	}
	internal enum ReturnCode
	{
		Z_OK = 0,
		Z_STREAM_END = 1,
		Z_NEED_DICT = 2,
		Z_ERRNO = -1,
		Z_STREAM_ERROR = -2,
		Z_DATA_ERROR = -3,
		Z_MEM_ERROR = -4,
		Z_BUF_ERROR = -5,
		Z_VERSION_ERROR = -6,
		Z_INTERNAL_ERROR = -7
	}
	internal enum CompressionLevel
	{
		Z_NO_COMPRESSION = 0,
		Z_BEST_SPEED = 1,
		Z_BEST_COMPRESSION = 9,
		Z_DEFAULT_COMPRESSION = -1
	}
	internal enum CompressionStrategy
	{
		Z_FILTERED = 1,
		Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY = 2,
		Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY = 0
	}
	internal enum DataTypeValue
	{
		Z_BINARY,
		Z_ASCII,
		Z_UNKNOWN
	}
	internal class z_stream_s
	{

		}

		internal static ReturnCode DeflateInit(z_stream_s strm, int level)
		{
			return DeflateInit2(strm, level, 8, 15, 8, CompressionStrategy.Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY);
		}

		internal static ReturnCode Deflate(z_stream_s strm, FlushValue flush)
		{
			return XDeflate.deflate(strm, flush);
		}

		{
			return XDeflate.deflateEnd(strm);
		}

		{

		}

		}

		{

		}

		}

		}

		{
			return XInflate.InflateInit(ref strm, method, windowBits);
		}

		internal static ReturnCode InflateReset(ref z_stream_s strm)
		{
			return XInflate.InflateReset(ref strm);
		}

		internal static uint Adler32(uint adler, byte[] buf, uint startindex, uint len)
		{
			uint num = adler & 0xFFFF;
			uint num2 = (adler >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
			if (buf == null)
			{
				return 1u;
			}
			while (len != 0)
			{
				uint num3 = ((len < 5552) ? len : 5552u);
				len -= num3;
				while (true)
				{
					switch (num3)
					{
					default:
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num += buf[startindex++];
						num2 += num;
						num3 -= 16;
						continue;
					case 1u:
					case 2u:
					case 3u:
					case 4u:
					case 5u:
					case 6u:
					case 7u:
					case 8u:
					case 9u:
					case 10u:
					case 11u:
					case 12u:
					case 13u:
					case 14u:
					case 15u:
						do
						{
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
						}
						while (--num3 != 0);
						goto case 0u;
					case 0u:
						num %= 65521;
						num2 %= 65521;
						break;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return (num2 << 16) | num;
		}
